
===== FILE backend/tests/test_workflow_snapshot_backfill.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
import os
import subprocess
import sys
from pathlib import Path

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, func, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.audit import canonical_json
from app.database import Base
from app.models import (
    ProductionRun,
    WorkflowDefinition,
    WorkflowSnapshotBlob,
    WorkflowVersion,
)
from app.migrations.postgres_baseline import create_postgres_baseline
from app.migrations.mysql_baseline import create_mysql_baseline
from app.workflow_snapshot_backfill import (
    SnapshotBackfillError,
    backfill_workflow_snapshots,
)
from app.workflow_snapshot_store import load_snapshot_blob


def _workflow_version(db: Session) -> WorkflowVersion:
    definition = WorkflowDefinition(
        workflow_key="snapshot-backfill-test",
        name="Snapshot backfill test",
        description="",
        owner="test",
        allowed_categories_json="[]",
        status="active",
        created_by="test",
    )
    db.add(definition)
    db.flush()
    version = WorkflowVersion(
        workflow_definition_id=definition.id,
        version="1",
        status="active",
        workflow_schema_version="workflow-v1",
        step_manifest_json="[]",
        edge_manifest_json="[]",
        input_schema_json='{"type":"object"}',
        output_schema_json='{"type":"object"}',
        resource_policy_json="{}",
        canonical_hash="a" * 64,
        validation_report_json='{"ok":true,"errors":[]}',
        created_by="test",
    )
    db.add(version)
    db.flush()
    return version


def _run(
    db: Session,
    version: WorkflowVersion,
    *,
    suffix: str,
    snapshot_json: str,
    snapshot_hash: str,
) -> ProductionRun:
    run = ProductionRun(
        run_key=f"backfill-run-{suffix}",
        idempotency_key=f"backfill-idempotency-{suffix}",
        workflow_definition_id=version.workflow_definition_id,
        workflow_version_id=version.id,
        snapshot_json=snapshot_json,
        snapshot_hash=snapshot_hash,
        category_key="knowledge_graph",
        queue_class="validation",
        status="succeeded",
        blockers_json="[]",
        requested_by="test",
        owner="test",
        reason="snapshot backfill test",
        environment="dry_run",
        model_execution_mode="simulated",
        total_steps=0,
        completed_steps=0,
        failed_steps=0,
        attempt_count=0,
        error_code="",
        error_message="",
    )
    db.add(run)
    db.flush()
    return run


def test_backfill_binds_without_changing_legacy_json_and_is_idempotent(
    tmp_path,
) -> None:
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'snapshot-backfill.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        document = {"payload": "x" * 1000, "kind": "test"}
        canonical = canonical_json(document).encode("utf-8")
        snapshot_hash = hashlib.sha256(canonical).hexdigest()
        first_json = json.dumps(document, ensure_ascii=False)
        second_json = json.dumps(
            {"kind": "test", "payload": "x" * 1000},
            ensure_ascii=False,
            indent=2,
        )
        with Session(engine) as db:
            version = _workflow_version(db)
            first_run = _run(
                db,
                version,
                suffix="1",
                snapshot_json=first_json,
                snapshot_hash=snapshot_hash,
            )
            second_run = _run(
                db,
                version,
                suffix="2",
                snapshot_json=second_json,
                snapshot_hash=snapshot_hash,
            )
            before = {
                first_run.id: first_run.snapshot_json.encode("utf-8"),
                second_run.id: second_run.snapshot_json.encode("utf-8"),
            }

            first = backfill_workflow_snapshots(db, batch_size=10)
            db.flush()
            second = backfill_workflow_snapshots(db, batch_size=10)

            assert first.scanned == 2
            assert first.bound == 2
            assert first.reused == 1
            assert first.logical_bytes == len(canonical) * 2
            assert first.physical_bytes > 0
            assert first.avoided_bytes == (
                first.logical_bytes - first.physical_bytes
            )
            assert first.mismatches == ()
            assert first.complete is True
            assert second.scanned == 0
            assert second.bound == 0
            assert second.complete is True
            assert db.scalar(
                select(func.count()).select_from(WorkflowSnapshotBlob)
            ) == 1

            db.expire_all()
            persisted = db.scalars(
                select(ProductionRun).order_by(ProductionRun.id)
            ).all()
            assert all(row.snapshot_blob_id is not None for row in persisted)
            assert {
                row.id: row.snapshot_json.encode("utf-8") for row in persisted
            } == before
            assert load_snapshot_blob(persisted[0].snapshot_blob) == document
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_hash_mismatch_aborts_the_whole_backfill_batch(tmp_path) -> None:
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'snapshot-mismatch.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            version = _workflow_version(db)
            good_json = '{"a":1}'
            good_hash = hashlib.sha256(good_json.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
            good = _run(
                db,
                version,
                suffix="good",
                snapshot_json=good_json,
                snapshot_hash=good_hash,
            )
            bad = _run(
                db,
                version,
                suffix="bad",
                snapshot_json='{"b":2}',
                snapshot_hash="b" * 64,
            )

            with pytest.raises(SnapshotBackfillError) as captured:
                backfill_workflow_snapshots(db, batch_size=10)

            assert captured.value.report.scanned == 2
            assert captured.value.report.bound == 0
            assert captured.value.report.mismatches == (bad.id,)
            assert good.snapshot_blob_id is None
            assert bad.snapshot_blob_id is None
            assert db.scalar(
                select(func.count()).select_from(WorkflowSnapshotBlob)
            ) == 0
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_backfill_cli_reports_the_scanned_mismatch_without_writes(
    tmp_path,
) -> None:
    database_path = tmp_path / "snapshot-cli-mismatch.db"
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{database_path}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            version = _workflow_version(db)
            bad = _run(
                db,
                version,
                suffix="cli-bad",
                snapshot_json='{"b":2}',
                snapshot_hash="b" * 64,
            )
            db.commit()
            bad_id = bad.id
    finally:
        engine.dispose()

    script = (
        Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
        / "scripts"
        / "backfill-workflow-snapshots.py"
    )
    environment = dict(os.environ)
    environment["SNAPSHOT_BACKFILL_TEST_URL"] = f"sqlite:///{database_path}"
    completed = subprocess.run(
        [
            sys.executable,
            str(script),
            "--database-url-env",
            "SNAPSHOT_BACKFILL_TEST_URL",
            "--batch-size",
            "10",
        ],
        cwd=script.parents[1],
        env=environment,
        capture_output=True,
        text=True,
        check=False,
    )

    assert completed.returncode == 1
    report = json.loads(completed.stdout)
    assert report["scanned"] == 1
    assert report["bound"] == 0
    assert report["mismatches"] == [bad_id]
    with Session(create_engine(f"sqlite:///{database_path}")) as db:
        assert db.get(ProductionRun, bad_id).snapshot_blob_id is None
        assert db.scalar(
            select(func.count()).select_from(WorkflowSnapshotBlob)
        ) == 0


def test_postgres_locked_rows_are_not_reported_as_complete(pg_engine) -> None:
    with pg_engine.begin() as connection:
        create_postgres_baseline(connection)
    with Session(pg_engine) as seed_db:
        version = _workflow_version(seed_db)
        snapshot_json = '{"a":1}'
        run = _run(
            seed_db,
            version,
            suffix="postgres-locked",
            snapshot_json=snapshot_json,
            snapshot_hash=hashlib.sha256(snapshot_json.encode()).hexdigest(),
        )
        seed_db.commit()
        run_id = run.id

    locker = Session(pg_engine)
    try:
        locked = locker.scalar(
            select(ProductionRun)
            .where(ProductionRun.id == run_id)
            .with_for_update()
        )
        assert locked is not None

        with Session(pg_engine) as contender:
            report = backfill_workflow_snapshots(
                contender,
                batch_size=10,
            )
            assert report.scanned == 0
            assert report.complete is False
    finally:
        locker.rollback()
        locker.close()


def test_mysql_locked_rows_are_skipped_but_not_reported_as_complete(
    mysql_engine,
) -> None:
    with mysql_engine.begin() as connection:
        create_mysql_baseline(connection)
    with Session(mysql_engine) as seed_db:
        version = _workflow_version(seed_db)
        snapshot_json = '{"a":1}'
        run = _run(
            seed_db,
            version,
            suffix="mysql-locked",
            snapshot_json=snapshot_json,
            snapshot_hash=hashlib.sha256(snapshot_json.encode()).hexdigest(),
        )
        seed_db.commit()
        run_id = run.id

    locker = Session(mysql_engine)
    try:
        locked = locker.scalar(
            select(ProductionRun)
            .where(ProductionRun.id == run_id)
            .with_for_update()
        )
        assert locked is not None

        with Session(mysql_engine) as contender:
            contender.connection().exec_driver_sql(
                "SET SESSION innodb_lock_wait_timeout = 1"
            )
            report = backfill_workflow_snapshots(
                contender,
                batch_size=10,
            )
            assert report.scanned == 0
            assert report.complete is False
    finally:
        locker.rollback()
        locker.close()

===== FILE backend/tests/test_workflow_snapshot_compaction.py =====
import json

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, select, text
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.database import Base
from app.models import ProductionRun, WorkflowSnapshotBlob
from app.workflow_snapshot_store import bind_run_snapshot, read_run_snapshot_text
from app.workflow_snapshot_compaction import (
    SnapshotCompactionError, execute_batch, plan_batch, plan_digest,
)


@pytest.fixture
def db(tmp_path):
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'compaction.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    with Session(engine) as session:
        yield session
    engine.dispose()


def seed(db, number, **overrides):
    run = ProductionRun(run_key=f"run-{number}", idempotency_key=f"item-{number}",
        workflow_definition_id=1, workflow_version_id=1, requested_by="test", owner="test",
        source_type="baseline_regression_item", status="succeeded",
        run_mode="stock_regression", execution_pool="stock")
    bind_run_snapshot(db, run, {"runtime_context": {"run_mode": "stock_regression"},
                                "frozen": number, "说明": "原文"})
    for key, value in overrides.items():
        setattr(run, key, value)
    db.add(run)
    db.commit()
    return run.id


def plan(db):
    value = plan_batch(db, after_id=0, through_id=1000)
    return value, plan_digest(value)


def raw(db):
    return [dict(x) for x in db.execute(select(ProductionRun.__table__)
                                       .order_by(ProductionRun.id)).mappings()]


def test_roundtrip_exact_bytes_all_other_fields_and_blobs_unchanged(db):
    for number in range(3):
        seed(db, number)
    before = raw(db)
    blobs = [dict(x) for x in db.execute(select(WorkflowSnapshotBlob.__table__)).mappings()]
    p, h = plan(db)
    result = execute_batch(db, p, expected_sha256=h)
    db.commit()
    assert result["changed"] == 3 and result["physical_bytes_reclaimed"] is None
    assert result["inline_bytes_changed"] == sum(len(x["snapshot_json"].encode()) for x in before)
    db.expire_all()
    for expected in before:
        run = db.get(ProductionRun, expected["id"])
        assert run.snapshot_json is None
        assert read_run_snapshot_text(run) == expected["snapshot_json"]
    assert execute_batch(db, p, expected_sha256=h)["already_applied"] == 3
    db.commit()
    assert execute_batch(db, p, expected_sha256=h, restore=True)["changed"] == 3
    db.commit()
    assert raw(db) == before
    assert [dict(x) for x in db.execute(select(WorkflowSnapshotBlob.__table__)).mappings()] == blobs
    assert execute_batch(db, p, expected_sha256=h, restore=True)["already_applied"] == 3


def test_scope_excludes_incremental_active_unbound_leased_and_noncanonical(db):
    target = seed(db, 0)
    seed(db, 1, run_mode="incremental", source_type="content_ingress_event", execution_pool="incremental")
    seed(db, 2, status="running")
    seed(db, 3, lease_owner="active-worker")
    seed(db, 4, held_by_release="release-window")
    noncanonical = seed(db, 5)
    unbound = seed(db, 6)
    original = db.scalar(text("SELECT snapshot_json FROM production_runs WHERE id=:id"), {"id": noncanonical})
    db.execute(text("UPDATE production_runs SET snapshot_json=:value WHERE id=:id"),
               {"id": noncanonical, "value": json.dumps(json.loads(original), indent=2)})
    db.execute(text("UPDATE production_runs SET snapshot_blob_id=NULL WHERE id=:id"), {"id": unbound})
    db.commit()
    before = raw(db)
    p, h = plan(db)
    assert [x["id"] for x in p["rows"]] == [target]
    assert p["skipped"] == [{"id": noncanonical, "reason": "source_not_canonical"}]
    execute_batch(db, p, expected_sha256=h)
    db.commit()
    after = raw(db)
    assert after[1:] == before[1:]
    assert after[0]["snapshot_json"] is None


@pytest.mark.parametrize("drift", ["status", "inline", "hash", "blob"])
def test_changed_batch_fails_before_any_clear(db, drift):
    seed(db, 0)
    second = seed(db, 1)
    p, h = plan(db)
    if drift == "status":
        db.execute(text("UPDATE production_runs SET status='running' WHERE id=:id"), {"id": second})
    elif drift == "inline":
        db.execute(text("UPDATE production_runs SET snapshot_json='{}' WHERE id=:id"), {"id": second})
    elif drift == "hash":
        db.execute(text("UPDATE production_runs SET snapshot_hash=:hash WHERE id=:id"), {"id": second, "hash": "e" * 64})
    else:
        db.execute(text("UPDATE workflow_snapshot_blobs SET payload_bytes=:bad WHERE id=(SELECT snapshot_blob_id FROM production_runs WHERE id=:id)"), {"id": second, "bad": b"broken"})
    db.commit()
    before = raw(db)
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError):
        execute_batch(db, p, expected_sha256=h)
    assert raw(db) == before


def test_plan_tamper_and_out_of_scope_plan_rejected(db):
    run_id = seed(db, 0)
    p, h = plan(db)
    p["rows"][0]["utf8_bytes"] += 1
    with pytest.raises(SnapshotCompactionError, match="digest"):
        execute_batch(db, p, expected_sha256=h)
    p, h = plan(db)
    db.execute(text("UPDATE production_runs SET run_mode='incremental' WHERE id=:id"), {"id": run_id})
    db.commit()
    with pytest.raises(SnapshotCompactionError, match="scope"):
        execute_batch(db, p, expected_sha256=h)
    assert raw(db)[0]["snapshot_json"] is not None


def test_bounded_keyset_and_caller_rollback(db):
    for number in range(3):
        seed(db, number)
    before = raw(db)
    p = plan_batch(db, after_id=0, through_id=2, batch_size=1)
    assert p["last_scanned_id"] == 1 and len(p["rows"]) == 1
    db.rollback()
    execute_batch(db, p, expected_sha256=plan_digest(p))
    db.rollback()
    assert raw(db) == before
    with pytest.raises(SnapshotCompactionError, match="bounded"):
        plan_batch(db, after_id=0, through_id=100, batch_size=201)

===== FILE backend/tests/test_workflow_snapshot_store.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import gzip
import hashlib
import json
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor
from threading import Lock
from types import SimpleNamespace

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, event, func, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.database import Base
from app.models import ProductionRun, WorkflowSnapshotBlob
from app.migrations.mysql_baseline import create_mysql_baseline
from app.workflow_snapshot_store import (
    SnapshotStoreError,
    canonical_snapshot_bytes,
    get_or_create_snapshot_blob,
    load_snapshot_blob,
    read_run_snapshot_document,
    read_run_snapshot_text,
    prefetch_run_snapshot_blobs,
)
from app.workflow_runtime import _frozen_runtime_node
from app.workflow_runtime_api import build_workflow_runtime_router
from app.incremental_correction import selectable_levels
from app import incremental_correction


def _run_with_blob(document: dict) -> ProductionRun:
    canonical = canonical_snapshot_bytes(document)
    payload = gzip.compress(canonical, mtime=0)
    digest = hashlib.sha256(canonical).hexdigest()
    blob = WorkflowSnapshotBlob(
        id=17,
        snapshot_hash=digest,
        canonical_size_bytes=len(canonical),
        compressed_size_bytes=len(payload),
        compression="gzip",
        payload_bytes=payload,
    )
    run = ProductionRun(
        snapshot_json=None,
        snapshot_hash=digest,
        snapshot_blob_id=blob.id,
    )
    run.snapshot_blob = blob
    return run


def test_run_snapshot_text_preserves_noncanonical_source() -> None:
    run = ProductionRun(snapshot_json=' { "b": 2, "a": 1 } ', snapshot_hash="x" * 64)
    assert read_run_snapshot_text(run) == ' { "b": 2, "a": 1 } '
    assert read_run_snapshot_document(run) == {"a": 1, "b": 2}


def test_run_snapshot_empty_text_uses_verified_blob() -> None:
    run = _run_with_blob({"runtime_context": {"run_mode": "stock_regression"}})
    run.snapshot_json = ""
    assert read_run_snapshot_document(run)["runtime_context"]["run_mode"] == "stock_regression"


def test_run_snapshot_missing_corrupt_and_mismatched_blob_fail_closed() -> None:
    run = ProductionRun(snapshot_json=None, snapshot_hash="a" * 64)
    with pytest.raises(SnapshotStoreError, match="snapshot_blob_missing"):
        read_run_snapshot_text(run)

    run = _run_with_blob({"a": 1})
    run.snapshot_blob = None
    with pytest.raises(SnapshotStoreError, match="snapshot_blob_missing"):
        read_run_snapshot_text(run)

    run = _run_with_blob({"a": 1})
    run.snapshot_blob.payload_bytes = b"broken"
    with pytest.raises(SnapshotStoreError, match="snapshot_blob_corrupt"):
        read_run_snapshot_text(run)

    run = _run_with_blob({"a": 1})
    run.snapshot_hash = "b" * 64
    with pytest.raises(SnapshotStoreError, match="snapshot_blob_corrupt"):
        read_run_snapshot_text(run)


def test_compact_run_reaches_runtime_snapshot_api_and_correction() -> None:
    run = _run_with_blob({
        "workflow": {"manifest": {
            "nodes": [{"node_instance_id": "frozen-node", "kind": "script"}],
            "resource_policy": {"enabled_levels": ["L1", "L3"]},
        }},
        "runtime_context": {"run_mode": "stock_regression"},
    })
    run.run_key = "compact-run"
    assert _frozen_runtime_node(run, "frozen-node")["kind"] == "script"
    assert selectable_levels(run) == ("L1", "L3")

    class RunLookup:
        def scalar(self, _statement):
            return run

    router = build_workflow_runtime_router(lambda: None)
    endpoint = next(
        route.endpoint for route in router.routes
        if route.path == "/api/runtime/runs/{run_key}/snapshot"
    )
    response = endpoint(run_key="compact-run", _user=object(), db=RunLookup())
    assert response == {
        "run_key": "compact-run",
        "snapshot_hash": run.snapshot_hash,
        "snapshot": json.loads(read_run_snapshot_text(run)),
    }


def test_compact_run_page_prefetches_blobs_in_one_query(tmp_path) -> None:
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'compact-page.db'}")
    column = ProductionRun.__table__.c.snapshot_json
    was_nullable = column.nullable
    try:
        # Stage the future nullable shape only in this disposable SQLite schema.
        column.nullable = True
        Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    finally:
        column.nullable = was_nullable
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            for number in range(12):
                document = {"item": number}
                blob = get_or_create_snapshot_blob(db, document)
                db.add(ProductionRun(
                    run_key=f"compact-{number}",
                    idempotency_key=f"compact-{number}",
                    workflow_definition_id=1,
                    workflow_version_id=1,
                    snapshot_json=None,
                    snapshot_hash=blob.snapshot_hash,
                    snapshot_blob_id=blob.id,
                    requested_by="test",
                    owner="test",
                ))
            db.commit()
            db.expire_all()
            runs = db.scalars(select(ProductionRun).order_by(ProductionRun.id)).all()
            statements: list[str] = []

            def record(_connection, _cursor, statement, _parameters, _context, _many):
                statements.append(statement.lower())

            event.listen(engine, "before_cursor_execute", record)
            try:
                prefetch_run_snapshot_blobs(db, runs)
                assert [read_run_snapshot_document(run)["item"] for run in runs] == list(range(12))
            finally:
                event.remove(engine, "before_cursor_execute", record)
            assert len([sql for sql in statements if "from workflow_snapshot_blobs" in sql]) == 1
            assert len(statements) == 1  # only the missing blob relationships
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_compact_prefetch_preserves_unflushed_run_changes(tmp_path) -> None:
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'prefetch-dirty.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine, autoflush=False) as db:
            blob = get_or_create_snapshot_blob(db, {"frozen": True})
            run = ProductionRun(run_key="compact-dirty", idempotency_key="compact-dirty",
                workflow_definition_id=1, workflow_version_id=1, requested_by="test",
                owner="test", snapshot_json=None, snapshot_hash=blob.snapshot_hash,
                snapshot_blob_id=blob.id, status="succeeded")
            db.add(run); db.commit(); db.expire_all()
            run = db.get(ProductionRun, run.id)
            run.status = "paused"
            run.held_by_release = "reviewed-window"
            prefetch_run_snapshot_blobs(db, [run])
            assert run.status == "paused" and run.held_by_release == "reviewed-window"
            assert read_run_snapshot_document(run) == {"frozen": True}
            db.rollback()
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_correction_stream_closes_before_compact_blob_prefetch(monkeypatch) -> None:
    run = _run_with_blob({"workflow": {"manifest": {
        "resource_policy": {"enabled_levels": ["L1", "L3"]},
    }}})
    run.id = 42
    result = SimpleNamespace(level="L1", review_revision=0, correction_history_json="[]")

    class Stream:
        closed = False

        def __iter__(self):
            yield result, run

        def close(self):
            self.closed = True

    stream = Stream()
    calls: list[list[int]] = []

    class Database:
        def get_bind(self):
            return SimpleNamespace(dialect=SimpleNamespace(name="sqlite"))

        def execute(self, _statement):
            return stream

    def prefetch(_db, runs):
        assert stream.closed
        calls.append([item.id for item in runs])

    monkeypatch.setattr(incremental_correction, "prefetch_run_snapshot_blobs", prefetch)
    payload = incremental_correction.correction_dashboard(Database(), window=1)
    assert calls == [[42]]
    assert payload["levels"] == ["L1", "L3"]


def test_equivalent_key_order_reuses_one_snapshot_blob(tmp_path) -> None:
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'snapshot-store.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            first = get_or_create_snapshot_blob(db, {"b": 2, "a": 1})
            second = get_or_create_snapshot_blob(db, {"a": 1, "b": 2})
            db.commit()

            assert canonical_snapshot_bytes({"b": 2, "a": 1}) == (
                b'{"a":1,"b":2}'
            )
            assert first.id == second.id
            assert db.scalar(
                select(func.count()).select_from(WorkflowSnapshotBlob)
            ) == 1
            assert load_snapshot_blob(first) == {"a": 1, "b": 2}
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_corrupt_compressed_snapshot_fails_closed() -> None:
    blob = WorkflowSnapshotBlob(
        snapshot_hash="a" * 64,
        canonical_size_bytes=2,
        compressed_size_bytes=6,
        compression="gzip",
        payload_bytes=b"broken",
    )

    with pytest.raises(SnapshotStoreError, match="snapshot_blob_corrupt"):
        load_snapshot_blob(blob)


def test_snapshot_hash_mismatch_fails_closed() -> None:
    payload = gzip.compress(b'{"a":1}', mtime=0)
    blob = WorkflowSnapshotBlob(
        snapshot_hash="b" * 64,
        canonical_size_bytes=7,
        compressed_size_bytes=len(payload),
        compression="gzip",
        payload_bytes=payload,
    )

    with pytest.raises(SnapshotStoreError, match="snapshot_blob_corrupt"):
        load_snapshot_blob(blob)


def test_existing_corrupt_blob_is_never_reused(tmp_path) -> None:
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'corrupt-existing.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        canonical = b'{"a":1}'
        with Session(engine) as db:
            db.add(
                WorkflowSnapshotBlob(
                    snapshot_hash=hashlib.sha256(canonical).hexdigest(),
                    canonical_size_bytes=len(canonical),
                    compressed_size_bytes=6,
                    compression="gzip",
                    payload_bytes=b"broken",
                )
            )
            db.commit()

            with pytest.raises(
                SnapshotStoreError,
                match="snapshot_blob_corrupt",
            ):
                get_or_create_snapshot_blob(db, {"a": 1})
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_mysql_concurrent_snapshot_creation_reuses_one_blob(mysql_engine) -> None:
    with mysql_engine.begin() as connection:
        create_mysql_baseline(connection)
    blob_ids: list[int] = []
    blob_lock = Lock()

    def create_once(_worker_number: int) -> None:
        with Session(mysql_engine, expire_on_commit=False) as db:
            blob = get_or_create_snapshot_blob(
                db,
                {"category": "knowledge_graph", "version": 1},
            )
            db.commit()
            blob_id = blob.id
        with blob_lock:
            blob_ids.append(blob_id)

    with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=8) as pool:
        list(pool.map(create_once, range(8)))

    assert len(blob_ids) == 8
    assert len(set(blob_ids)) == 1
    with Session(mysql_engine) as db:
        assert db.scalar(
            select(func.count()).select_from(WorkflowSnapshotBlob)
        ) == 1

from __future__ import annotations

from copy import deepcopy

import pytest


_SCHEMA = {"type": "object", "properties": {"score": {"type": "number"}}}

_FIRST_SEEN_SCHEMA = {
    "type": "object",
    "properties": {
        "status": {
            "type": "string",
            "enum": ["found", "not_found", "unavailable", "failed"],
        },
        "web_first_seen_at": {"type": ["string", "null"]},
        "source_url": {"type": ["string", "null"]},
        "provider": {"type": "string"},
        "match_kind": {
            "type": "string",
            "enum": ["exact", "partial", "similar", "unknown"],
        },
        "confidence": {"type": "number"},
        "checked_at": {"type": "string"},
        "evidence": {
            "type": "object",
            "properties": {
                "provider_result_id": {"type": ["string", "null"]},
                "matched_urls_count": {"type": "integer"},
                "result_hash": {"type": "string"},
            },
            "required": [
                "provider_result_id",
                "matched_urls_count",
                "result_hash",
            ],
            "additionalProperties": False,
        },
    },
    "required": [
        "status",
        "web_first_seen_at",
        "source_url",
        "provider",
        "match_kind",
        "confidence",
        "checked_at",
        "evidence",
    ],
    "additionalProperties": False,
}


def _node(
    node_id: str,
    kind: str,
    config: dict,
    *,
    output_schema: dict | None = None,
) -> dict:
    return {
        "node_instance_id": node_id,
        "kind": kind,
        "display_name": node_id.replace("_", " "),
        "config": config,
        "input_schema": {"type": "object"},
        "output_schema": output_schema or {"type": "object"},
        "error_policy": {"kind": "fail"},
        "retry_policy": {
            "kind": "fixed",
            "max_attempts": 2,
            "delay_seconds": 0,
        },
        "evidence_fields": ["request_id", "trace_id"],
    }


def _rule(node_id: str, version_id: int, *, input_from: str = "prompt_1") -> dict:
    return _node(
        node_id,
        "rule_module",
        {
            "asset_key": "eligibility_redline",
            "module_type_key": "eligibility_redline",
            "rule_module_version_id": version_id,
            "input_bindings": {"model_result": f"{input_from}.result"},
            "scope": "default",
        },
        output_schema=_SCHEMA,
    )


def _valid_manifest() -> dict:
    nodes = [
        _node(
            "upstream",
            "upstream_connector",
            {"connector_key": "logical.space_image.input"},
        ),
        _node(
            "prompt_1",
            "prompt",
            {
                "asset_key": "prompt_c",
                "prompt_version_id": 11,
                "model_registry_id": 3,
                "max_tokens": 4096,
                "thinking_level": "medium",
            },
        ),
        _rule("redline_1", 7),
        _node(
            "downstream",
            "downstream_projection",
            {"connector_key": "logical.space_image.output"},
        ),
    ]
    return {
        "schema_version": "workflow-v3",
        "category_key": "space_image",
        "lane_key": "evaluation",
        "run_modes": ["evaluation", "stock_regression"],
        "nodes": nodes,
        "edges": [
            {"from": "upstream", "to": "prompt_1"},
            {"from": "prompt_1", "to": "redline_1"},
            {"from": "redline_1", "to": "downstream"},
        ],
        "queue_class": "validation",
        "input_schema": {"type": "object"},
        "output_schema": {"type": "object"},
        "resource_policy": {"max_parallel": 1},
    }


def _strict_item_executor() -> dict:
    return {
        "kind": "prompt",
        "asset_key": "prompt_c",
        "input_modality": "image",
        "prompt_version_id": 11,
        "model_registry_id": 3,
        "max_tokens": 4096,
        "thinking_level": "none",
        "thinking_enabled": False,
        "structured_output": True,
        "output_contract": {
            "mode": "json_schema",
            "name": "case_image_result",
            "description": "案例单图结果",
            "strict": True,
            "schema": {
                "type": "object",
                "properties": {"score": {"type": "number"}},
                "required": ["score"],
                "additionalProperties": False,
            },
        },
    }


def _foreach_manifest(**config_overrides: object) -> dict:
    manifest = _valid_manifest()
    node = manifest["nodes"][1]
    node["kind"] = "foreach_image"
    node["display_name"] = "逐图片评测"
    node["config"] = {
        "items_path": "$.case.images",
        "item_identity_path": "$.cloud_id",
        "max_items": 1000,
        "max_parallel_items": 4,
        "failure_policy": "fail_open",
        "item_executor": _strict_item_executor(),
        **config_overrides,
    }
    return manifest

def test_prompt_thinking_switch_is_optional_for_legacy_and_consistent_when_set() -> None:
    from app.workflow_v3 import validate_workflow_v3

    legacy = _valid_manifest()
    assert validate_workflow_v3(legacy) == []

    disabled = deepcopy(legacy)
    disabled_prompt = next(
        node for node in disabled["nodes"] if node["kind"] == "prompt"
    )
    disabled_prompt["config"].update(
        {"thinking_level": "none", "thinking_enabled": False}
    )
    assert validate_workflow_v3(disabled) == []

    enabled = deepcopy(legacy)
    enabled_prompt = next(
        node for node in enabled["nodes"] if node["kind"] == "prompt"
    )
    enabled_prompt["config"].update(
        {"thinking_level": "high", "thinking_enabled": True}
    )
    assert validate_workflow_v3(enabled) == []

    inconsistent = deepcopy(disabled)
    inconsistent_prompt = next(
        node for node in inconsistent["nodes"] if node["kind"] == "prompt"
    )
    inconsistent_prompt["config"]["thinking_level"] = "high"
    codes = {error.code for error in validate_workflow_v3(inconsistent)}
    assert "workflow_model_thinking_configuration_inconsistent" in codes

def test_short_circuit_rule_keys_must_be_unique_across_the_workflow() -> None:
    from app.workflow_v3 import validate_workflow_v3

    manifest = _valid_manifest()
    rule = {
        "rule_version": "short-circuit-rule-v1",
        "rule_key": "shared-direct-level",
        "when": {"path": "output.hit", "op": "eq", "value": True},
        "target_level": "L4",
        "priority": 50,
        "skip_roles": ["grading"],
    }
    prompt = next(node for node in manifest["nodes"] if node["kind"] == "prompt")
    redline = next(
        node for node in manifest["nodes"] if node["kind"] == "rule_module"
    )
    prompt["config"]["short_circuit_rules"] = [deepcopy(rule)]
    redline["config"]["short_circuit_rules"] = [
        {**deepcopy(rule), "target_level": "L5"}
    ]

    errors = validate_workflow_v3(manifest)

    assert any(error.code == "short_circuit_rule_key_duplicate" for error in errors)

    second_rule = _rule("redline_2", 8, input_from="downstream_a")
    second_downstream = _node(
        "downstream_b",
        "downstream_projection",
        {
            "connector_key": "logical.space_image.output",
            "completion_mode": "continue",
        },
    )
    manifest["nodes"].extend([second_rule, second_downstream])

    )

def test_v3_allows_one_waiting_downstream_and_matching_continuation_upstream() -> None:
    from app.workflow_v3 import validate_workflow_v3

    manifest = _valid_manifest()
    waiting = manifest["nodes"][-1]
    waiting["node_instance_id"] = "send_vendor"
    waiting["config"].update(
        {
            "completion_mode": "await_follow_up",
            "continuation_key": "vendor-stage",
            "resume_node_instance_id": "receive_vendor",
            "wait_timeout_seconds": 3600,
            "timeout_policy": "manual_review",
        }
    )
    continuation = _node(
        "receive_vendor",
        "upstream_connector",
        {
            "connector_key": "logical.space_image.vendor_result",
            "input_mode": "continuation_event",
            "continuation_key": "vendor-stage",
            "connector_contract_version_id": 42,
        },
    )
    final_projection = _node(
        "downstream_final",
        "downstream_projection",
        {
            "connector_key": "logical.space_image.output",
            "completion_mode": "continue",
        },
    )
    manifest["nodes"].extend([continuation, final_projection])
    manifest["edges"][-1]["to"] = "send_vendor"
    manifest["edges"].extend(
        [
            {"from": "send_vendor", "to": "receive_vendor"},
            {"from": "receive_vendor", "to": "downstream_final"},
        ]
    )

    assert validate_workflow_v3(manifest) == []

    from app.workflow_v3 import validate_workflow_v3

            ),
            _node(
                "wait_b",
                "downstream_projection",
                {
                    "connector_key": "logical.space_image.output.b",
                    "completion_mode": "await_follow_up",
                    "continuation_key": "shared",
                    "resume_node_instance_id": "resume_a",
                    "wait_timeout_seconds": 3600,
                    "timeout_policy": "fail",
                },
            ),
            _node(
                "resume_a",
                "upstream_connector",
                {
                    "connector_key": "logical.space_image.input.resume",
                    "input_mode": "continuation_event",
                    "continuation_key": "different",
                    "connector_contract_version_id": 42,
                },
            ),
        ]
    )

    codes = {error.code for error in validate_workflow_v3(manifest)}

    assert "workflow_continuation_key_duplicate" in codes
    assert "workflow_continuation_pair_mismatch" in codes

        "first_seen",

            "response_contract_version_id": 22,
            "wait_timeout_seconds": 3600,
            "timeout_policy": "manual_review",

    )

    assert validate_workflow_v3(manifest) == []

    first_seen["config"]["api_key"] = "must-not-persist"
    codes = {error.code for error in validate_workflow_v3(manifest)}
    assert "workflow_node_config_field_unknown" in codes

def test_same_rule_version_with_different_input_is_rejected() -> None:

    manifest = _valid_manifest()
    manifest["nodes"].insert(
        -1,
        _rule("redline_2", 7, input_from="redline_1"),
    )
    manifest["edges"] = [

        {"from": "prompt_1", "to": "redline_1"},
        {"from": "redline_1", "to": "redline_2"},

    ]

def test_shared_parallel_output_requires_registered_merge_policy() -> None:
    from app.workflow_v3 import validate_workflow_v3

    manifest = _valid_manifest()
    manifest["nodes"] = [
        manifest["nodes"][0],
        _node(
            "split",
            "branch",
            {"branch_key": "checks", "branches": ["a", "b"]},
        ),
        _rule("redline_a", 7, input_from="split"),
        _rule("redline_b", 8, input_from="split"),
        _node("join", "join", {"join_for": "checks", "mode": "all"}),
        manifest["nodes"][-1],
    ]
    manifest["edges"] = [
        {"from": "upstream", "to": "split"},
        {"from": "split", "to": "redline_a", "branch": "a"},
        {"from": "split", "to": "redline_b", "branch": "b"},
        {"from": "redline_a", "to": "join"},
        {"from": "redline_b", "to": "join"},
        {"from": "join", "to": "downstream"},
    ]

    errors = validate_workflow_v3(manifest)
    assert any(error.code == "workflow_output_collision" for error in errors)

    join = next(node for node in manifest["nodes"] if node["kind"] == "join")
    join["config"]["merge_policy"] = {"score": "minimum_cap"}
    assert validate_workflow_v3(manifest) == []

    from app.workflow_v3 import validate_workflow_v3

                "thinking_level": "medium",

        ),
    )

    assert "workflow_terminal_not_unique" in codes

    manifest["edges"] = [

        {"from": "prompt_1", "to": "redline_1"},
        {"from": "prompt_1", "to": "redline_2"},
        {"from": "redline_1", "to": "downstream"},

def test_v3_branch_edges_and_join_must_match_declared_routes() -> None:
    from app.workflow_v3 import validate_workflow_v3

    manifest = _valid_manifest()
    manifest["nodes"] = [
        manifest["nodes"][0],
        _node(
            "split",
            "branch",
            {"branch_key": "checks", "branches": ["a", "b"]},
        ),
        _rule("redline_a", 7, input_from="split"),
        _rule("redline_b", 8, input_from="split"),
        _node("join", "join", {"join_for": "checks", "mode": "all"}),
        manifest["nodes"][-1],
    ]
    manifest["edges"] = [
        {"from": "upstream", "to": "split"},
        {"from": "split", "to": "redline_a", "branch": "a"},
        {"from": "split", "to": "redline_b", "branch": "unknown"},
        {"from": "redline_a", "to": "join"},
        {"from": "redline_b", "to": "join"},
        {"from": "join", "to": "downstream"},
    ]

    codes = {error.code for error in validate_workflow_v3(manifest)}

    assert "workflow_branch_edge_unknown" in codes
    assert "workflow_branch_edge_missing" in codes

    manifest["edges"][2]["branch"] = "b"
    join = next(node for node in manifest["nodes"] if node["kind"] == "join")
    join["config"]["join_for"] = "missing"
    join["config"]["mode"] = "any"

    codes = {error.code for error in validate_workflow_v3(manifest)}

    assert "workflow_join_branch_unknown" in codes
    assert "workflow_join_mode_invalid" in codes


def test_v3_exclusive_branch_requires_one_condition_per_route() -> None:
    """Exclusive routing must be explicit so runtime never guesses a route."""

    from app.workflow_v3 import validate_workflow_v3

    manifest = _valid_manifest()
    manifest["nodes"] = [
        manifest["nodes"][0],
        _node(
            "split",
            "branch",
            {
                "branch_key": "source",
                "branches": ["domestic", "overseas"],
                "mode": "exclusive",
            },
        ),
        _rule("redline_domestic", 7, input_from="split"),
        _rule("redline_overseas", 8, input_from="split"),
        _node(
            "join",
            "join",
            {
                "join_for": "source",
                "mode": "all",
                "merge_policy": {"score": "minimum_cap"},
            },
        ),
        manifest["nodes"][-1],
    ]
    manifest["edges"] = [
        {"from": "upstream", "to": "split"},
        {
            "from": "split",
            "to": "redline_domestic",
            "branch": "domestic",
            "condition": {
                "op": "eq",
                "path": "steps.upstream.output.source",
                "value": "domestic",
            },
        },
        {
            "from": "split",
            "to": "redline_overseas",
            "branch": "overseas",
            "condition": {
                "op": "eq",
                "path": "steps.upstream.output.source",
                "value": "overseas",
            },
        },
        {"from": "redline_domestic", "to": "join"},
        {"from": "redline_overseas", "to": "join"},
        {"from": "join", "to": "downstream"},
    ]

    assert validate_workflow_v3(manifest) == []

    del manifest["edges"][2]["condition"]

    codes = {error.code for error in validate_workflow_v3(manifest)}
    assert "workflow_exclusive_condition_required" in codes


def test_v3_parallel_branch_rejects_route_conditions() -> None:
    """Parallel branches run every route, so a condition must not be ignored."""

    from app.workflow_v3 import validate_workflow_v3

    manifest = _valid_manifest()
    manifest["nodes"] = [
        manifest["nodes"][0],
        _node(
            "split",
            "branch",
            {
                "branch_key": "checks",
                "branches": ["a", "b"],
                "mode": "parallel",
            },
        ),
        _rule("redline_a", 7, input_from="split"),
        _rule("redline_b", 8, input_from="split"),
        _node(
            "join",
            "join",
            {
                "join_for": "checks",
                "mode": "all",
                "merge_policy": {"score": "minimum_cap"},
            },
        ),
        manifest["nodes"][-1],
    ]
    manifest["edges"] = [
        {"from": "upstream", "to": "split"},
        {
            "from": "split",
            "to": "redline_a",
            "branch": "a",
            "condition": {
                "op": "eq",
                "path": "steps.upstream.output.source",
                "value": "domestic",
            },
        },
        {"from": "split", "to": "redline_b", "branch": "b"},
        {"from": "redline_a", "to": "join"},
        {"from": "redline_b", "to": "join"},
        {"from": "join", "to": "downstream"},
    ]

    codes = {error.code for error in validate_workflow_v3(manifest)}

    assert "workflow_parallel_condition_forbidden" in codes

def test_v3_rejects_unknown_or_invalid_error_and_retry_policies() -> None:
    from app.workflow_v3 import validate_workflow_v3

    manifest = _valid_manifest()
    prompt = next(node for node in manifest["nodes"] if node["kind"] == "prompt")
    prompt["error_policy"] = {"kind": "execute_shell", "command": "ignored"}
    prompt["retry_policy"] = {
        "kind": "fixed",
        "max_attempts": 99,
        "delay_seconds": -1,
        "sql_text": "ignored",
    }

    codes = {error.code for error in validate_workflow_v3(manifest)}

    assert "workflow_error_policy_invalid" in codes
    assert "workflow_error_policy_field_unknown" in codes
    assert "workflow_retry_policy_invalid" in codes
    assert "workflow_retry_policy_field_unknown" in codes


@pytest.mark.parametrize("delay_seconds", [float("inf"), float("nan"), 1e308])
def test_v3_rejects_non_finite_or_unbounded_retry_delay(
    delay_seconds: float,
) -> None:
    from app.workflow_v3 import validate_workflow_v3

    manifest = _valid_manifest()
    prompt = next(node for node in manifest["nodes"] if node["kind"] == "prompt")
    prompt["retry_policy"]["delay_seconds"] = delay_seconds

    codes = {error.code for error in validate_workflow_v3(manifest)}

    assert "workflow_retry_policy_invalid" in codes


def test_v3_validates_vote_sampling_fields() -> None:
    """多次采样投票：vote_samples 1..9、vote_temperature 0..2；越界要报错，合法要静默。"""
    from app.workflow_v3 import validate_workflow_v3, VOTE_MAX_SAMPLES

    manifest = _valid_manifest()
    prompt = next(node for node in manifest["nodes"] if node.get("kind") == "prompt")
    prompt["config"]["vote_samples"] = VOTE_MAX_SAMPLES + 1
    prompt["config"]["vote_temperature"] = 5
    codes = {error.code for error in validate_workflow_v3(manifest)}
    assert "workflow_model_vote_samples_invalid" in codes
    assert "workflow_model_vote_temperature_invalid" in codes

    prompt["config"]["vote_samples"] = 5
    prompt["config"]["vote_temperature"] = 0.7
    codes = {error.code for error in validate_workflow_v3(manifest)}
    assert "workflow_model_vote_samples_invalid" not in codes
    assert "workflow_model_vote_temperature_invalid" not in codes
    assert "workflow_node_config_field_unknown" not in codes


def test_v3_validates_pairwise_anchor_fields() -> None:
    """成对锚点比较：pairwise_anchors 布尔、pairwise_samples 1..5、pairwise_prompt 非空 ≤4000。"""
    from app.workflow_v3 import validate_workflow_v3, PAIRWISE_MAX_SAMPLES

    manifest = _valid_manifest()
    prompt = next(node for node in manifest["nodes"] if node.get("kind") == "prompt")
    prompt["config"]["pairwise_anchors"] = "yes"
    prompt["config"]["pairwise_samples"] = PAIRWISE_MAX_SAMPLES + 1
    prompt["config"]["pairwise_prompt"] = "   "
    codes = {error.code for error in validate_workflow_v3(manifest)}
    assert {"workflow_model_pairwise_anchors_invalid", "workflow_model_pairwise_samples_invalid",
            "workflow_model_pairwise_prompt_invalid"} <= codes

    prompt["config"]["pairwise_anchors"] = True
    prompt["config"]["pairwise_samples"] = 2
    prompt["config"]["pairwise_prompt"] = "待评图相对参照图更好、相当还是更差？"
    codes = {error.code for error in validate_workflow_v3(manifest)}
    assert not {c for c in codes if c.startswith("workflow_model_pairwise")}
    assert "workflow_node_config_field_unknown" not in codes


def _first_seen_node() -> dict:
    return _node(
        "first_seen",
        "image_first_seen_lookup",
        {
            "provider_connector_key": "image.lookup.provider",
            "request_contract_version_id": 21,
            "response_contract_version_id": 22,
            "wait_timeout_seconds": 3600,
            "timeout_policy": "manual_review",
        },
        output_schema=deepcopy(_FIRST_SEEN_SCHEMA),
    )


def _split_manifest(mode: str, *, wait_on_arm: bool) -> dict:
    manifest = _valid_manifest()
    split_config = {"branch_key": "checks", "branches": ["a", "b"], "mode": mode}
    nodes = [
        manifest["nodes"][0],
        _node("split", "branch", split_config),
        _rule("redline_a", 7, input_from="split"),
        _rule("redline_b", 8, input_from="split"),
        _node("join", "join", {"join_for": "checks", "mode": "all" if mode == "parallel" else "any",
                                "merge_policy": {"score": "minimum_cap"}}),
        _first_seen_node(),
        manifest["nodes"][-1],
    ]
    route_a = {"from": "split", "to": "redline_a", "branch": "a"}
    route_b = {"from": "split", "to": "redline_b", "branch": "b"}
    if mode == "exclusive":
        condition = {"op": "eq", "path": "steps.upstream.output.source", "value": "domestic"}
        route_a["condition"] = condition
        route_b["condition"] = {"op": "ne", "path": "steps.upstream.output.source", "value": "domestic"}
    if wait_on_arm:
        edges = [{"from": "upstream", "to": "split"}, route_a, route_b,
                 {"from": "redline_a", "to": "first_seen"}, {"from": "first_seen", "to": "join"},
                 {"from": "redline_b", "to": "join"}, {"from": "join", "to": "downstream"}]
    else:
        edges = [{"from": "upstream", "to": "split"}, route_a, route_b,
                 {"from": "redline_a", "to": "join"}, {"from": "redline_b", "to": "join"},
                 {"from": "join", "to": "first_seen"}, {"from": "first_seen", "to": "downstream"}]
    manifest["nodes"] = nodes
    manifest["edges"] = edges
    return manifest


def test_v3_rejects_external_wait_inside_a_parallel_route() -> None:
    """Entering waiting_external would cancel the sibling route's paid calls."""

    from app.workflow_v3 import validate_workflow_v3

    inside = {e.code for e in validate_workflow_v3(_split_manifest("parallel", wait_on_arm=True))}
    assert "workflow_external_wait_in_parallel_branch" in inside
    after_join = {e.code for e in validate_workflow_v3(_split_manifest("parallel", wait_on_arm=False))}
    assert "workflow_external_wait_in_parallel_branch" not in after_join
    exclusive = {e.code for e in validate_workflow_v3(_split_manifest("exclusive", wait_on_arm=True))}
    assert "workflow_external_wait_in_parallel_branch" not in exclusive

from __future__ import annotations

import json

import pytest
from fastapi import FastAPI
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
from sqlalchemy import create_engine
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session, sessionmaker
from sqlalchemy.pool import StaticPool

from app.database import Base, get_db
from app.evaluation_components import (
    create_component_asset,
    generate_component_version,
    save_component_working_copy,
)
from app.models import (
    EvaluationCategoryProfile,
    ModelConfig,
    ModelRegistryEntry,
    WorkflowDefinition,
    User,
)
from app.vision_input_policy import SEED_20_PROFILE

from test_workflow_v3 import _valid_manifest


def _engine(tmp_path):
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'workflow-working-copy.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    return engine


def _seed_managed_dependencies(db: Session) -> tuple[WorkflowDefinition, dict]:
    db.add(
        EvaluationCategoryProfile(
            category_key="space_image",
            display_name="空间图",
            status="active",
            allowed_mime_types_json='["image/jpeg"]',
            preprocess_config_json="{}",
            pipeline_config_json="{}",
            created_by="ops",
        )
    )
    source_model = ModelConfig(
        name="工作流测试模型",
        provider="fixture",
        protocol="custom_json",
        capabilities_json='["text","vision","structured_output"]',
        vision_capability_profile_json=json.dumps(
            dict(SEED_20_PROFILE), separators=(",", ":"), sort_keys=True
        ),
        description="dry-run",
        base_url="https://fixture.invalid",
        api_path="/dry-run",
        model_id="fixture-v3",
        temperature=0.1,
        max_tokens=8192,
        timeout_seconds=120,
        max_retries=1,
        max_concurrency=2,
        thinking_mode="auto",
        structured_output=True,
        active=True,
    )
    db.add(source_model)
    db.flush()
    model = ModelRegistryEntry(
        role="main",
        name="工作流测试模型",
        provider="fixture",
        protocol="custom_json",
        capabilities_json='["text","vision","structured_output"]',
        vision_capability_profile_json=json.dumps(
            dict(SEED_20_PROFILE), separators=(",", ":"), sort_keys=True
        ),
        description="dry-run",
        base_url="https://fixture.invalid",
        api_path="/dry-run",
        model_id="fixture-v3",
        temperature=0.1,
        max_tokens=8192,
        timeout_seconds=120,
        max_retries=1,
        max_concurrency=2,
        thinking_mode="auto",
        level="standard",
        structured_output=True,
        active=True,
        source_model_config_id=source_model.id,
        created_by="ops",
    )
    db.add(model)
    db.flush()
    prompt_asset = create_component_asset(
        db,
        category_key="space_image",
        asset_key="prompt_c",
        kind="prompt",
        display_name="提示词 C",
        prompt_stage="C",
        created_by="ops",
    )
    save_component_working_copy(
        db,
        asset_id=prompt_asset.id,
        expected_lock_version=1,
        content={
            "stage": "C",
            "system_prompt": "你是空间图评测助手。",
            "user_prompt": "请返回结构化结果。",
            "rubric_version": "rubric-v1",
        },
        updated_by="ops",
    )
    prompt_version = generate_component_version(
        db, asset_id=prompt_asset.id, created_by="ops"
    )
    rule_asset = create_component_asset(
        db,
        category_key="space_image",
        asset_key="eligibility_redline",
        kind="rule_module",
        display_name="准入与红线",
        module_type_key="eligibility_redline",
        created_by="ops",
    )
    save_component_working_copy(
        db,
        asset_id=rule_asset.id,
        expected_lock_version=1,
        content={
            "enabled": True,
            "hit_level": "L5",
            "hit_score_cap": 49,
            "rules": [],
        },
        updated_by="ops",
    )
    rule_version = generate_component_version(
        db, asset_id=rule_asset.id, created_by="ops"
    )
    definition = WorkflowDefinition(
        workflow_key="space_image.main",
        name="空间图主评测",
        description="",
        owner="platform",
        allowed_categories_json='["space_image"]',
        status="active",
        created_by="ops",
    )
    db.add(definition)
    db.flush()
    manifest = _valid_manifest()
    prompt = next(node for node in manifest["nodes"] if node["kind"] == "prompt")
    prompt["config"]["prompt_version_id"] = prompt_version.id
    prompt["config"]["model_registry_id"] = model.id
    rule = next(
        node for node in manifest["nodes"] if node["kind"] == "rule_module"
    )
    rule["config"]["rule_module_version_id"] = rule_version.id
    return definition, manifest

def test_managed_foreach_reuses_prompt_references_and_freezes_nested_executor(
    tmp_path,
) -> None:
    from app.workflow_v3 import (
        freeze_component_references,
        validate_managed_workflow_v3,
    )

    engine = _engine(tmp_path)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            _, manifest = _seed_managed_dependencies(db)
            prompt = next(
                node for node in manifest["nodes"] if node["kind"] == "prompt"
            )
            executor = dict(prompt["config"])
            executor.update(
                {
                    "kind": "prompt",
                    "input_modality": "image",
                    "thinking_level": "none",
                    "thinking_enabled": False,
                    "structured_output": True,
                    "output_contract": {
                        "mode": "json_schema",
                        "name": "case_image_result",
                        "description": "案例单图结果",
                        "strict": True,
                        "schema": {
                            "type": "object",
                            "properties": {"score": {"type": "number"}},
                            "required": ["score"],
                            "additionalProperties": False,
                        },
                    },
                }
            )
            model = db.get(ModelRegistryEntry, executor["model_registry_id"])
            assert model is not None
            source_model = db.get(ModelConfig, model.source_model_config_id)
            assert source_model is not None
            model.protocol = "openai_chat"
            source_model.protocol = "openai_chat"
            prompt["kind"] = "foreach_image"
            prompt["display_name"] = "逐图片评测"
            prompt["config"] = {
                "items_path": "$.case.images",
                "item_identity_path": "$.cloud_id",
                "max_items": 1000,
                "max_parallel_items": 4,
                "failure_policy": "fail_open",
                "item_executor": executor,
            }

            assert validate_managed_workflow_v3(db, manifest) == []
            frozen = freeze_component_references(db, manifest)
            frozen_executor = frozen["nodes"][1]["config"]["item_executor"]

            assert frozen_executor["thinking_enabled"] is False
            assert frozen_executor["thinking_level"] == "none"
            assert frozen_executor["component_snapshot"]["kind"] == "prompt"
            assert frozen_executor["model_snapshot"]["protocol"] == "openai_chat"
            assert len(frozen_executor["vision_policy_hash"]) == 64
    finally:
        engine.dispose()

def test_mechanism_generation_requires_a_registered_node_executor(
    monkeypatch, tmp_path
) -> None:
    """An otherwise valid version must not activate with a missing executor."""

    from app.workflow_working_copies import (
        WorkflowWorkingCopyError,
        generate_evaluation_mechanism_version,
        get_or_create_workflow_working_copy,
    )
    import app.workflow_node_executors as executors

    engine = _engine(tmp_path)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            definition, manifest = _seed_managed_dependencies(db)
            get_or_create_workflow_working_copy(
                db,
                workflow_definition_id=definition.id,
                initial_manifest=manifest,
                actor="ops",
            )
            monkeypatch.delitem(executors._EXECUTORS, "prompt")

            with pytest.raises(WorkflowWorkingCopyError) as error:
                generate_evaluation_mechanism_version(
                    db,
                    workflow_definition_id=definition.id,
                    expected_lock_version=1,
                    created_by="ops",
                )

            assert error.value.code == "workflow_executor_missing"
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_working_copy_save_conflict_and_generate_frozen_v3_version(tmp_path) -> None:
    from app.workflow_registry import persisted_workflow_manifest
    from app.workflow_working_copies import (
        WorkflowWorkingCopyError,
        generate_evaluation_mechanism_version,
        get_or_create_workflow_working_copy,
        save_workflow_working_copy,
    )

    engine = _engine(tmp_path)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            from tests.v3_contract_fixtures import add_active_v3_contract

            active_v3 = add_active_v3_contract(db)
            definition, manifest = _seed_managed_dependencies(db)
            working = get_or_create_workflow_working_copy(
                db,
                workflow_definition_id=definition.id,
                initial_manifest=manifest,
                actor="ops",
            )
            assert working.lock_version == 1

            manifest["nodes"][1]["display_name"] = "空间理解提示词"
            saved = save_workflow_working_copy(
                db,
                workflow_definition_id=definition.id,
                expected_lock_version=1,
                manifest=manifest,
                actor="ops",
            )
            assert saved.lock_version == 2
            with pytest.raises(WorkflowWorkingCopyError) as excinfo:
                save_workflow_working_copy(
                    db,
                    workflow_definition_id=definition.id,
                    expected_lock_version=1,
                    manifest=manifest,
                    actor="stale-ops",
                )
            assert excinfo.value.code == "workflow_working_copy_conflict"
            assert excinfo.value.status_code == 409

            version = generate_evaluation_mechanism_version(
                db,
                workflow_definition_id=definition.id,
                expected_lock_version=2,
                created_by="ops",
            )
            frozen = persisted_workflow_manifest(version)

            assert version.status == "active"
            assert version.workflow_schema_version == "workflow-v3"
            assert version.version == "1"
            assert json.loads(version.validation_report_json) == {
                "ok": True,
                "errors": [],
            }
            assert frozen["nodes"][1]["display_name"] == "空间理解提示词"
            assert frozen["nodes"][0]["config"]["input_mode"] == "initial_intake"
            assert frozen["nodes"][-1]["config"]["completion_mode"] == "continue"
            prompt_config = frozen["nodes"][1]["config"]
            rule_config = frozen["nodes"][2]["config"]
            assert len(prompt_config["component_snapshot"]["content_hash"]) == 64
            assert prompt_config["model_snapshot"]["model_id"] == "fixture-v3"
            assert "base_url" not in prompt_config["model_snapshot"]
            assert prompt_config["model_snapshot"]["runtime_config"][
                "base_url"
            ] == "https://fixture.invalid"
            assert "encrypted_api_key" not in prompt_config["model_snapshot"][
                "runtime_config"
            ]
            assert prompt_config["temperature"] == 0.1
            assert prompt_config["structured_output"] is True
            assert prompt_config["vision_input_policy"]["version"] == (
                "legacy-default"
            )
            assert prompt_config["vision_capability_snapshot"]["profile_key"] == (
                SEED_20_PROFILE["profile_key"]
            )
            assert len(prompt_config["vision_policy_hash"]) == 64
            projection_snapshot = frozen["resource_policy"][
                "legacy_projection_snapshot"
            ]
            assert projection_snapshot["schema_version"] == (
                "legacy-projection-workflow-fields-v1"
            )
            assert projection_snapshot["workflow_fields"]["contract"][
                "spec_version"
            ] == active_v3["contract"]["spec_version"]
            assert len(rule_config["component_snapshot"]["content_hash"]) == 64
            assert json.loads(rule_config["component_snapshot"]["content_json"])[
                "hit_level"
            ] == "L5"
            db.refresh(working)
            assert working.base_workflow_version_id == version.id
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_freeze_rejects_unsupported_vision_policy_and_hash_drift(tmp_path) -> None:
    from copy import deepcopy

    from app.vision_input_policy import default_vision_input_policy, seed_20_capability
    from app.workflow_registry import WorkflowRegistryError
    from app.workflow_v3 import freeze_component_references

    engine = _engine(tmp_path)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            _, manifest = _seed_managed_dependencies(db)
            prompt = next(
                node for node in manifest["nodes"] if node["kind"] == "prompt"
            )
            policy = default_vision_input_policy(seed_20_capability()).as_dict()
            policy["roles"]["target"]["fidelity"] = {
                "mode": "detail",
                "value": "xhigh",
            }
            prompt["config"]["vision_input_policy"] = policy
            model = db.get(
                ModelRegistryEntry, prompt["config"]["model_registry_id"]
            )
            assert model is not None
            limited_profile = dict(SEED_20_PROFILE)
            limited_profile["profile_key"] = "fixture-high-only-v1"
            limited_profile["detail_modes"] = ["low", "high"]
            model.vision_capability_profile_json = json.dumps(limited_profile)
            source = db.get(ModelConfig, model.source_model_config_id)
            assert source is not None
            source.vision_capability_profile_json = json.dumps(limited_profile)
            policy["provider_profile"] = limited_profile["profile_key"]

            with pytest.raises(WorkflowRegistryError) as excinfo:
                freeze_component_references(db, manifest)

            assert excinfo.value.code == "workflow_vision_fidelity_unsupported"

            model.vision_capability_profile_json = json.dumps(dict(SEED_20_PROFILE))
            source.vision_capability_profile_json = json.dumps(dict(SEED_20_PROFILE))
            policy["provider_profile"] = SEED_20_PROFILE["profile_key"]
            policy["roles"]["target"]["fidelity"] = {
                "mode": "detail",
                "value": "high",
            }
            frozen = freeze_component_references(db, manifest)
            frozen_prompt = next(
                node for node in frozen["nodes"] if node["kind"] == "prompt"
            )
            frozen_prompt["config"]["vision_policy_hash"] = "0" * 64

            with pytest.raises(WorkflowRegistryError) as excinfo:
                freeze_component_references(db, frozen)

            assert excinfo.value.code == "workflow_vision_policy_hash_drift"
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_operator_owned_node_keeps_policy_but_strips_runtime_vision_fields() -> None:
    from app.vision_input_policy import default_vision_input_policy
    from app.workflow_working_copies import _operator_owned_node

    node = _valid_manifest()["nodes"][1]
    policy = default_vision_input_policy().as_dict()
    node["config"].update(
        {
            "vision_input_policy": policy,
            "vision_capability_snapshot": dict(SEED_20_PROFILE),
            "vision_policy_hash": "a" * 64,
        }
    )

    editable = _operator_owned_node(node)

    assert editable["config"]["vision_input_policy"] == policy
    assert "vision_capability_snapshot" not in editable["config"]
    assert "vision_policy_hash" not in editable["config"]

def test_generation_freezes_knowledge_graph_contract_into_prompt_and_projection(
    tmp_path,
) -> None:
    from app.knowledge_graph_contracts import get_knowledge_graph_contract
    from app.workflow_registry import persisted_workflow_manifest
    from app.workflow_working_copies import (
        generate_evaluation_mechanism_version,
        get_or_create_workflow_working_copy,
    )

    engine = _engine(tmp_path)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            definition, manifest = _seed_managed_dependencies(db)
            contract_key = "kg.model_3d_su.domestic.whole.v1"
            prompt = next(
                node for node in manifest["nodes"] if node["kind"] == "prompt"
            )
            downstream = next(
                node
                for node in manifest["nodes"]
                if node["kind"] == "downstream_projection"
            )
            prompt["config"]["output_contract_key"] = contract_key
            downstream["config"]["field_contract_key"] = contract_key
            get_or_create_workflow_working_copy(
                db,
                workflow_definition_id=definition.id,
                initial_manifest=manifest,
                actor="ops",
            )

            version = generate_evaluation_mechanism_version(
                db,
                workflow_definition_id=definition.id,
                expected_lock_version=1,
                created_by="ops",
            )
            frozen = persisted_workflow_manifest(version)
            frozen_prompt = next(
                node for node in frozen["nodes"] if node["kind"] == "prompt"
            )
            frozen_downstream = next(
                node
                for node in frozen["nodes"]
                if node["kind"] == "downstream_projection"
            )
            contract = get_knowledge_graph_contract(contract_key)
            assert contract is not None

            assert frozen_prompt["config"]["output_contract_snapshot"] == (
                contract.snapshot()
            )
            assert frozen_prompt["output_schema"] == contract.output_schema
            assert frozen_downstream["config"]["field_contract_snapshot"] == (
                contract.snapshot()
            )
    finally:
        engine.dispose()
