def test_working_copy_api_initializes_validates_and_generates_without_status() -> None:
    from app.workflow_registry_api import build_workflow_registry_router

    engine = create_engine(
        "sqlite://",
        connect_args={"check_same_thread": False},
        poolclass=StaticPool,
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    sessions = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    with sessions() as db:
        _, manifest = _seed_managed_dependencies(db)
        db.commit()

    def db_override():
        with sessions() as db:
            yield db

    def user_override() -> User:
        return User(
            id=1,
            username="manager",
            password_hash="x",
            display_name="运营管理员",
            role="product_rd",
            is_admin=False,
        )

    app = FastAPI()
    app.include_router(build_workflow_registry_router(user_override))
    app.dependency_overrides[get_db] = db_override
    client = TestClient(app)
    try:
        initialized = client.put(
            "/api/workflows/space_image.main/working-copy",
            json={"expected_lock_version": 0, "manifest": manifest},
        )
        assert initialized.status_code == 200, initialized.text
        assert initialized.json()["lock_version"] == 1

        validated = client.post(
            "/api/workflows/space_image.main/working-copy/validate"
        )
        assert validated.status_code == 200
        assert validated.json()["validation_report"]["ok"] is True

        generated = client.post(
            "/api/workflows/space_image.main/generate-version",
            json={"expected_lock_version": 1},
        )
        assert generated.status_code == 201, generated.text
        assert generated.json()["workflow_schema_version"] == "workflow-v3"
        assert generated.json()["validation_report"] == {
            "ok": True,
            "errors": [],
        }
        assert "status" not in generated.json()
        direct = client.post(
            "/api/workflows/space_image.main/versions",
            json={"version": "manual-v3", "manifest": manifest},
        )
        assert direct.status_code == 409
        assert direct.json()["detail"]["code"] == "workflow_v3_working_copy_required"
        transition = client.post(
            "/api/workflows/space_image.main/versions/1/transition",
            json={"target_status": "deprecated"},
        )
        assert transition.status_code == 409
        assert transition.json()["detail"]["code"] == "workflow_v3_lifecycle_not_supported"
    finally:
        client.close()
        engine.dispose()


def test_legacy_v2_conversion_assigns_stable_instance_ids_and_module_versions() -> None:
    from app.workflow_working_copies import legacy_v2_to_v3_working_copy

    schema = {"type": "object"}
    common = {
        "input_schema": schema,
        "output_schema": schema,
        "error_policy": {"kind": "fail"},
        "retry_policy": {"kind": "fixed", "max_attempts": 2, "delay_seconds": 0},
        "evidence_fields": ["request_id"],
    }
    legacy = {
        "schema_version": "workflow-v2",
        "category_key": "space_image",
        "lane_key": "evaluation",
        "run_modes": ["evaluation", "stock_regression"],
        "nodes": [
            {
                "key": "prompt-a",
                "kind": "prompt",
                "display_name": "提示词 A",
                "config": {
                    "asset_key": "prompt_a",
                    "prompt_ref": "prompt:A@v1",
                    "model_registry_id": 3,
                    "max_tokens": 4096,
                    "thinking_level": "medium",
                },
                **common,
            },
            {
                "key": "rule-contract",
                "kind": "rule_contract",
                "display_name": "规则合同",
                "config": {"asset_key": "rule_contract", "rule_revision_id": 5},
                **common,
            },
        ],
        "edges": [{"from": "prompt-a", "to": "rule-contract"}],
        "queue_class": "validation",
        "input_schema": schema,
        "output_schema": schema,
        "resource_policy": {"max_parallel": 1},
    }

    first = legacy_v2_to_v3_working_copy(
        legacy,
        prompt_version_ids={"prompt:A@v1": 11},
        rule_module_version_ids={"eligibility_redline": 7},
    )
    second = legacy_v2_to_v3_working_copy(
        legacy,
        prompt_version_ids={"prompt:A@v1": 11},
        rule_module_version_ids={"eligibility_redline": 7},
    )

    assert first == second
    assert [node["node_instance_id"] for node in first["nodes"]] == [
        "prompt-a",
        "rule-contract",
    ]
    assert first["nodes"][0]["config"]["prompt_version_id"] == 11
    assert first["nodes"][1]["kind"] == "rule_module"
    assert first["nodes"][1]["config"]["module_type_key"] == "eligibility_redline"
    assert first["nodes"][1]["config"]["rule_module_version_id"] == 7


def test_mechanism_generation_rejects_redline_cap_outside_level_band(tmp_path) -> None:
    """发布新机制版本时拦截“红线封顶分与命中等级分数段矛盾”（跨模块校验）。"""
    from test_workflow_v3 import _rule
    from app.workflow_working_copies import (
        WorkflowWorkingCopyError,
        generate_evaluation_mechanism_version,
        get_or_create_workflow_working_copy,
    )

    engine = _engine(tmp_path)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            from tests.v3_contract_fixtures import add_active_v3_contract

            add_active_v3_contract(db)
            definition, manifest = _seed_managed_dependencies(db)
            matcher_asset = create_component_asset(
                db,
                category_key="space_image",
                asset_key="score_level_matcher",
                kind="rule_module",
                display_name="分数汇总与等级撮合",
                module_type_key="score_level_matcher",
                created_by="ops",
            )
            save_component_working_copy(
                db,
                asset_id=matcher_asset.id,
                expected_lock_version=1,
                content={
                    "version": "category-level-scale-v1",
                    "levels": [
                        {"level": "L1", "enabled": True, "min_score": 80},
                        {"level": "L2", "enabled": True, "min_score": 60},
                        {"level": "L3", "enabled": True, "min_score": 40},
                        {"level": "L4", "enabled": True, "min_score": 20},
                        {"level": "L5", "enabled": True, "min_score": 0},
                    ],
                },
                updated_by="ops",
            )
            matcher_version = generate_component_version(
                db, asset_id=matcher_asset.id, created_by="ops"
            )
            matcher_node = _rule("matcher_1", matcher_version.id, input_from="redline_1")
            matcher_node["config"]["asset_key"] = "score_level_matcher"
            matcher_node["config"]["module_type_key"] = "score_level_matcher"
            manifest["nodes"].insert(3, matcher_node)
            manifest["edges"] = [
                {"from": "upstream", "to": "prompt_1"},
                {"from": "prompt_1", "to": "redline_1"},
                {"from": "redline_1", "to": "matcher_1"},
                {"from": "matcher_1", "to": "downstream"},
            ]
            get_or_create_workflow_working_copy(
                db,
                workflow_definition_id=definition.id,
                initial_manifest=manifest,
                actor="ops",
            )
            # 红线命中 L5 封顶 49，但 L5 的分数段只有 [0, 19]。
            with pytest.raises(WorkflowWorkingCopyError) as excinfo:
                generate_evaluation_mechanism_version(
                    db,
                    workflow_definition_id=definition.id,
                    expected_lock_version=1,
                    created_by="ops",
                )
            assert excinfo.value.code == "redline_cap_outside_level_band"
            assert "[0, 19]" in str(excinfo.value)
    finally:
        engine.dispose()

===== FILE backend/tests/test_write_permission_guards.py =====
"""写接口不能只要求登录：v3 候选写入、金丝雀推进、投影 manifest 落库都要写权限。"""
import pytest
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient

from app.database import get_db
from app.main import app, current_user
from app.models import User

_V3 = "/api/category-evaluation/v3-config"

WRITE_ROUTES = [
    # (method, path, 放行角色所需权限)
    ("post", f"{_V3}/", "evaluation_mechanisms:write"),
    ("post", f"{_V3}/inspiration_image/revisions", "evaluation_mechanisms:write"),
    ("post", f"{_V3}/inspiration_image/revisions/1/rebase", "evaluation_mechanisms:write"),
    ("post", "/api/canary-runs", "releases:write"),
    ("post", "/api/canary-runs/run-1/transitions/preflight", "releases:write"),
    ("post", "/api/canary-runs/run-1/cancel", "releases:write"),
    ("post", "/api/canary-runs/run-1/fail", "releases:write"),
    ("post", "/api/projection-contracts/1/manifest", "releases:write"),
]


def _call(role: str, method: str, path: str):
    previous = dict(app.dependency_overrides)
    app.dependency_overrides[current_user] = lambda: User(
        username=role, password_hash="unused", role=role, is_admin=role == "admin"
    )

    def no_database():
        yield None

    app.dependency_overrides[get_db] = no_database
    try:
        return TestClient(app, raise_server_exceptions=False).request(method, path, json={})
    finally:
        app.dependency_overrides.clear()
        app.dependency_overrides.update(previous)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("role", ["viewer", "reviewer", "analyst"])
@pytest.mark.parametrize("method,path,_permission", WRITE_ROUTES)
def test_read_only_roles_cannot_call_write_routes(role, method, path, _permission):
    response = _call(role, method, path)
    assert response.status_code == 403, (role, path, response.text)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("method,path,permission", WRITE_ROUTES)
def test_admin_passes_the_permission_gate(method, path, permission):
    # Coarse legacy dependency only; real-cookie action/category tests exercise
    # current_user and reject non-admin publication/activation separately.
    response = _call("admin", method, path)
    assert response.status_code != 403, (path, permission, response.text)


def test_v3_config_reads_still_only_need_login():
    response = _call("viewer", "get", f"{_V3}/profiles")
    assert response.status_code == 200, response.text

===== FILE backend/tests/v3_contract_fixtures.py =====
from copy import deepcopy

from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.dimension_schema_registry import canonical_json
from app.inspiration_category_seed import (
    build_inspiration_classification_map,
    build_inspiration_subcategory_dimensions,
    build_inspiration_v3_contract,
)
from app.models import CategoryEvaluationV3Config


def add_active_v3_contract(
    db: Session,
    category_key: str = "space_image",
) -> dict:
    contract = deepcopy(build_inspiration_v3_contract())
    contract["category_key"] = category_key
    contract["spec_version"] = f"{category_key}-test-v3"
    classification_map = deepcopy(build_inspiration_classification_map())
    dimensions = deepcopy(build_inspiration_subcategory_dimensions())
    row = CategoryEvaluationV3Config(
        category_key=category_key,
        display_name=f"{category_key} 测试 v3",
        status="active",
        contract_json=canonical_json(contract),
        classification_map_json=canonical_json(classification_map),
        subcategory_dimensions_json=canonical_json(dimensions),
        revision=1,
        created_by="test:v3-only",
    )
    db.add(row)
    db.flush()
    return {
        "contract": contract,
        "classification_map": classification_map,
        "subcategory_dimensions": dimensions,
    }

export default function App() {
  const location = useLocation()
  const me = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["me"],
    queryFn: () => api<User>("/api/auth/me"),
    retry: false,
    refetchInterval: 30_000,
    refetchOnWindowFocus: "always",
  })

  if (me.isLoading) {
    return (
      <div className="flex min-h-[100dvh] items-center justify-center bg-white">
        <div className="text-center"><div className="font-editorial text-5xl font-bold">3d66</div><div className="mx-auto mt-5 h-1 w-28 overflow-hidden bg-alias-surface-009"><div className="status-pulse h-full w-1/2 bg-primary" /></div><p className="mt-4 text-sm text-[var(--muted)]">正在进入标签系统</p></div>
      </div>
    )
  }

  const unauthenticated = me.error instanceof ApiError && me.error.status === 401
  if (me.isError && !unauthenticated) {
    const permissionDenied = me.error instanceof ApiError && me.error.status === 403
    return (
      <div className="flex min-h-[100dvh] items-center justify-center bg-workspace px-5">
        <div className="w-full max-w-lg border-y border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-white px-6 py-8 text-center">
          <WarningCircle className="mx-auto text-warning" size={34} weight="fill" />
          <h1 className="font-editorial mt-4 text-2xl font-bold">
            {permissionDenied ? "当前账号无法进入标签系统" : "暂时无法连接标签系统"}
          </h1>
          <p className="mt-3 text-sm leading-6 text-[var(--muted)]">
            {permissionDenied
              ? "请联系管理员确认账号权限，或使用具备访问权限的账号重新登录。"
              : "请检查网络和服务状态。连接恢复后可以直接重试，不会影响已经保存的记录。"}
          </p>
          <Button className="mt-6" onClick={() => me.refetch()}><ArrowClockwise />重新连接</Button>
        </div>
      </div>
    )
  }
  const user = unauthenticated ? undefined : me.data
  scopeCorrectionDrafts(user?.id ?? null)

  return (
    <>
      <Routes>
        <Route path="/login" element={<LoginPage user={user} />} />
        {user ? (
          <Route element={<AppShell key={user.id} user={user} />}>
            <Route index element={<Navigate to={roleHome(user)} replace />} />
            <Route path="account" element={<AccountPage />} />
            <Route path="workflow/dashboard" element={<Suspense fallback={<RouteLoading />}><DashboardPage /></Suspense>} />
            <Route path="workflow/incremental" element={<Suspense fallback={<RouteLoading />}><IncrementalWorkspacePage /></Suspense>} />
            <Route path="workflow/incremental/dashboard" element={<Suspense fallback={<RouteLoading />}><IncrementalDashboardPage /></Suspense>} />
            <Route path="workflow/incremental/correction" element={<Suspense fallback={<RouteLoading />}><IncrementalCorrectionPage /></Suspense>} />
            <Route path="workflow/incremental/correction-analysis" element={<Suspense fallback={<RouteLoading />}><CorrectionAnalysisPage /></Suspense>} />
            <Route path="workflow/incremental/review-capacity" element={<LegacyRoute><Suspense fallback={<RouteLoading />}><DailyReviewPage /></Suspense></LegacyRoute>} />
            <Route path="workflow/operations" element={<Suspense fallback={<RouteLoading />}><OperationsCenterPage /></Suspense>} />
            <Route path="workflow/operations/detail" element={<Suspense fallback={<RouteLoading />}><OperationsDetailPage /></Suspense>} />
            <Route path="workflow/failures" element={<Suspense fallback={<RouteLoading />}><FailureConsolePage /></Suspense>} />
            <Route path="workflow/automation" element={<Suspense fallback={<RouteLoading />}><AutomationOverviewPage /></Suspense>} />
            <Route path="workflow/quality-assets" element={<Suspense fallback={<RouteLoading />}><QualityAssetsPage /></Suspense>} />
            <Route path="workflow/production-line" element={<LegacyRoute><Suspense fallback={<RouteLoading />}><EvaluationPackagePipelinePage user={user} /></Suspense></LegacyRoute>} />
            <Route path="workflow/materials/packages" element={<AssetsPage />} />
            <Route path="workflow/materials/assets" element={<Navigate to="/workflow/materials/packages" replace />} />
            <Route path="workflow/materials/jobs" element={<JobsPage />} />
            <Route path="workflow/review/:reviewView" element={<Suspense fallback={<RouteLoading />}><ReviewPage user={user} /></Suspense>} />
            <Route path="workflow/optimization/cases" element={<Suspense fallback={<RouteLoading />}><OptimizationCasesPage /></Suspense>} />
            <Route path="workflow/optimization/automation" element={<Suspense fallback={<RouteLoading />}><AutomationControlPage /></Suspense>} />

            <Route path="workflow/governance/canary" element={<Suspense fallback={<RouteLoading />}><CanaryRunsPage /></Suspense>} />
            <Route path="workflow/governance/audit" element={<Suspense fallback={<RouteLoading />}><AuditEventsPage /></Suspense>} />
            <Route path="workflow/governance/projections" element={<Suspense fallback={<RouteLoading />}><ProjectionGovernancePage /></Suspense>} />
            <Route path="workflow/governance/tag-demand-contracts" element={<Suspense fallback={<RouteLoading />}><TagDemandContractsPage /></Suspense>} />
            <Route path="workflow/governance/workflow-manager" element={<Suspense fallback={<RouteLoading />}><WorkflowComposerPage /></Suspense>} />

            <Route path="assets" element={<Navigate to="/workflow/materials/packages" replace />} />
            <Route path="jobs" element={<Navigate to="/workflow/materials/jobs" replace />} />
            <Route path="review" element={<Navigate to="/workflow/review/low-confidence" replace />} />
            <Route path="review/:reviewStage" element={<LegacyReviewRedirect />} />
            <Route path="prompts" element={<Navigate to="/workflow/governance/workflow-manager/prompts" replace />} />
            <Route path="model" element={<Navigate to="/workflow/governance/model-registry" replace />} />
            <Route path="sample-sets" element={<Navigate to="/legacy/sample-sets" replace />} />
            <Route path="historical-corrections" element={<Navigate to="/legacy/historical-corrections" replace />} />
            <Route path="migrations" element={<Navigate to="/workflow/models/migration" replace />} />
            <Route path="canary-runs" element={<Navigate to="/workflow/governance/canary" replace />} />
            <Route path="legacy/review/:reviewStage" element={<Suspense fallback={<RouteLoading />}><ReviewPage user={user} /></Suspense>} />
            <Route path="legacy/sample-sets" element={<Suspense fallback={<RouteLoading />}><SampleSetsPage /></Suspense>} />
            <Route path="legacy/historical-corrections" element={<LegacyRoute><Suspense fallback={<RouteLoading />}><HistoricalCorrectionsPage /></Suspense></LegacyRoute>} />
          </Route>
        ) : (
          <Route path="*" element={<Navigate to={`/login?return_to=${encodeURIComponent(location.pathname + location.search)}`} replace />} />
        )}
        <Route path="*" element={<Navigate to={unauthenticated ? "/login" : "/"} replace />} />
      </Routes>
      <Toaster position="top-right" theme="light" richColors closeButton className="visual-toaster" toastOptions={{ className: "visual-toast" }} />
    </>
  )
}

function LegacyReviewRedirect() {
  const { reviewStage } = useParams()
  if (reviewStage === "secondary" || reviewStage === "arbitration") {
    return <Navigate to={`/legacy/review/${reviewStage}`} replace />
  }
  return <Navigate to={`/workflow/review/${reviewStage === "completed" ? "completed" : "low-confidence"}`} replace />
}

function RouteLoading() {
  return <div className="mx-auto shell-content px-5 py-10 md:px-8 lg:px-10"><div className="h-64 animate-pulse border-y border-[var(--line)] bg-white" /></div>
}

import { PageHeading } from "@/components/page-heading"
import { hasUnsavedCorrectionDrafts, correctionDraftsBusy } from "@/features/baseline-regression/correction-draft-store"
import { useEffect, useId, useLayoutEffect, useRef, useState, type ReactNode } from "react"
import { useMotionValue } from "motion/react"

// 五个一级任务域，顺序即运营主链：待办 → 素材 → 增量 → 存量 → 系统。
// 原先的运行中心、自动组批、质量资产不再占一级位置，按「这个能力服务于哪条主线」
// 下沉为二级：运行中心与自动组批服务增量产出，质量资产服务存量回归的基准。
//
// 2026-09-16 老大裁决：「纠偏与发布」整域和「今日人审」退出导航，收进系统管理域末尾的
// 「历史入口」。原因是它们服务的是旧链路——纠偏与发布那套要先有完整会审面板才能发布，
// 而增量结果现在是先发后审、由 Outbox 直接写下游；今日人审是每天抽 100 条的抽样队列，
// 已被 100% 进队的「增量纠偏」取代（生产库 human_review_daily_batches 一行都没有）。
// 路由保留可达，数据一行不动，只是不再占导航位置。
//
// matches 必须列具体路径，不能用 `/workflow/optimization` 这种泛化前缀——该前缀下
// 既有存量回归的页，也有归系统管理的评测机制管理，泛化会让两个域互相吞（详见
// 下方 active 的注释：域间前缀重叠会导致多个域同时点亮）。

    ],

export const workflowDomains = [...primaryWorkflowDomains, advancedWorkflowDomain] as const

// 返回命中的最长前缀长度，未命中为 -1。
// 按路径分段比对（要么全等，要么后面紧跟 `/`），避免 `/assets` 误吞 `/assets-archive`
// 这类同前缀但不同段的路径。
function domainMatchLength(pathname: string, matches: readonly string[]) {
  let best = -1
  for (const match of matches) {
    if (pathname === match || pathname.startsWith(`${match}/`)) {
      best = Math.max(best, match.length)
    }
  }
  return best
}

// 取「最长匹配前缀」的域，而不是数组里第一个匹配的域。
// 域之间存在前缀重叠：纠偏与发布持有 `/workflow/review`，系统管理持有更具体的
// `/workflow/review/model-evaluation`。若按声明顺序取首个匹配，宽前缀会吞掉具体路径，
// 导致人在诊断页却显示「纠偏与发布」。最长前缀胜出让具体路径永远压过宽前缀，
// 与声明顺序无关。
function resolveActiveDomain(pathname: string) {
  let winner: (typeof workflowDomains)[number] | undefined
  let winnerScore = -1
  for (const item of workflowDomains) {
    const score = domainMatchLength(pathname, item.matches)
    if (score > winnerScore) {
      winner = item
      winnerScore = score
    }
  }
  return winnerScore >= 0 ? winner : undefined
}

export function AppShell({ user }: { user: User }) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false)
  const [desktop, setDesktop] = useState(() => window.matchMedia("(min-width: 1024px)").matches)
  const sidebar = useRef<HTMLElement>(null)
  const openButton = useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null)
  const navigationId = useId()
  const ready = useMotionFeaturesReady()
  const reduced = useReducedMotionPreference()
  const canAnimate = ready && !reduced
  const visible = desktop || open
  const drawerX = useMotionValue<string | number>(visible ? 0 : "-100%")
  const location = useLocation()
  const navigate = useNavigate()
  const queryClient = useQueryClient()
  const scrollKey = routeScrollKey(location.pathname, location.search)
  useEffect(() => {
    window.scrollTo({ top: 0, left: 0, behavior: "auto" })
  }, [scrollKey])
  useEffect(() => {
    const media = window.matchMedia("(min-width: 1024px)")
    const update = () => { setDesktop(media.matches); if (media.matches) setOpen(false) }
    media.addEventListener("change", update)
    return () => media.removeEventListener("change", update)
  }, [])
  useLayoutEffect(() => {
    // A failed feature chunk or live reduced-motion change must settle the
    // actual value too. Do not mark this host data-visual-motion: that class's
    // transform:none fallback would expose a closed off-screen drawer.
    if (!canAnimate || desktop) {
      drawerX.stop()
      drawerX.set(visible ? 0 : "-100%")
      // Before domMax is available there is no renderer subscribed to x. Keep
      // the same DOM node (and focus) but apply the ordinary static style too.
      if (sidebar.current) sidebar.current.style.transform = visible ? "none" : "translateX(-100%)"
    }
  }, [canAnimate, desktop, drawerX, visible])
  useEffect(() => { setOpen(false) }, [location.pathname])
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open || desktop) return
    const previousOverflow = document.body.style.overflow
    const returnFocus = openButton.current
    document.body.style.overflow = "hidden"
    const controls = () => [...(sidebar.current?.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('a[href], button:not(:disabled), [tabindex="0"]') ?? [])]
      .filter(node => node.getClientRects().length > 0 && getComputedStyle(node).visibility !== "hidden" && !node.closest("[inert]"))
    // Workspace dialogs render above navigation and retain their modal
    // boundary through exit. Never compete with their first focused frame.
    const workspaceModalPresent = () => Boolean(document.querySelector('[role="dialog"][data-workspace-layer]'))
    if (!workspaceModalPresent()) (controls()[0] ?? sidebar.current)?.focus({ preventScroll: true })
    let hadWorkspaceModal = workspaceModalPresent()
    const modalRemoval = new MutationObserver(() => {
      const present = workspaceModalPresent()
      // The dialog's entry may now be inert behind navigation, so restoring
      // that entry cannot generate a focus event. Resume when it unmounts.
      if (hadWorkspaceModal && !present) (controls()[0] ?? sidebar.current)?.focus({ preventScroll: true })
      hadWorkspaceModal = present
    })
    modalRemoval.observe(document.body, { childList: true, subtree: true })
    const onKeyDown = (event: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (workspaceModalPresent()) return
      if (event.key === "Escape") { event.preventDefault(); setOpen(false); return }
      if (event.key !== "Tab") return
      const items = controls(), first = items[0], last = items.at(-1)
      if (!first) { event.preventDefault(); sidebar.current?.focus(); return }
      if (event.shiftKey && (document.activeElement === first || !sidebar.current?.contains(document.activeElement))) {
        event.preventDefault(); last?.focus()
      } else if (!event.shiftKey && (document.activeElement === last || !sidebar.current?.contains(document.activeElement))) {
        event.preventDefault(); first.focus()
      }
    }
    document.addEventListener("keydown", onKeyDown)
    return () => {
      document.removeEventListener("keydown", onKeyDown)
      modalRemoval.disconnect()
      document.body.style.overflow = previousOverflow
      if (!workspaceModalPresent() && returnFocus?.isConnected && !window.matchMedia("(min-width: 1024px)").matches) returnFocus.focus({ preventScroll: true })
    }
  }, [open, desktop])
  const logout = useMutation({
    mutationFn: () => api("/api/auth/logout", { method: "POST" }),
    onSuccess: async () => {
      // Removing an actively observed query does not clear the observer's last
      // successful value. Cancel an older in-flight auth request first so it
      // cannot restore the previous user after logout, then write an explicit
      // logged-out sentinel and discard data that must not cross user sessions.
      await queryClient.cancelQueries({ queryKey: ["me"], exact: true })
      queryClient.setQueryData(["me"], null)
      queryClient.removeQueries({
        predicate: (query) => query.queryKey[0] !== "me",
      })
      navigate("/login", { replace: true })
    },
  })
  const active = resolveActiveDomain(location.pathname)

  // 面包屑第二段。精确命中优先；再退到最长前缀，用于 `/…/packages/:id` 这类详情页。
  // 前缀匹配必须排除「域首页」那一项（它的 to 等于域自己的 to），否则该域下每个深层页
  // 都会被它前缀吃掉，显示成「系统管理首页」。
  // 都不命中时返回 undefined，面包屑只显示域名，不硬凑第二段。
  const currentTab = active
    ? active.tabs.find((tab) => tab.to === location.pathname) ??
      active.tabs
        .filter((tab) => tab.to !== active.to && location.pathname.startsWith(`${tab.to}/`))
        .sort((a, b) => b.to.length - a.to.length)[0]
    : undefined

  return (
    <div className="min-h-[100dvh] bg-background text-foreground">
      <header inert={open && !desktop} className="sticky top-0 z-30 flex h-16 items-center justify-between bg-background px-4 lg:hidden">
        <div className="flex items-baseline gap-2">
          <span className="visual-item-title">特鹏</span>
          <span className="text-sm font-semibold">标签中台</span>
        </div>
        <Button ref={openButton} type="button" variant="ghost" size="icon" aria-label="打开导航" aria-expanded={open} aria-controls={navigationId} onClick={() => setOpen(true)}>
          <List weight="bold" />
        </Button>
      </header>

      {open && (
        <Button
          type="button"
          variant="ghost"
          className="fixed inset-0 z-40 h-auto w-full rounded-none bg-black/20 p-0 hover:bg-black/20 lg:hidden"
          // The unlayered button press rule must not shrink a full-screen veil.
          style={{ transform: "none" }}
          aria-label="关闭导航遮罩"
          onClick={() => setOpen(false)}
        />
      )}

      <m.aside
        ref={sidebar}
        id={navigationId}
        data-shell-sidebar=""
        role={desktop ? undefined : "dialog"}
        aria-label="导航菜单"
        aria-modal={!desktop && open ? true : undefined}
        aria-hidden={!visible}
        inert={!visible}
        tabIndex={-1}
        initial={false}
        style={{ x: drawerX }}
        animate={{ x: visible ? 0 : "-100%" }}
        transition={canAnimate && !desktop ? (open ? motionTokens.soft : motionTokens.easeOut) : { duration: 0 }}
        className={cn(
          "fixed inset-y-0 left-0 z-50 w-[252px] p-3",
          !visible && "pointer-events-none",
        )}
      >
        <div className="flex h-full min-w-0 flex-col rounded-lg bg-canvas shadow-card">
          <div className="flex shrink-0 items-center gap-3 px-4 py-5">
            <div className="flex size-11 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-md bg-brand">
              <span className="font-editorial hidden text-xl font-bold lg:block" aria-hidden="true">LL</span>
              <Button type="button" size="icon" aria-label="关闭导航" className="lg:hidden" onClick={() => setOpen(false)}><X /></Button>
            </div>
            <div className="min-w-0">
              <p className="visual-item-title">特鹏标签中台</p>
              <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground">Label System · 标签与内容中台底座</p>
            </div>
          </div>
          {/* 五个域同列同款式。系统管理与运营主链的导航层级保持一致。
              二级页面收纳在所属一级域下方，且只在该域为当前域时展开——任何时刻侧栏里
              只有一组二级项，既看得清「我在哪个域的哪一页」，又不会五个域全摊开。
              只有一个二级项的域不展开（那一项等于域本身，展开是重复信息）。 */}
          <nav className="min-h-0 flex-1 space-y-1 overflow-y-auto px-3 py-2" aria-label="主导航">
            <PersonalFavorites />
            {workflowDomains.map((item) => {
              const Icon = item.icon
              const isDomainActive = active?.to === item.to
              const showChildren = isDomainActive && item.tabs.length > 1
              return (
                <div key={item.to}>
                  <Link
                    to={item.to}
                    aria-current={isDomainActive ? "page" : undefined}
                    onClick={() => setOpen(false)}
                    className={cn(
                      "relative isolate flex min-h-11 items-center gap-2.5 rounded-md px-3 text-sm transition-colors duration-[180ms] ease-[var(--visual-ease-out)]",
                      isDomainActive
                        ? "font-bold text-active-text"
                        : "font-semibold text-muted-foreground hover:bg-surface-sunken hover:text-foreground",
                    )}
                  >
                    {isDomainActive && <m.span aria-hidden="true" data-visual-motion="" data-shell-indicator=""
                      className="pointer-events-none absolute inset-0 -z-10 rounded-md bg-active-surface"
                      layoutId={canAnimate && workflowDomains.length <= maxLayoutItems ? `${navigationId}-current` : undefined}
                      initial={false} transition={canAnimate ? motionTokens.spring : { duration: 0 }} />}
                    <Icon size={18} weight={isDomainActive ? "fill" : "regular"} />
                    <span className="min-w-0 flex-1 truncate">{item.label}</span>
                    <span
                      className={cn(
                        "font-data text-xs",
                        isDomainActive ? "text-active-text" : "text-muted-foreground",
                      )}
                    >
                      {item.index}
                    </span>
                  </Link>

                  {showChildren && (
                    <div className="pb-1.5 pl-3 pt-0.5" role="group" aria-label={`${item.label}二级页面`}>
                      <MotionRegion visibleItems={item.tabs.length}>
                      {item.tabs.filter((tab) => tab.to !== "/workflow/governance/users" || user.is_admin).map((tab) => (
                        <NavLink
                          key={tab.to}
                          to={tab.to}
                          end={tab.to === item.to}
                          onClick={() => setOpen(false)}
                          className={({ isActive }) =>
                            cn(
                              "flex min-h-hit items-center gap-2 rounded-sm pl-2.5 pr-2 text-xs transition-colors duration-[180ms] ease-[var(--visual-ease-out)]",
                              isActive
                                ? "font-bold text-foreground"
                                : "text-muted-foreground hover:bg-surface-sunken hover:text-foreground",
                            )
                          }
                        >
                          {({ isActive }) => (
                            <>
                              {/* 点标与字重共同标识当前子项，装饰不接收点击。 */}
                              <span
                                aria-hidden="true"
                                className={cn(
                                  "size-1.5 shrink-0 rounded-full",
                                  isActive ? "bg-active-border" : "bg-transparent",
                                )}
                              />
                              <span className="min-w-0 flex-1 truncate">{tab.label}</span>
                            </>
                          )}
                        </NavLink>
                      ))}
                      </MotionRegion>
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>
              )
            })}
          </nav>
          <div className="shrink-0 border-t border-line p-4">
            <div className="mb-3 break-words [&>span]:text-xs"><AppVersion /></div>
            <div className="mb-3 flex items-center gap-3">
              <div className="flex size-9 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-sm bg-surface-sunken text-xs font-bold">
                {user.display_name.slice(0, 1)}
              </div>
              <div className="min-w-0">
                <p className="truncate text-sm font-semibold">{user.display_name}</p>
                <p className="truncate text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">{user.role_label ?? user.username}</p>
                <Link data-testid="personal-settings-link" to="/account" className="mt-1 inline-block text-xs font-bold underline">个人设置</Link>
              </div>
            </div>
            <Button
              variant="ghost"
              size="sm"
              className="w-full justify-start"
              onClick={() => {
                if (correctionDraftsBusy()) { window.alert("纠偏字段仍在保存，请稍后退出。"); return }
                if (hasUnsavedCorrectionDrafts() && !window.confirm("退出登录会放弃未保存的纠偏草稿与备注，是否继续？")) return
                logout.mutate()
              }}
              disabled={logout.isPending}
            >
              <SignOut />退出登录
            </Button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </m.aside>

      <main inert={open && !desktop} className="workspace-main min-h-[100dvh] min-w-0 lg:pl-[252px]">
        {active && (
          // 二级页面已收纳进侧栏，这里不再重复平铺一排 tab——同一组链接出现两次，
          // 人得先判断该点哪一处。改成面包屑：只回答「我在哪」，不承担跳转选择。
          <div className="workspace-breadcrumb flex min-h-12 items-center gap-2">
            <nav aria-label="面包屑" className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-2">
              <span className="font-data shrink-0 text-xs text-muted-foreground">{active.index}</span>
              <NavLink
                to={active.to}
                className="shrink-0 text-xs font-semibold text-[var(--muted)] transition-colors hover:text-foreground"
              >
                {active.label}
              </NavLink>
              {currentTab && (
                <>
                  <span aria-hidden="true" className="shrink-0 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                    /
                  </span>
                  <span aria-current="page" className="min-w-0 truncate text-xs font-bold text-foreground">
                    {currentTab.label}
                  </span>
                </>
              )}
            </nav>
            <FavoriteButton path={location.pathname} />
          </div>
        )}
        <Outlet />
      </main>
    </div>
  )
}

export function PageHeader({
  title,
  description,
  actions,
  context,
}: {
  index: string
  title: string
  description: string
  actions?: ReactNode
  context?: ReactNode
}) {
  return <PageHeading title={title} description={description} actions={actions} context={context} />
}

===== FILE frontend/src/components/asset-thumbnail.tsx =====
import { useState } from "react"
import { ImageBroken } from "@phosphor-icons/react"

import { HoverPreview, ImageSurface } from "@/components/hover-preview"
import type { ImagePreview } from "@/components/image-lightbox"
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"

/** 列表里的素材缩略图。
 *
 *  运行和任务都是随机 key 命名，光看名字看不出在跑什么内容，所以列表要直接把图显示出来。
 *  两种情况会没有图：快照里本来就没有素材 id（历史运行），或者原图已经不在存储上了。
 *  这两种都不能让整行报错，只显示一个占位，别的列照常可读。 */
export function AssetThumbnail({
  assetId,
  alt,
  size = "size-12",
  onPreview,
}: {
  assetId: number | null | undefined
  alt: string
  size?: string
  onPreview?: (preview: ImagePreview) => void
}) {
  const [failure, setFailure] = useState({ assetId, broken: false })
  const broken = failure.assetId === assetId && failure.broken
  const setBroken = (broken: boolean) => setFailure({ assetId, broken })

  if (!assetId || broken) {
    return (
      <div
        data-testid="asset-thumbnail-missing"
        title={assetId ? "原图已不在存储上" : "这条运行没有记录素材"}
        className={cn(
          "flex shrink-0 flex-col items-center justify-center gap-0.5 rounded-md border border-dashed border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-surface-sunken text-xs text-muted-foreground",
          size,
        )}
      >
        <ImageBroken size={16} />
        <span>无原图</span>
      </div>
    )
  }

  const src = `/api/assets/${assetId}/file`
  // 外层固定成缩略图的尺寸：图片带 max-width:100%，在表格里不占最小宽度，列一紧就被压成窄长条
  // （10-03 纠偏分析页的“怪异椭圆”）。小缩略图圆角用 8px，14px 放在 48px 的框上太圆。
  const image = (
    <span className={cn("inline-flex shrink-0 align-middle", size)}>
      <HoverPreview src={src} alt={alt}>
        <ImageSurface
          src={src}
          alt=""
          loading="lazy"
          decoding="async"
          onError={() => setBroken(true)}
          className={size}
          frameClassName="rounded-md"
          matte="white"
        />
      </HoverPreview>
    </span>
  )

  if (!onPreview) return image

  return (
    <button
      type="button"
      aria-label={`查看原图：${alt}`}
      className="min-h-hit min-w-hit shrink-0 cursor-zoom-in rounded-md focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-primary focus-visible:ring-offset-2"
      onClick={() => onPreview({ src, alt })}
    >
      {image}
    </button>
  )
}

===== FILE frontend/src/components/content-identity-drawer.tsx =====
import { SecondaryDrawer } from "@/components/workspace-page"
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge"
import type { ContentIdentityRecord, SourceIdentityVerification } from "@/lib/types"
import type { ReactNode } from "react"

const statusLabels: Record<ContentIdentityRecord["identity_status"], string> = {
  legacy_unverified: "旧链路未签认",
  pending_verification: "身份待签认",
  verified: "已签认",
  conflict: "身份冲突",
}

export function ContentIdentityDrawer({
  record,
  verification,
  open,
  onOpenChange,
}: {
  record: ContentIdentityRecord | null
  verification: SourceIdentityVerification | null
  open: boolean
  onOpenChange: (open: boolean) => void
}) {
  const blocked = record?.identity_status === "pending_verification" || record?.identity_status === "conflict"
  const candidateKey = record?.source_res_type && record.source_ll_id
    ? `${record.source_system} + ${record.source_res_type} + ${record.source_ll_id}`
    : `${record?.source_system ?? "source_system"} + res_type + ll_id`

  return (
    <SecondaryDrawer
      open={open}
      onOpenChange={onOpenChange}
      title={record ? `内容身份 · ${record.content_id}` : "内容身份"}
      description="只展示进入生产主线所需的候选键、签认状态和证据引用，不展示完整上游载荷或探查明细。"
    >
      {!record ? <p className="text-sm text-[var(--muted)]">请选择一条内容记录。</p> : <div className="space-y-6">
        <section className="grid gap-4 border-y border-[var(--line)] py-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
          <Detail label="候选复合键"><span className="visual-code break-all">{candidateKey}</span></Detail>
          <Detail label="身份状态"><Badge tone={record.identity_status === "verified" ? "success" : blocked ? "warning" : "neutral"}>{statusLabels[record.identity_status]}</Badge></Detail>
          <Detail label="Canonical content_key"><span className="visual-code break-all">{record.content_key ?? "身份待签认"}</span></Detail>
          <Detail label="身份哈希"><span className="visual-code break-all">{record.identity_hash ?? "未生成"}</span></Detail>
        </section>

        <section>
          <h2 className="visual-card-title">来源字段</h2>
          <div className="mt-3 grid gap-3 border-y border-[var(--line)] px-3 py-4 text-xs sm:grid-cols-2">
            <Detail label="source_system"><span>{record.source_system}</span></Detail>
            <Detail label="res_type"><span>{record.source_res_type ?? "未提供"}</span></Detail>
            <Detail label="ll_id"><span>{record.source_ll_id ?? "未提供"}</span></Detail>
            <Detail label="res_id"><span>{record.source_res_id ?? "未提供"}</span></Detail>
          </div>
        </section>

        <section>
          <h2 className="visual-card-title">签认证据</h2>
          <div className="mt-3 grid gap-3 border-y border-[var(--line)] px-3 py-4 text-xs sm:grid-cols-2">
            <Detail label="verification id"><span className="visual-code">{record.identity_verification_id ?? "未绑定"}</span></Detail>
            <Detail label="签认证据哈希"><span className="visual-code break-all">{verification?.probe_hash ?? "未绑定"}</span></Detail>
            <Detail label="数据窗口"><span>{verification?.data_window ?? "未签认"}</span></Detail>
            <Detail label="审核人"><span>{verification?.approved_by ?? "未审核"}</span></Detail>
          </div>
        </section>

        <section className={`border rounded-md p-4 text-xs leading-6 ${blocked ? "border-warning bg-warning-wash text-warning" : "border-[var(--line)] bg-surface-sunken"}`}>
          {blocked
            ? "pending 或 conflict 内容记录不能启动正式标签生产；需完成只读探查、人工签认，并由新事件重新进入链路。"
            : "该身份视图只证明接入身份状态，不代表标签事实已经发布，也不会触发模型或下游投影。"}
        </section>
      </div>}
    </SecondaryDrawer>
  )
}

function Detail({ label, children }: { label: string; children: ReactNode }) {
  return <div className="space-y-1"><p className="text-[var(--muted)]">{label}</p><div>{children}</div></div>
}

===== FILE frontend/src/components/evaluation-boundary-note.tsx =====
import { useState } from "react"
import { CaretDown, Info } from "@phosphor-icons/react"

/**
 * ADR-0033 三位一体边界说明。
 *
 * 评测流程有三个可配置位置，职责严格分离，避免使用者把规则填错位置：
 * - 调用 A（预检/事实识别）：只看“这是什么、有没有硬伤/红线信号”，产出事实字段与红线信号，
 *   不出维度分、不出最终 L 等级。
 * - 调用 B（维度评价）：只看“做得好不好”，按赛道逐条判定扣分规则并给可见证据，
 *   不出总分、不做媒介降权与封顶。
 * - 维度层（服务端确定性）：把 A、B 的结果用纯函数算成分数与 L 等级（红线→赛道→维度扣分→
 *   媒介降权→高分压分→赛道封顶→分数映射），可回归、不调用模型。
 *
 * 传 `slot` 高亮当前配置位置对应的那一条。
 */
export type EvaluationBoundarySlot = "A" | "B" | "dimension"

const ROWS: { slot: EvaluationBoundarySlot; title: string; body: string }[] = [
  {
    slot: "A",
    title: "调用 A · 预检（事实识别）",
    body: "只识别“这是什么、是否命中红线信号、有没有可见硬伤”。产出分类、图片形态、画质、生产字段与红线信号，不打维度分、不给最终 L 等级。",
  },
  {
    slot: "B",
    title: "调用 B · 维度评价（好不好）",
    body: "只按赛道逐条判断维度扣分规则是否命中，并为每条命中规则给出独立证据；不算总分、不做媒介降权与封顶。旧评测机制没有扣分规则时才兼容 grade（1-5）路径。",
  },
  {
    slot: "dimension",
    title: "维度层 · 服务端确定性算分",
    body: "把 A、B 的结果按固定顺序算成分数与 L 等级：红线门 → 赛道分类 → 维度扣分 → 媒介降权 → 高分一票压分 → 赛道封顶 → 分数映射。纯函数、可回归、不调用模型；红线与降权规则只落在这里，不写进提示词。",
  },
]

export function EvaluationBoundaryNote({
  slot,
  defaultOpen = false,
}: {
  slot?: EvaluationBoundarySlot
  defaultOpen?: boolean
}) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(defaultOpen)
  return (
    <div className="rounded-sm border border-[var(--line)] bg-surface-sunken">
      <button
        type="button"
        className="visual-native-button flex w-full items-center justify-between gap-2 px-4 py-2.5 text-left"
        aria-expanded={open}
        onClick={() => setOpen((value) => !value)}
      >
        <span className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-semibold text-active-text">
          <Info size={16} weight="fill" />
          调用 A / 调用 B / 维度：各自填什么、边界在哪
        </span>
        <CaretDown
          size={14}
          className={`shrink-0 transition-transform duration-180 ease-[var(--visual-ease-out)] ${open ? "rotate-180" : ""}`}
        />
      </button>
      {open && (
        <div className="space-y-2 border-t border-[var(--line)] px-4 py-3">
          {ROWS.map((row) => {
            const active = slot === row.slot
            return (
              <div
                key={row.slot}
                className={`rounded-sm px-3 py-2 text-xs leading-5 ${
 active
 ? "border border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-white"
 : "bg-white/60"
 }`}
              >
                <p className="font-semibold">
                  {row.title}
                  {active && (
                    <span className="ml-2 rounded-md bg-active-text px-1.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-semibold text-white">
                      当前配置位置
                    </span>
                  )}
                </p>
                <p className="mt-1 text-[var(--muted)]">{row.body}</p>
              </div>
            )
          })}
          <p className="px-1 pt-1 text-xs leading-4 text-[var(--muted)]">
            提示：红线判定与媒介降权是确定性规则，必须由维度层执行，不能塞进调用 B 的提示词靠模型自觉，否则不可复现。
          </p>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

===== FILE frontend/src/components/hover-preview.tsx =====
import { useEffect, useLayoutEffect, useRef, useState, type CSSProperties, type ReactNode, type SyntheticEvent } from "react"
import { createPortal } from "react-dom"
import { useMotionFeaturesReady } from "@/components/motion/motion-root"
import { useReducedMotionPreference } from "@/lib/motion/reduced-motion"
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"

const checkerboard: CSSProperties = {
  backgroundColor: "#eef0eb",
  backgroundImage: "linear-gradient(45deg,#cfd4ca 25%,transparent 25%),linear-gradient(-45deg,#cfd4ca 25%,transparent 25%),linear-gradient(45deg,transparent 75%,#cfd4ca 75%),linear-gradient(-45deg,transparent 75%,#cfd4ca 75%)",
  backgroundPosition: "0 0,0 8px,8px -8px,-8px 0",
  backgroundSize: "16px 16px",
}
// 列表里的小缩略图用白底（老大 10-02 批准过“白底、完整显示不裁切”）：棋盘格铺满 48px 的小框，
// 四周留白像一块花纹，10-03 体验时被指出难看。看细节的地方（灯箱、悬停放大）仍用棋盘格认透明图。
const whiteMatte: CSSProperties = { backgroundColor: "var(--canvas)" }

type ImageSurfaceProps = {
  src: string
  alt: string
  className?: string
  style?: CSSProperties
  testId?: string
  /** 外框的附加样式，例如小缩略图用更小的圆角。 */
  frameClassName?: string
  /** 图片四周的底：默认棋盘格（能认出透明图）；列表小缩略图用白底。 */
  matte?: "checker" | "white"
  loading?: "lazy" | "eager"
  decoding?: "async" | "sync" | "auto"
  onLoad?: (event: SyntheticEvent<HTMLImageElement>) => void
  onError?: () => void
}

/** W05 内部共用画面：不换 URL、不预加载。换图时换 img，不能把上一张留作占位。 */
export function ImageSurface(props: ImageSurfaceProps) {
  return <ImageSurfaceContent key={props.src} {...props} />
}

function ImageSurfaceContent({ src, alt, className, frameClassName, matte = "checker", style, testId, loading, decoding, onLoad, onError }: ImageSurfaceProps) {
  const image = useRef<HTMLImageElement>(null)
  const [broken, setBroken] = useState(false)
  const [outline, setOutline] = useState<CSSProperties | null>(null)

  // object-contain 的留白不是图片。内描边按实际画面矩形放置，不压在带色边框上。
  const measure = () => {
    const node = image.current
    if (!node?.naturalWidth || !node.naturalHeight) return
    const computed = getComputedStyle(node)
    const left = parseFloat(computed.paddingLeft), top = parseFloat(computed.paddingTop)
    const width = node.getBoundingClientRect().width - left - parseFloat(computed.paddingRight)
    const height = node.getBoundingClientRect().height - top - parseFloat(computed.paddingBottom)
    const scale = Math.min(width / node.naturalWidth, height / node.naturalHeight)
    const paintedWidth = node.naturalWidth * scale, paintedHeight = node.naturalHeight * scale
    const next = { left: left + (width - paintedWidth) / 2, top: top + (height - paintedHeight) / 2, width: paintedWidth, height: paintedHeight }
    setOutline(previous => previous && Object.entries(next).every(([key, value]) => previous[key as keyof CSSProperties] === value) ? previous : next)
  }
  useLayoutEffect(() => {
    const node = image.current
    if (!node) return
    const observer = new ResizeObserver(measure)
    observer.observe(node)
    measure()
    return () => observer.disconnect()
  }, [])

  return (
    <span data-image-surface="" className={cn("relative inline-flex max-w-full rounded-md align-middle", frameClassName)} style={matte === "white" ? whiteMatte : checkerboard}>
      <img
        ref={image}
        data-testid={testId}
        src={src}
        alt={alt}
        loading={loading}
        decoding={decoding}
        onLoad={event => { setBroken(false); measure(); onLoad?.(event) }}
        onError={() => { setBroken(true); onError?.() }}
        className={cn(className, "block max-w-full rounded-none border-0 bg-transparent p-1 object-contain", broken && "invisible")}
        style={{ ...style, objectFit: "contain", borderRadius: 0, backgroundColor: "transparent" }}
      />
      {!broken && outline && <span data-image-outline="" aria-hidden="true" className="pointer-events-none absolute" style={{ ...outline, boxShadow: "var(--image-outline)" }} />}
      {broken && <span role="status" className="absolute inset-0 flex items-center justify-center rounded-md bg-surface-sunken p-1 text-center text-xs text-muted-foreground">图片加载失败</span>}
    </span>
  )
}

/** 鼠标悬停在素材缩略图上时浮出的放大预览。
 *
 *  列表里的缩略图只有 56～72px，看不清内容；点开原图又要打断当前操作。
 *  这个浮窗跟着指针走，离开缩略图即消失，不改变点击行为（点击仍走各页原有的查看原图 / 灯箱）。
 *  只在有真实指针的设备上启用：触屏没有 hover，长按会误触。
 *  预览用的是同一张图（缩略图本来就是原图缩放显示），不多发请求。 */

const SHOW_DELAY_MS = 220
const MAX_SIZE = 480
const GAP = 18

type Point = { x: number; y: number }

function hoverCapable() {
  if (typeof window === "undefined") return false
  // 浏览器契约测试跑在无头 Chrome 里，媒体查询不可靠，允许页面显式声明有指针
  if (document.documentElement.dataset.forceHover === "1") return true
  return window.matchMedia?.("(hover: hover) and (pointer: fine)").matches ?? false
}

function placement(point: Point, width: number, height: number) {
  const vw = window.innerWidth
  const vh = window.innerHeight
  let left = point.x + GAP
  if (left + width > vw - 8) left = point.x - GAP - width
  if (left < 8) left = Math.max(8, Math.min(vw - width - 8, point.x + GAP))
  let top = point.y - height / 2
  top = Math.max(8, Math.min(vh - height - 8, top))
  return { left, top }
}

export function HoverPreview({
  src,
  alt,
  children,
  className,
  disabled,
}: {
  src: string | null | undefined
  alt?: string
  children: ReactNode
  className?: string
  disabled?: boolean
}) {
  const ready = useMotionFeaturesReady()
  const reduced = useReducedMotionPreference()
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false)
  const [point, setPoint] = useState<Point | null>(null)
  const [size, setSize] = useState<{ src: string; width: number; height: number } | null>(null)
  const timer = useRef<number | null>(null)

  const clear = () => {
    if (timer.current !== null) {
      window.clearTimeout(timer.current)
      timer.current = null
    }
  }
  const hide = () => {
    clear()
    setOpen(false)
  }

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return
    // 滚动或切页时浮窗不能留在原地
    window.addEventListener("scroll", hide, true)
    window.addEventListener("blur", hide)
    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener("scroll", hide, true)
      window.removeEventListener("blur", hide)
    }
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [open])

  useEffect(() => clear, [])

  const active = Boolean(src) && !disabled

  const onEnter = (event: React.MouseEvent) => {
    if (!active || !hoverCapable()) return
    setPoint({ x: event.clientX, y: event.clientY })
    clear()
    timer.current = window.setTimeout(() => setOpen(true), SHOW_DELAY_MS)
  }
  const onMove = (event: React.MouseEvent) => {
    if (!active) return
    setPoint({ x: event.clientX, y: event.clientY })
  }

  const limit = typeof window === "undefined" ? MAX_SIZE : Math.min(MAX_SIZE, window.innerWidth - 38, window.innerHeight - 38)
  const box = size && size.src === src
    ? (() => {
        const scale = Math.min(1, limit / Math.max(size.width, size.height))
        return { width: Math.max(96, Math.round(size.width * scale)), height: Math.max(96, Math.round(size.height * scale)) }
      })()
    : { width: limit, height: limit }

  return (
    <span
      className={className ?? "contents"}
      onMouseEnter={onEnter}
      onMouseMove={onMove}
      onMouseLeave={hide}
      onMouseDown={hide}
    >
      {children}
      {open && point && src
        ? createPortal(
            <div
              role="presentation"
              data-testid="hover-preview"
              data-visual-motion=""
              className={cn("pointer-events-none fixed z-[80] rounded-lg border border-[var(--line)] bg-white p-2.5 opacity-100 shadow-float",
                ready && !reduced && "starting:opacity-0 transition-opacity duration-[var(--visual-ease-out-ms)] ease-[var(--visual-ease-out)]")}
              style={{ ...placement(point, box.width + 22, box.height + 22), width: box.width + 22 }}
            >
              <ImageSurface
                src={src}
                alt={alt ?? ""}
                decoding="async"
                onLoad={(event) => {
                  const image = event.currentTarget
                  if (image.naturalWidth && image.naturalHeight) {
                    setSize({ src, width: image.naturalWidth, height: image.naturalHeight })
                  }
                }}
                style={{ width: box.width, height: box.height }}
              />
            </div>,
            document.body,
          )
        : null}
    </span>
  )
}

===== FILE frontend/src/components/image-lightbox.tsx =====
import * as Dialog from "@radix-ui/react-dialog"
import { X } from "@phosphor-icons/react"
import { useLayoutEffect, useRef, useState } from "react"
import { flushSync } from "react-dom"
import { animate, useMotionValue } from "motion/react"
import * as m from "motion/react-m"

import { ImageSurface } from "@/components/hover-preview"

import { useReducedMotionPreference } from "@/lib/motion/reduced-motion"
import { enterDistance, motionTokens } from "@/lib/motion/tokens"

const exitTransition = { ...motionTokens.easeOut, duration: 120 / 1000 }

export type ImagePreview = {

  alt: string

export function ImagePreviewButton({

  alt,
  imageClassName,

export function ImageReferenceDock({
  preview,
  onOpenChange,
}: {
  preview: ImagePreview | null
  onOpenChange: (open: boolean) => void
}) {
  if (!preview) return null

  return (
    <aside
      aria-label="原图参考浮窗"
      className="sticky top-4 z-20 mt-3 overflow-hidden rounded-lg border border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-white shadow-float"
    >
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 border-b border-[var(--line)] bg-alias-surface-051 px-4 py-3">

          <p className="text-xs font-bold">原图参考浮窗</p>
          <p className="mt-1 truncate text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">{preview.alt}</p>
        </div>
        <button
          type="button"
          aria-label="关闭原图参考浮窗"
          className="flex size-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-md border border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-white text-foreground hover:bg-alias-surface-009 focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-primary"
          onClick={() => onOpenChange(false)}
        >
          <X size={18} />
        </button>
      </div>
      <div
        data-testid="image-reference-dock-canvas"
        className="flex max-h-[calc(100dvh-12rem)] min-h-64 items-center justify-center overflow-auto p-3"
        style={{
          backgroundColor: "#eef0eb",
          backgroundImage:
            "linear-gradient(45deg,#cfd4ca 25%,transparent 25%),linear-gradient(-45deg,#cfd4ca 25%,transparent 25%),linear-gradient(45deg,transparent 75%,#cfd4ca 75%),linear-gradient(-45deg,transparent 75%,#cfd4ca 75%)",
          backgroundPosition: "0 0,0 8px,8px -8px,-8px 0",
          backgroundSize: "16px 16px",
        }}
      >
        <ImageSurface
          testId="image-reference-dock-image"
          src={preview.src}
          alt={preview.alt}
          className="h-auto max-h-[calc(100dvh-14rem)] w-auto max-w-full"
        />
      </div>
    </aside>
  )
}

export function ImageLightbox({
  preview,
  onOpenChange,
}: {
  preview: ImagePreview | null
  onOpenChange: (open: boolean) => void
}) {
  // 父组件的 preview 仍是唯一业务开关；这里只留退出动画所需的最后一张画面。
  const [presented, setPresented] = useState(preview)
  const [closingAt, setClosingAt] = useState<number | null>(null)
  if (preview && presented !== preview) setPresented(preview)
  if (preview && closingAt !== null) setClosingAt(null)
  if (!preview && presented && closingAt === null) setClosingAt(performance.now())
  const closing = preview === null

  return (
    <Dialog.Root open={presented !== null} onOpenChange={open => {
      if (!closing) {
        if (!open) setClosingAt(performance.now())
        onOpenChange(open)
      }
    }}>
      {presented && <LightboxContent preview={preview ?? presented} closing={closing} closingAt={closingAt} onExited={() => {
        // 快速重开后到达的旧动画回调不能关闭新图。
        if (preview === null) setPresented(null)
      }} />}
    </Dialog.Root>
  )
}

function LightboxContent({ preview, closing, closingAt, onExited }: {
  preview: ImagePreview
  closing: boolean
  closingAt: number | null
  onExited: () => void
}) {
  const ready = useMotionFeaturesReady()
  const reduced = useReducedMotionPreference()
  const canAnimate = ready && !reduced
  const enter = useRef(canAnimate)
  const returnFocus = useRef<HTMLElement | null>(null)
  const isClosing = useRef(closing)
  const exitCallback = useRef(onExited)
  const opacity = useMotionValue(enter.current ? 0 : 1)
  const y = useMotionValue(enter.current ? enterDistance : 0)

  useLayoutEffect(() => { exitCallback.current = onExited }, [onExited])
  useLayoutEffect(() => {
    isClosing.current = closing
    if (!canAnimate) {
      opacity.stop()
      y.stop()
      opacity.set(1)
      y.set(0)
      if (closing) exitCallback.current()
      return
    }
    // 数值动画直接从当前值开始，避免 DOM 关键帧解析再排队几帧。
    // 进入沿用冻结的 180ms，退出沿用同一 ease-out 曲线并缩短为 120ms。
    // 完成时立即释放模态，不追加卸载等待。
    let finished = false
    const finish = () => {
      if (!closing || !isClosing.current || finished) return
      finished = true
      opacity.stop(); y.stop()
      opacity.set(0); y.set(enterDistance)
      flushSync(() => exitCallback.current())
    }
    const transition = closing ? exitTransition : motionTokens.easeOut
    const move = animate(y, closing ? enterDistance : 0, transition)
    const fade = animate(opacity, closing ? 0 : 1, { ...transition, onComplete: finish })
    // 忙时/后台帧可能迟到；同一令牌的时钟到点即到最终状态，不额外等待动画回调。
    // 从请求关闭计时，React 提交/inert 布局已经花掉的时间也在这 120ms 内。
    const remaining = Math.max(0, transition.duration * 1000 - (performance.now() - (closingAt ?? performance.now())))
    const deadline = closing ? window.setTimeout(finish, remaining) : undefined
    return () => { window.clearTimeout(deadline); move.stop(); fade.stop() }
  }, [canAnimate, closing, closingAt, opacity, y])

  return (
      <Dialog.Portal>
        <Dialog.Overlay className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-black/70" />
        <Dialog.Content
          asChild
          aria-describedby={undefined}
          onOpenAutoFocus={() => { returnFocus.current = document.activeElement instanceof HTMLElement ? document.activeElement : null }}
          onCloseAutoFocus={event => {
            event.preventDefault()
            if (returnFocus.current?.isConnected) returnFocus.current.focus({ preventScroll: true })
          }}
        >
          <m.div
            data-visual-motion=""
            data-image-phase={closing ? "closing" : "open"}
            data-image-enter-ms={motionTokens.easeOut.duration * 1000}
            data-image-exit-ms={exitTransition.duration * 1000}
            data-image-transition-ms={(closing ? exitTransition : motionTokens.easeOut).duration * 1000}
            inert={closing}
            className="fixed left-1/2 top-1/2 z-50 flex max-h-[calc(100dvh-2rem)] w-[min(96vw,96rem)] -translate-x-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 flex-col items-center justify-center gap-3 overflow-hidden rounded-lg border border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-white p-4 shadow-float focus:outline-none"
            style={{ opacity, y }}
            initial={false}
            onClickCapture={event => { if (closing) { event.preventDefault(); event.stopPropagation() } }}
            onKeyDownCapture={event => { if (closing) { event.preventDefault(); event.stopPropagation() } }}
          >
          <Dialog.Title className="sr-only">
            原图预览：{preview.alt}
          </Dialog.Title>
          <Dialog.Close
            aria-label="关闭原图预览"
            disabled={closing}
            className="flex size-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center self-end rounded-md border border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-white text-foreground hover:bg-alias-surface-009 focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-primary"
          >
            <X size={20} />
          </Dialog.Close>
            <div
              data-testid="image-lightbox-inspection-canvas"
              className="flex max-h-[calc(100dvh-8rem)] max-w-full items-center justify-center overflow-auto rounded-md p-3"
              style={{
                backgroundColor: "#eef0eb",
                backgroundImage:
                  "linear-gradient(45deg,#cfd4ca 25%,transparent 25%),linear-gradient(-45deg,#cfd4ca 25%,transparent 25%),linear-gradient(45deg,transparent 75%,#cfd4ca 75%),linear-gradient(-45deg,transparent 75%,#cfd4ca 75%)",
                backgroundPosition: "0 0,0 8px,8px -8px,-8px 0",
                backgroundSize: "16px 16px",
              }}
            >
              <ImageSurface
                testId="image-lightbox-image"
                src={preview.src}
                alt={preview.alt}
                className="h-auto max-h-[calc(100dvh-10rem)] w-auto max-w-full"
              />
            </div>
          </m.div>
        </Dialog.Content>
      </Dialog.Portal>
  )
}

===== FILE frontend/src/components/motion/motion-region.tsx =====
import { useLayoutEffect, useRef, type CSSProperties, type ReactNode } from "react"
import { useMotionValue } from "motion/react"
import * as m from "motion/react-m"
import { useReducedMotionPreference } from "../../lib/motion/reduced-motion"
import { enterDistance, maxLayoutItems, motionTokens } from "../../lib/motion/tokens"
import { useMotionFeaturesReady } from "./motion-root"

const finalState = { opacity: 1, y: 0 }

/** 低频区域进入/布局包装。不是 Dialog，不接管焦点、inert 或业务开关。
 * 子节点永不因功能加载或系统设置改变而更换宿主，表单状态得以保留。
 * 首屏功能未就绪时直接显示；加载成功后不会把已显示内容藏回去再播放。
 */
export function MotionRegion({ children, id, className, style, layoutId, visibleItems = 0 }: {
  children: ReactNode
  id?: string
  className?: string
  style?: CSSProperties
  layoutId?: string
  /** 使用 layoutId 时须传入当前可见列表项数；0 表示单个区域。 */
  visibleItems?: number
}) {
  const ready = useMotionFeaturesReady()
  const reduced = useReducedMotionPreference()
  const enter = useRef(ready && !reduced)
  const opacity = useMotionValue(enter.current ? 0 : 1)
  const y = useMotionValue(enter.current ? enterDistance : 0)
  const allowLayout = ready && !reduced && Number.isFinite(visibleItems) && visibleItems >= 0 && visibleItems <= maxLayoutItems

  useLayoutEffect(() => {
    if (!ready || reduced) {
      enter.current = false
      opacity.stop()
      y.stop()
      opacity.set(1)
      y.set(0)
    }
    return () => { opacity.stop(); y.stop() }
  }, [opacity, y, ready, reduced])

  return (
    <m.div id={id} className={className} data-visual-motion="" style={{ ...style, opacity, y }}
      initial={ready && !reduced && enter.current ? { opacity: 0, y: enterDistance } : false}
      animate={finalState} transition={ready && !reduced ? motionTokens.soft : { duration: 0 }}
      layout={allowLayout && Boolean(layoutId)} layoutId={allowLayout ? layoutId : undefined}>
      {children}
    </m.div>
  )
}

===== FILE frontend/src/components/motion/motion-root.tsx =====
import { createContext, useContext, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState, type ReactNode } from "react"
import { LazyMotion, MotionConfig, type FeatureBundle } from "motion/react"

const Ready = createContext(false)
export const useMotionFeaturesReady = () => useContext(Ready)

// Vite 把动态 import 的网络错误先广播到 window。可选的 domMax 不能走
// 全局业务路由的整页重载恢复；只截住本源 features 块，其它错误照旧传播。
function keepOptionalMotionStatic(event: Event) {
  const error = (event as Event & { payload?: unknown }).payload
  if (!(error instanceof Error)) return
  const url = error.message.match(/https?:\/\/\S+\/features-[\w-]+\.js\b/)?.[0]
  if (url && new URL(url).origin === location.origin) event.stopImmediatePropagation()
  // 不 preventDefault：Vite 仍抛出原错，由下面的 import 拒绝分支收口。
}

/** 必须先于全局路由 preload 恢复安装；window 目标上的监听器按注册顺序调用。 */
export function installMotionPreloadFallback() {
  window.addEventListener("vite:preloadError", keepOptionalMotionStatic)
}

/** 未加载/失败时所有包装都保持最终状态；失败不产生未处理的 Promise 拒绝。 */
export function MotionRoot({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const [ready, setReady] = useState(false)
  const alive = useRef(false)
  useEffect(() => {
    alive.current = true
    return () => { alive.current = false }
  }, [])
  const load = useMemo(() => () => import("../../lib/motion/features").then(
    ({ default: features }) => {
      if (alive.current) setReady(true)
      return features
    },
    // 库的类型要求 renderer，运行时却以 undefined 表示「尚无 renderer」。
    // 空功能集保留这一静态 DOM 状态；浏览器故障用例覆盖这个窄的类型断言。
    () => ({} as FeatureBundle),
  ), [])

  return (
    <MotionConfig reducedMotion="user">
      <LazyMotion features={load} strict>
        <Ready.Provider value={ready}>{children}</Ready.Provider>
      </LazyMotion>
    </MotionConfig>
  )
}

import { NavLink } from "react-router-dom"

/** 同一任务下并列的两三个视图，用页内 tab 切换，不再各占一个侧栏入口。 */
export function PageTabs({ tabs, ariaLabel }: { tabs: readonly { to: string; label: string }[]; ariaLabel: string }) {
  return (
    <nav aria-label={ariaLabel} role="tablist" className="mx-auto shell-content min-w-0 px-page flex gap-1 border-b border-[var(--line)]">

          key={tab.to}
          to={tab.to}
          role="tab"
          end
          className={({ isActive }) => `-mb-px border-b-2 px-3 py-2 text-sm ${isActive ? "border-active-border font-semibold text-[var(--ink)]" : "border-transparent text-[var(--muted)] hover:text-[var(--ink)]"}`}
        >
          {tab.label}
        </NavLink>
      ))}
    </nav>
  )
}
