export interface SecondaryDrawerProps {
  open: boolean
  onOpenChange: (open: boolean) => void
  title: string
  description?: string
  children: ReactNode
  footer?: ReactNode
  size?: "default" | "wide"
  className?: string
}

// Contract §5.1 permits 120–220ms ease-out exits. Use the lower end so
// unmount/layout work also has room inside the 220ms interaction deadline.
const exitMilliseconds = 120
const exitTransition = { ...motionTokens.easeOut, duration: exitMilliseconds / 1000 }

/** Escape ownership: inner controls first, the focused workspace layer last.
 * - An open native picker (select, date) gets the key alone: the browser's
 *   default action closes it, and no page listener (Radix included) runs.
 * - Inner Radix layers such as menus are the highest Radix layer and dismiss
 *   themselves with preventDefault. Inner inputs that own Escape stop
 *   propagation. IME composition keeps its Escape.
 * - Only an unclaimed Escape closes the workspace layer that holds focus, or
 *   the most recently opened one when focus fell back to body.
 * Layers register in the commit that opens them, so a dialog that already has
 * focus owns Escape before Radix's passive layer stack catches up.
 */
type WorkspaceLayer = { panel: () => HTMLElement | null; dismiss: () => void }
const workspaceLayers: WorkspaceLayer[] = []
// Escapes whose preventDefault came only from a workspace dialog blocking
// Radix's own dismissal; any other prior preventDefault means it was claimed.
const dialogBlockedEscapes = new WeakSet<Event>()

function nativePickerOpen(target: EventTarget | null) {
  if (!(target instanceof HTMLSelectElement || target instanceof HTMLInputElement)) return false
  try { return target.matches(":open") } catch { return false } // No :open support: treat as closed.
}
function yieldEscapeToNativePicker(event: KeyboardEvent) {
  // Window capture runs before every document listener. Never preventDefault:
  // the picker closes through its default action.
  if (event.key === "Escape" && nativePickerOpen(event.target)) event.stopImmediatePropagation()
}
function dismissWorkspaceLayer(event: KeyboardEvent) {
  if (event.key !== "Escape" || event.isComposing || event.keyCode === 229) return
  if (event.defaultPrevented && !dialogBlockedEscapes.has(event)) return
  const target = event.target instanceof Element ? event.target : null
  const dialog = target?.closest('[role="dialog"]')
  let owner: WorkspaceLayer | undefined
  // Focus in another dialog (lightbox, navigation, popover) or in a portaled
  // menu is not ours. Body is the refused-dismissal fallback.
  if (dialog) owner = workspaceLayers.find(layer => layer.panel() === dialog)
  else if (!target || target === document.body || target === document.documentElement) {
    owner = [...workspaceLayers].reverse().find(layer => layer.panel()?.isConnected)
  }
  if (!owner) return
  event.preventDefault()
  owner.dismiss()
}
function registerWorkspaceLayer(layer: WorkspaceLayer) {
  if (!workspaceLayers.length) {
    window.addEventListener("keydown", yieldEscapeToNativePicker, true)
    window.addEventListener("keydown", dismissWorkspaceLayer)
  }
  workspaceLayers.push(layer)
  return () => {
    const index = workspaceLayers.indexOf(layer)
    if (index >= 0) workspaceLayers.splice(index, 1)
    if (!workspaceLayers.length) {
      window.removeEventListener("keydown", yieldEscapeToNativePicker, true)
      window.removeEventListener("keydown", dismissWorkspaceLayer)
    }
  }
}

/** Radix keeps the modal boundary until the exiting content actually unmounts.
 * The parent remains the only owner of open; no animation callback changes it.
 */
function DialogLayer({ open, onOpenChange, kind, className, children }: {
  open: boolean
  onOpenChange: (open: boolean) => void
  kind: "drawer" | "confirm"
  className: string
  children: ReactNode
}) {
  const ready = useMotionFeaturesReady()
  const reduced = useReducedMotionPreference()
  const animated = ready && !reduced
  const [retained, setRetained] = useState(open)
  const present = open || retained
  const panelRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null)
  const capturedPanelRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null)
  const returnFocusRef = useRef<HTMLElement | null>(null)
  const wasOpenRef = useRef(false)
  const closeDeadlineRef = useRef(0)
  const requestChange = useCallback((next: boolean) => { if (open) onOpenChange(next) }, [open, onOpenChange])
  const rememberEntry = () => {
    const active = typeof document !== "undefined" && document.activeElement instanceof HTMLElement ? document.activeElement : null
    // Do not replace a live entry with body in the removal/autofocus gap, or
    // with a child that already took autofocus during a StrictMode replay.
    if ((active && active !== document.body && !panelRef.current?.contains(active)) || !returnFocusRef.current?.isConnected) {
      returnFocusRef.current = active
    }
  }
  // Preserve the old component's capture-before-mount behavior: children such
  // as category-asset-manager use React autoFocus, ahead of Radix's effect.
  if (open && !wasOpenRef.current && !panelRef.current?.isConnected) rememberEntry()
  if (!open && wasOpenRef.current) closeDeadlineRef.current = performance.now() + exitMilliseconds
  wasOpenRef.current = open

  useLayoutEffect(() => {
    if (open) { setRetained(true); return }
    if (!retained) return
    if (!animated) { setRetained(false); return }
    // Commit/layout work consumes this same exit budget; never start another
    // full delay after an expensive closing commit.
    const remaining = Math.max(0, closeDeadlineRef.current - performance.now())
    const timer = window.setTimeout(() => flushSync(() => setRetained(false)), remaining)
    // Reopening or changing the motion preference invalidates this close.
    return () => window.clearTimeout(timer)
  }, [open, animated, retained])
  useLayoutEffect(() => {
    if (open || !present) return
    // Inert can move focus to body. Keep Tab/Enter from reaching background
    // controls while the modal's final frame is still on screen.
    const block = (event: KeyboardEvent) => {
      const targetDialog = event.target instanceof Element ? event.target.closest<HTMLElement>('[role="dialog"]') : null
      // A different modal can be opened by the parent while this one exits.
      if (targetDialog && targetDialog !== panelRef.current && !targetDialog.inert) return
      event.preventDefault(); event.stopImmediatePropagation()
    }
    document.addEventListener("keydown", block, true)
    return () => document.removeEventListener("keydown", block, true)
  }, [open, present])
  const dismissRef = useRef(requestChange)
  useLayoutEffect(() => { dismissRef.current = requestChange })
  useLayoutEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return
    // Same commit as the focus move; an exiting layer no longer takes Escape.
    return registerWorkspaceLayer({ panel: () => panelRef.current, dismiss: () => dismissRef.current(false) })
  }, [open])

  return (
    <Dialog.Root open={present} onOpenChange={requestChange}>
      {present && <Dialog.Portal>
        <Dialog.Overlay asChild>
          <DialogShade open={open} animated={animated} />
        </Dialog.Overlay>
        <Dialog.Content asChild ref={panelRef}
          onEscapeKeyDown={(event) => {
            // Radix's stack can still be the previous one in a new layer's
            // first frame. Block its dismissal; dismissWorkspaceLayer decides.
            if (!event.defaultPrevented) dialogBlockedEscapes.add(event)
            event.preventDefault()
          }}
          onOpenAutoFocus={() => {
            // StrictMode replays effects on the same host. Do not replace the
            // entry focus with a control inside the already-open dialog.
            if (capturedPanelRef.current !== panelRef.current) {
              capturedPanelRef.current = panelRef.current
              rememberEntry()
            }
          }}
          onCloseAutoFocus={(event) => {
            event.preventDefault()
            // Radix dispatches this after unmount on a timer. Ignore both its
            // StrictMode cleanup and a stale cleanup after an immediate reopen.
            if (!panelRef.current?.isConnected) returnFocusRef.current?.focus()
          }}>
          <DialogPanel open={open} animated={animated} kind={kind} className={className}>{children}</DialogPanel>
        </Dialog.Content>
      </Dialog.Portal>}
    </Dialog.Root>
  )
}

const DialogShade = forwardRef<HTMLDivElement, { open: boolean; animated: boolean } & ComponentProps<typeof m.div>>(
  function DialogShade({ open, animated, ...props }, ref) {
    const enter = useRef(animated)
    const opacity = useMotionValue(enter.current ? 0 : 1)
    useLayoutEffect(() => {
      if (!animated) { opacity.stop(); opacity.set(open ? 1 : 0) }
      return () => opacity.stop()
    }, [animated, open, opacity])
    return <m.div {...props} ref={ref} className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-foreground/30" style={{ ...props.style, opacity }}
      initial={enter.current ? { opacity: 0 } : false} animate={{ opacity: open ? 1 : 0 }}
      transition={!animated ? { duration: 0 } : open ? motionTokens.easeOut : exitTransition} />
  },
)

// Radix's asChild must reach the same DOM host for focus, dismiss and refs.
// Keep a mounted host when reduced motion changes; never replace a live form.
const DialogPanel = forwardRef<HTMLDivElement, {
  open: boolean
  animated: boolean
  kind: "drawer" | "confirm"
} & ComponentProps<typeof m.div>>(function DialogPanel({ open, animated, kind, children, ...props }, ref) {
  const enter = useRef(animated)
  const opacity = useMotionValue(enter.current ? 0 : 1)
  const offset = useMotionValue(enter.current ? enterDistance : 0)
  useLayoutEffect(() => {
    if (!animated) {
      opacity.stop(); offset.stop()
      opacity.set(open ? 1 : 0); offset.set(0)
    }
    return () => { opacity.stop(); offset.stop() }
  }, [animated, open, opacity, offset])
  const blockClosing = (event: SyntheticEvent) => {
    if (!open) { event.preventDefault(); event.stopPropagation() }
  }
  return <m.div {...props} ref={ref} data-workspace-layer={kind} data-visual-state={open ? "open" : "closing"} inert={!open}
    onClickCapture={(event) => { blockClosing(event); if (open) props.onClickCapture?.(event) }}
    onPointerDownCapture={(event) => { blockClosing(event); if (open) props.onPointerDownCapture?.(event) }}
    onKeyDownCapture={(event) => { blockClosing(event); if (open) props.onKeyDownCapture?.(event) }}
    style={{ ...props.style, opacity, ...(kind === "drawer" ? { x: offset } : { y: offset }) }}
    initial={enter.current ? { opacity: 0, [kind === "drawer" ? "x" : "y"]: enterDistance } : false}
    animate={{ opacity: open ? 1 : 0, [kind === "drawer" ? "x" : "y"]: open || !animated ? 0 : enterDistance }}
    transition={!animated ? { duration: 0 } : open ? motionTokens[kind === "drawer" ? "soft" : "spring"] : exitTransition}>
    {children}
  </m.div>
})

export function SecondaryDrawer({ open, onOpenChange, title, description, children, footer, size = "default", className }: SecondaryDrawerProps) {
  return (
        <DialogLayer open={open} onOpenChange={onOpenChange} kind="drawer"
          className={cn(
            "fixed inset-y-2 right-2 z-50 flex flex-col overflow-hidden rounded-lg bg-canvas shadow-float focus:outline-none",
            size === "wide" ? "w-[min(820px,calc(100vw-1rem))]" : "w-[min(680px,calc(100vw-1rem))]",
            className,
          )}
        >
          <div className="flex shrink-0 items-start justify-between gap-4 px-6 pb-2 pt-5 sm:pl-7 sm:pr-4">
            <div className="min-w-0">
              <Dialog.Title className="font-editorial break-words text-[26px] font-bold leading-[1.2]">{title}</Dialog.Title>
              {description && <Dialog.Description className="mt-2 text-sm leading-6 text-[var(--muted)]">{description}</Dialog.Description>}
            </div>
            <Dialog.Close asChild>
              <Button variant="secondary" size="icon" aria-label="关闭抽屉"><X size={20} /></Button>
            </Dialog.Close>
          </div>
          <div className="min-h-0 flex-1 overflow-y-auto px-6 pb-6 pt-3 sm:px-7">{children}</div>
          {footer && <div className="shrink-0 border-t border-line px-6 pb-6 pt-4 sm:px-7">{footer}</div>}
        </DialogLayer>
  )
}

export function ConfirmDialog({
  open,
  onOpenChange,
  title,
  description,
  confirmLabel = "确认",
  cancelLabel = "取消",
  onConfirm,
  children,
}: {
  open: boolean
  onOpenChange: (open: boolean) => void
  title: string
  description?: string
  confirmLabel?: string
  cancelLabel?: string
  onConfirm: () => void
  children?: ReactNode
}) {
  const confirmed = useRef(false)
  const [pending, setPending] = useState(false)
  useLayoutEffect(() => {
    if (open) { confirmed.current = false; setPending(false) }
  }, [open])
  const confirmOnce = () => {
    if (!open || confirmed.current) return
    confirmed.current = true
    setPending(true)
    onConfirm()
  }

  return (
        <DialogLayer open={open} onOpenChange={onOpenChange} kind="confirm"
          className="fixed left-1/2 top-1/2 z-50 flex max-h-[calc(100dvh-2rem)] w-[min(460px,calc(100vw-2rem))] -translate-x-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 flex-col overflow-y-auto rounded-lg bg-canvas p-6 shadow-float focus:outline-none sm:p-7"
        >
          <Dialog.Title className="visual-card-title break-words pr-12">{title}</Dialog.Title>
          {description && <Dialog.Description className="mt-2 text-sm leading-6 text-[var(--muted)]">{description}</Dialog.Description>}
          {children && <div className="mt-4">{children}</div>}
          <div className="mt-6 flex flex-wrap justify-end gap-2">
            <Dialog.Close asChild><Button variant="secondary">{cancelLabel}</Button></Dialog.Close>
            <Button onClick={confirmOnce} disabled={pending}>{confirmLabel}</Button>
          </div>
          <Dialog.Close asChild><Button type="button" variant="ghost" size="icon" aria-label="关闭对话框" className="absolute right-3 top-3"><X size={18} /></Button></Dialog.Close>
        </DialogLayer>
  )
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/assets/asset-format.ts =====
/** 素材页格式化与状态推导 —— 从 assets-page.tsx 抽出的纯函数，不含 JSX。 */

import { ApiError } from "@/lib/api"
import type { Asset, EvaluationCategoryProfile, UploadFileIssue } from "@/lib/types"

export type CategoryKey = EvaluationCategoryProfile["category_key"]

export type UploadFeedback = {
  source: string
  successful: string[]
  skipped: UploadFileIssue[]
  failed: UploadFileIssue[]
}

export function fileSize(bytes: number) {
  if (bytes < 1024 * 1024) return `${Math.max(1, Math.round(bytes / 1024))} KB`
  return `${(bytes / 1024 / 1024).toFixed(1)} MB`
}

export function fileType(mimeType: string) {
  return mimeType.split("/")[1]?.toUpperCase().replace("JPEG", "JPG") || "图片"
}

export function snapshotFiles(files: FileList | null) {
  return files ? Array.from(files) : []
}

export function relativeBrowserFileName(file: File) {
  const relativePath = (file as File & { webkitRelativePath?: string }).webkitRelativePath
  const raw = (relativePath || file.name).replaceAll("\\", "/")
  const parts = raw.split("/").filter((part) => part && part !== "." && part !== "..")
  const absolute = raw.startsWith("/") || raw.startsWith("//") || /^[A-Za-z]:\//.test(raw) || raw.split("/").includes("..")
  return (absolute ? parts.at(-1) : parts.join("/")) || "unnamed-file"
}

export function fileSkipReason(file: File, category?: EvaluationCategoryProfile) {
  const filename = relativeBrowserFileName(file)
  const parts = filename.split("/")
  const basename = parts.at(-1)?.toLowerCase() ?? ""
  if (
    parts.some((part) => part.startsWith("."))
    || parts.some((part) => part.toLowerCase() === "__macosx")
    || basename === "thumbs.db"
    || basename === "desktop.ini"
  ) return "隐藏或系统元数据"
  const suffixIndex = basename.lastIndexOf(".")
  const suffix = suffixIndex >= 0 ? basename.slice(suffixIndex) : ""
  const allowedSuffixes = new Set(category?.pipeline_config.allowed_suffixes.map((item) => item.toLowerCase()) ?? [])
  if (!allowedSuffixes.has(suffix)) return `当前类目不支持 ${suffix || "无扩展名"} 格式`
  return null
}

export function uploadIssuesFromError(error: unknown, key: "skipped_files" | "failed_files") {
  if (!(error instanceof ApiError) || !Array.isArray(error.detail?.[key])) return []
  return error.detail[key].filter((item): item is UploadFileIssue => (
    Boolean(item)
    && typeof item === "object"
    && "filename" in item
    && typeof item.filename === "string"
    && "reason" in item
    && typeof item.reason === "string"
  ))
}

export function evaluationStatus(value: Asset["evaluation_status"]) {
  return ({
    not_evaluated: "未评测",
    evaluated_old: "仅旧版本",
    evaluated_current: "当前版本已评测",
    queued: "已排队",
    running: "运行中",
    failed: "失败",
  } as const)[value ?? "not_evaluated"]
}

export function statusTone(value: Asset["evaluation_status"]) {
  if (value === "evaluated_current") return "success"
  if (value === "failed") return "danger"
  if (value === "queued" || value === "running") return "warning"
  return "active"
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/assets/case-dimension-import.tsx =====
import { useState, type ReactNode } from "react"
import { FolderSimple } from "@phosphor-icons/react"
import { useMutation } from "@tanstack/react-query"
import { toast } from "sonner"

import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input"
import { api } from "@/lib/api"
import type {
  DesignCaseDimensionImportResult,
  DesignCaseDimensionPreview,
} from "@/lib/types"

const LEVELS = ["L1", "L2", "L3", "L4", "L5"] as const
const CASE_GRADE: Record<string, string> = {
  L1: "好",
  L2: "中等",
  L3: "中差",
  L4: "极差",
  L5: "过滤",
}

function levelLine(counts: Record<string, number>, levels: readonly string[]) {
  return levels
    .filter((level) => counts[level])
    .map((level) => `${CASE_GRADE[level]}(${level}) ${counts[level]}`)
    .join(" · ")
}

/**
 * 案例两维度样本：从 NAS 交付目录导入。
 *
 * 一个目录同时喂两条基准——逐图人工档位给图片维度，案例正文里的聚合统计给
 * 素材维度。预检会把每张图都读一遍（要算内容哈希），所以看到的数字与真正
 * 落库的完全一致；目录大时这一步会慢，属于正常。
 */
export function CaseDimensionImport({
  categoryKey,
  packageName,
  disabled,
  onImported,
  render,
}: {
  categoryKey: string
  packageName: string
  disabled?: boolean
  onImported: () => Promise<void> | void
  /** Only presentation enters the drawer; the preview/mutation session stays. */
  render?: (controls: ReactNode) => ReactNode
}) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false)
  const [rootUri, setRootUri] = useState("")
  const [preview, setPreview] = useState<DesignCaseDimensionPreview | null>(null)

  function body() {
    return JSON.stringify({
      root_uri: rootUri.trim(),
      category_key: categoryKey,
      ...(packageName.trim() ? { package_name: packageName.trim() } : {}),
    })
  }

  const previewSamples = useMutation({
    mutationFn: () =>
      api<DesignCaseDimensionPreview>("/api/design-case-dimension-samples/preview", {
        method: "POST",
        body: body(),
      }),
    onSuccess: setPreview,
    onError: (error: Error) => {
      setPreview(null)
      toast.error(error.message)
    },
  })

  const importSamples = useMutation({
    mutationFn: () =>
      api<DesignCaseDimensionImportResult>("/api/design-case-dimension-samples/import", {
        method: "POST",
        body: body(),
      }),
    onSuccess: async (data) => {
      setPreview(null)
      setRootUri("")
      setOpen(false)
      await onImported()
      toast.success(
        `“${data.package.name}”已导入 ${data.summary.created_count} 个案例、`
          + `${data.summary.image_asset_created} 张新图`
          + (data.summary.image_asset_reused ? `（复用已有 ${data.summary.image_asset_reused} 张）` : "")
          + (data.summary.duplicate_count ? `，跳过重复案例 ${data.summary.duplicate_count} 个` : ""),
      )
    },
    onError: (error: Error) => toast.error(error.message),
  })

  const busy = previewSamples.isPending || importSamples.isPending

  const controls = (
    <>
      <Button variant="secondary" onClick={() => setOpen((value) => !value)} disabled={disabled}>
        <FolderSimple />案例两维度样本
      </Button>
      {open ? (
        <div className="col-span-full min-w-0 rounded-sm bg-surface-sunken p-4 text-sm">
          <label className="block">
            <span className="mb-2 block text-xs font-semibold">NAS 交付目录</span>
            <Input
              value={rootUri}
              onChange={(event) => {
                setRootUri(event.target.value)
                setPreview(null)
                previewSamples.reset()
                importSamples.reset()
              }}
              placeholder="nas://maps/采集任务交付文件/.../案例-普通样本/测试"
              disabled={busy}
            />
          </label>
          <p className="mt-2 text-xs leading-5 text-muted-foreground">
            目录下按 01好 / 02中等 / 03中差 / 04极差 / 05过滤 分档，
            每个案例一个「档位_案例编号」子目录，里面是正文 txt 与「档位_图片编号」图片。
            图片维度没有过滤档，单张图片只分好 / 中等 / 中差 / 极差。
          </p>
          <div className="mt-3 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
            <Button
              variant="secondary"
              onClick={() => previewSamples.mutate()}
              disabled={busy || !rootUri.trim()}
            >
              {previewSamples.isPending ? "正在读取 NAS" : "预检"}
            </Button>
            <Button
              variant="secondary"
              onClick={() => {
                setOpen(false)
                setPreview(null)
              }}
              disabled={busy}
            >
              取消
            </Button>
          </div>

          {previewSamples.error || importSamples.error ? (
            <p role="alert" className="mt-3 break-words rounded-sm bg-danger-wash px-3 py-2 text-sm text-danger">
              {(importSamples.error ?? previewSamples.error)?.message}
            </p>
          ) : null}

          {preview ? (
            <div className="mt-4 border-t border-[var(--line)] pt-3">
              <p className="font-semibold">
                共 {preview.summary.cases} 个案例、{preview.summary.images} 张图片
              </p>
              <dl className="mt-2 space-y-1 text-xs leading-5 text-muted-foreground">
                <div>
                  <dt className="inline font-semibold">案例档位</dt>
                  <dd className="ml-2 inline">{levelLine(preview.summary.case_levels, LEVELS)}</dd>
                </div>
                <div>
                  <dt className="inline font-semibold">图片档位</dt>
                  <dd className="ml-2 inline">
                    {levelLine(preview.summary.image_levels, LEVELS.slice(0, 4))}
                  </dd>
                </div>
                <div>
                  <dt className="inline font-semibold">图片类型</dt>
                  <dd className="ml-2 inline">
                    {Object.entries(preview.summary.traits)
                      .map(([name, count]) => `${name} ${count}`)
                      .join(" · ") || "（未统计）"}
                    {preview.summary.dropped_trait_images
                      ? `（剔除未返回类型 ${preview.summary.dropped_trait_images} 张）`
                      : ""}
                  </dd>
                </div>
              </dl>
              <ul className="mt-3 max-h-56 space-y-1 overflow-y-auto text-xs">
                {preview.cases.slice(0, 200).map((item) => (
                  <li key={item.cloud_id} className="flex flex-wrap justify-between gap-x-3 gap-y-1">
                    <span className="min-w-0 break-words">
                      {CASE_GRADE[item.case_level] ?? item.case_level} · {item.cloud_id}{" "}
                      {item.case_name}
                    </span>
                    <span className="text-muted-foreground">
                      {item.image_count} 张 · {levelLine(item.image_levels, LEVELS.slice(0, 4))}
                    </span>
                  </li>
                ))}
              </ul>
              {preview.cases.length > 200 ? (
                <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                  仅列出前 200 个，导入按全部 {preview.cases.length} 个执行
                </p>
              ) : null}
              <div className="mt-3">
                <Button onClick={() => importSamples.mutate()} disabled={busy || !preview.summary.cases}>
                  {importSamples.isPending
                    ? "正在导入"
                    : `确认导入 ${preview.summary.cases} 个案例 / ${preview.summary.images} 张图`}
                </Button>
              </div>
            </div>
          ) : null}
        </div>
      ) : null}
    </>
  )
  return render ? render(controls) : controls
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/assets/case-sample-import.tsx =====
import { useRef, useState, type ReactNode } from "react"
import { FileXls } from "@phosphor-icons/react"
import { useMutation } from "@tanstack/react-query"
import { toast } from "sonner"

import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { api } from "@/lib/api"
import type { CaseSampleImportResult, CaseSamplePreview } from "@/lib/types"

/** 素材（案例）维度样本：一行一个案例，正文里带图片总数、分数分布、类型分布。 */
export function CaseSampleImport({
  categoryKey,
  packageName,
  disabled,
  onImported,
  render,
}: {
  categoryKey: string
  packageName: string
  disabled?: boolean
  onImported: () => Promise<void> | void
  /** Keep the import session mounted while its drawer's controls are absent. */
  render?: (controls: ReactNode) => ReactNode
}) {
  const inputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null)
  const [file, setFile] = useState<File | null>(null)
  const [preview, setPreview] = useState<CaseSamplePreview | null>(null)

  function form(picked: File) {
    const body = new FormData()
    body.append("file", picked)
    body.append("category_key", categoryKey)
    if (packageName.trim()) body.append("package_name", packageName.trim())
    return body
  }

  const previewSamples = useMutation({
    mutationFn: (picked: File) =>
      api<CaseSamplePreview>("/api/design-case-samples/preview", { method: "POST", body: form(picked) }),
    onSuccess: (data, picked) => {
      setFile(picked)
      setPreview(data)
    },
    onError: (error: Error) => {
      setFile(null)
      setPreview(null)
      toast.error(error.message)
    },
  })

  const importSamples = useMutation({
    mutationFn: (picked: File) =>
      api<CaseSampleImportResult>("/api/design-case-samples/import", { method: "POST", body: form(picked) }),
    onSuccess: async (data) => {
      setFile(null)
      setPreview(null)
      await onImported()
      toast.success(
        `“${data.package.name}”已导入 ${data.summary.created_count} 个案例`
        + (data.summary.duplicate_count ? `，跳过重复 ${data.summary.duplicate_count} 个` : "")
        + (data.summary.rejected_rows ? `，无法解析 ${data.summary.rejected_rows} 行` : ""),
      )
    },
    onError: (error: Error) => toast.error(error.message),
  })

  const busy = previewSamples.isPending || importSamples.isPending
  const flagged = preview?.rows.filter((row) => !row.ok || row.warnings.length) ?? []

  const controls = (
    <>
      <input
        ref={inputRef}
        type="file"
        accept=".xlsx"
        className="sr-only"
        tabIndex={-1}
        onChange={(event) => {
          const picked = event.target.files?.[0]
          event.target.value = ""
          if (picked) previewSamples.mutate(picked)
        }}
      />
      <Button variant="secondary" onClick={() => inputRef.current?.click()} disabled={disabled || busy}>
        <FileXls />素材维度样本表
      </Button>
      {preview && file ? (
        <div className="col-span-full min-w-0 rounded-sm bg-surface-sunken p-4 text-sm">
          <p className="break-words font-semibold">
            {file.name}：共 {preview.summary.total_rows} 行，可导入 {preview.summary.usable_rows} 个案例
            {preview.summary.rejected_rows ? `，无法解析 ${preview.summary.rejected_rows} 行` : ""}
            {preview.summary.warned_rows ? `，${preview.summary.warned_rows} 行有提醒` : ""}
          </p>
          <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-5 text-muted-foreground">
            工作表 {preview.source.sheet_name} 第 {preview.source.column_number} 列
            {preview.source.header_is_data ? "（首行即数据）" : "（已跳过表头）"}
            ；带图片统计的案例才能进素材维度基准集
          </p>
          {flagged.length ? (
            <ul className="mt-3 max-h-48 space-y-1 overflow-y-auto text-xs">
              {flagged.slice(0, 50).map((row) => (
                <li key={row.row_number} className={`break-words ${row.ok ? "text-muted-foreground" : "text-danger"}`}>
                  第 {row.row_number} 行 {row.case_name || "（无标题）"}：
                  {[...row.issues, ...row.warnings].join("；")}
                </li>
              ))}
            </ul>
          ) : null}
          <div className="mt-3 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
            <Button onClick={() => importSamples.mutate(file)} disabled={busy || !preview.summary.usable_rows}>
              {importSamples.isPending ? "正在导入" : `确认导入 ${preview.summary.usable_rows} 个案例`}
            </Button>
            <Button variant="secondary" onClick={() => { setFile(null); setPreview(null) }} disabled={busy}>
              取消
            </Button>
          </div>
        </div>
      ) : null}
    </>
  )
  return render ? render(controls) : controls
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/assets/material-package-cover.tsx =====
import { useState } from "react"
import { ImageSquare } from "@phosphor-icons/react"
import type { MaterialPackageCoverAsset } from "@/lib/types"

function CoverSlot({ asset }: { asset?: MaterialPackageCoverAsset }) {
  const [broken, setBroken] = useState(false)
  return (
    <span data-cover-slot="" className="relative flex min-h-0 min-w-0 items-center justify-center bg-surface-sunken first:row-span-2">
      {asset && !broken ? (
        <img src={asset.image_url} alt="" loading="lazy" onError={() => setBroken(true)} className="block h-full w-full min-w-0 object-cover" />
      ) : (
        <span className="flex flex-col items-center gap-1 p-2 text-center text-xs text-muted-foreground">
          <ImageSquare size={20} aria-hidden="true" />
          {broken ? "封面加载失败" : "无封面"}
        </span>
      )}
    </span>
  )
}

/** The approved collage crops covers only. Gallery/original images stay whole.
 * Data comes solely from the list's optional cover_assets; no cover lookup API.
 */
export function MaterialPackageCover({ assets }: { assets?: MaterialPackageCoverAsset[] }) {
  return (
    <span data-package-cover="" className="relative grid aspect-[16/10] w-full shrink-0 grid-cols-[2fr_1fr] grid-rows-2 gap-1 overflow-hidden rounded-md">
      {[0, 1, 2].map(index => <CoverSlot key={`${index}:${assets?.[index]?.asset_id ?? "empty"}:${assets?.[index]?.image_url ?? ""}`} asset={assets?.[index]} />)}
      <span aria-hidden="true" className="pointer-events-none absolute inset-0 rounded-md shadow-[var(--image-outline)]" />
    </span>
  )
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/assets/pagination.ts =====
export type PageWindow = {
  page: number
  pageCount: number
  canPrevious: boolean
  canNext: boolean
}

export function pageWindow(total: number, offset: number, limit: number): PageWindow {
  const safeTotal = Number.isFinite(total) ? Math.max(0, Math.floor(total)) : 0
  const safeLimit = Number.isFinite(limit) && limit > 0 ? Math.floor(limit) : 1
  const pageCount = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(safeTotal / safeLimit))
  const requestedPage = Math.floor(Math.max(0, offset) / safeLimit) + 1
  const page = Math.min(pageCount, requestedPage)

  return {
    page,
    pageCount,
    canPrevious: page > 1,
    canNext: page < pageCount,
  }
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/assets/upload-feedback-panel.tsx =====
/** 上传结果反馈面板 —— 从 assets-page.tsx 抽出。 */

import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge"
import type { UploadFeedback } from "./asset-format"

export function UploadFeedbackPanel({ feedback }: { feedback: UploadFeedback }) {
  const groups: Array<{
    key: "successful" | "skipped" | "failed"
    label: string
    tone: "success" | "warning" | "danger"
    items: string[]
  }> = [
    { key: "successful", label: "成功", tone: "success", items: feedback.successful },
    {
      key: "skipped",
      label: "跳过",
      tone: "warning",
      items: feedback.skipped.map((item) => `${item.filename} · ${item.reason}`),
    },
    {
      key: "failed",
      label: "失败",
      tone: "danger",
      items: feedback.failed.map((item) => `${item.filename} · ${item.reason}`),
    },
  ]
  return (
    <div className="min-w-0 border-t border-line py-4" aria-live="polite">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
        <p className="min-w-0 break-words text-sm font-bold">{feedback.source}处理结果</p>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
          {groups.map((group) => <Badge key={group.key} tone={group.tone}>{group.label} {group.items.length}</Badge>)}
        </div>
      </div>
      <div className="mt-3 grid gap-3">
        {groups.map((group) => (
          <details key={group.key} className="visual-native-details border-t border-line pt-2" open={group.key !== "successful" && group.items.length > 0}>
            <summary className="cursor-pointer text-xs font-semibold">{group.label}文件（{group.items.length}）</summary>
            <div className="font-data mt-2 max-h-32 space-y-1 overflow-auto text-xs leading-5 text-muted-foreground">
              {group.items.length
                ? group.items.map((item, index) => <p className="break-all" key={`${item}-${index}`}>{item}</p>)
                : <p>无</p>}
            </div>
          </details>
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/baseline-regression/acceptance-query.ts =====
type AcceptanceRun = {
  id: number
  total: number
  status: string
  workflow_runtime?: { pending: number } | null
}

export function baselineAcceptanceReady(
  selectedRunId: number,
  run: AcceptanceRun | undefined,
): boolean {
  return selectedRunId > 0
    && run?.id === selectedRunId
    && run.total > 0
    && ["completed", "partial_failed", "failed"].includes(run.status)
    && (run.workflow_runtime?.pending ?? 0) === 0
}

/** The complete review queue is needed only after model execution settles. */
export function baselineAcceptanceQueryOptions<Item>({
  selectedRunId,
  run,
  fetchPage,
  requested = true,
}: {
  selectedRunId: number
  run: AcceptanceRun | undefined
  fetchPage: (runId: number, offset: number, limit: number, signal: AbortSignal) => Promise<{ items: Item[] }>
  requested?: boolean
}) {
  const pageSize = 1000
  return {
    queryKey: ["baseline-acceptance", selectedRunId, run?.total] as const,
    enabled: requested && baselineAcceptanceReady(selectedRunId, run),
    refetchInterval: false as const,
    queryFn: async ({ signal }: { signal: AbortSignal }): Promise<Item[][]> => {
      const pages: Item[][] = []
      for (let offset = 0; offset < (run?.total ?? 0); offset += pageSize) {
        signal.throwIfAborted()
        const detail = await fetchPage(selectedRunId, offset, pageSize, signal)
        pages.push(detail.items)
        if (detail.items.length < pageSize) break
      }
      return pages
    },
  }
}

export function isImageMimeType(value: unknown): boolean {
  return typeof value === "string" && value.startsWith("image/")
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/baseline-regression/asset-selection.ts =====
/** 基准集创建时的素材勾选：素材分页展示，但选择集必须跨页累加。 */

export function isPageFullySelected(
  selected: ReadonlySet<number>,
  pageAssetIds: readonly number[],
): boolean {
  // 不能拿选择集总数去比本页条数：跨页累加后总数必然大于本页，判断会失真。
  return pageAssetIds.length > 0 && pageAssetIds.every((id) => selected.has(id))
}

export function togglePageSelection(
  selected: ReadonlySet<number>,
  pageAssetIds: readonly number[],
): Set<number> {
  const next = new Set(selected)
  if (isPageFullySelected(selected, pageAssetIds)) {
    for (const id of pageAssetIds) next.delete(id)
  } else {
    for (const id of pageAssetIds) next.add(id)
  }
  return next
}

export function addToSelection(
  selected: ReadonlySet<number>,
  assetIds: readonly number[],
): Set<number> {
  const next = new Set(selected)
  for (const id of assetIds) next.add(id)
  return next
}

/** 分页取全所有 id 时的 offset 序列。 */
export function selectionPageOffsets(total: number, pageSize: number): number[] {
  if (total <= 0 || pageSize <= 0) return []
  const offsets: number[] = []
  for (let offset = 0; offset < total; offset += pageSize) offsets.push(offset)
  return offsets
}

const STRATEGY_LABELS: Record<BalancedRebuildStrategy, string> = {

export function BalancedRebuildForm({
  survey,
  loading,
  error,
  perLevel,
  strategy,
  seed,
  onPerLevel,
  onStrategy,
  onSeed,
}: {
  survey: BalancedRebuildSurvey | undefined
  loading: boolean
  error: unknown
  perLevel: number
  strategy: BalancedRebuildStrategy
  seed: number
  onPerLevel: (value: number) => void
  onStrategy: (value: BalancedRebuildStrategy) => void
  onSeed: (value: number) => void
}) {
  if (loading) {
    return <p className="text-sm text-[var(--muted)]">正在统计可用的人工评级素材…</p>
  }
  if (error) {
    return <div className="border border-alias-edge-036 bg-alias-surface-067 px-3 py-3 text-xs text-alias-content-041 rounded-md">

    </div>
  }
  if (!survey) return null

  const current = survey.current_balanced_set
  const overQuota = perLevel > survey.max_per_level
  const levels = Object.keys(survey.selectable_distribution).sort()

  return <div className="space-y-6">
    <section className="space-y-3">
      <div>
        <p className="text-sm font-bold">为什么需要重建</p>
        <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">

          所以想纳入新标注的素材，只能按新参数重新抽一份、冻结成新的基准集。
        </p>
      </div>
      {current && <div className="border border-[var(--line)] bg-alias-surface-051 px-3 py-3 text-xs leading-5 rounded-md">
        <p className="font-semibold">现有样本：{current.name}</p>
        <p className="text-[var(--muted)]">
          {current.item_count} 张 · 已跑过 {current.run_count} 轮回归 · 冻结于 {formatPlatformDate(current.created_at)}
          {current.max_asset_id != null && <> · 覆盖到 asset_id {current.max_asset_id}</>}
        </p>
        {current.max_asset_id != null && <p className="mt-1 text-[var(--muted)]">
          它是按 asset_id 升序抽的，所以 asset_id 大于 <span className="visual-code">{current.max_asset_id}</span> 的已评级素材永远进不了这份样本。
        </p>}
      </div>}
      <div className="border border-[var(--line)] px-3 py-3 text-xs leading-5 rounded-md">
        <p className="font-semibold">当前可抽取的素材</p>
        <p className="text-[var(--muted)]">
          共 {survey.candidate_total} 张带人工评级前缀；去掉已删除 {survey.deleted_excluded} 张、
          重复图 {survey.duplicate_sha256_skipped} 张后，每级最多可抽 <strong>{survey.max_per_level}</strong> 张。
        </p>
        <div className="mt-2 flex flex-wrap gap-x-4 gap-y-1 font-data text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">
          {levels.map((level) => (
            <span key={level}>{level}：{survey.selectable_distribution[level]}</span>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>

    <section className="space-y-4 border-t border-[var(--line)] pt-6">
      <p className="text-sm font-bold">抽样参数</p>
      <div className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
        <label>
          <span className="mb-2 block text-xs font-semibold">每个等级抽几张</span>
          <input
            type="number"
            className={"visual-native-input " + (inputClass)}
            min={1}
            max={survey.max_per_level || 1}
            value={perLevel}
            onChange={(event) => onPerLevel(Number(event.target.value))}
          />
          <span className="mt-1 block text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">
            L1–L5 各取这么多张，合计 {perLevel * 5} 张。
          </span>

          <select

            value={strategy}

          >
            {survey.strategies.map((item) => (
              <option key={item} value={item}>{STRATEGY_LABELS[item] ?? item}</option>
            ))}
          </select>
          <span className="mt-1 block text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">
            {STRATEGY_NOTES[strategy]}

        {/* 种子只对全局均匀抽样有意义：按时间取的两种方式完全由上传顺序决定，
            换种子不会改变结果，所以那时不显示这个输入。 */}
        {strategy === "stable_hash" && <label>

          <input
            type="number"

          </span>
        </label>}

      <p className="text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">

        新样本冻结后需要重新跑回归才有指标，历史回归仍然挂在原样本上。
      </p>
    </section>
  </div>
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/baseline-regression/baseline-error-guidance.ts =====
const WORKFLOW_ERROR_GUIDANCE: Record<string, string> = {
  call_b_unavailable:
    "AI 提示词调用节点失败：调用 B 没有产出美感基础分。请先检查模型可用性并重试；持续失败时，在工作流管理器切换提示词或模型版本。",
  prompt_pipeline_mismatch:
    "AI 提示词调用节点配置不匹配：所选提示词属于另一条评分管线。请返回工作流管理器，更换提示词或规则合同版本。",
  operator_prompt_contract_conflict:
    "提示词与规则合同节点版本冲突：两者要求的输出结构不同。请返回工作流管理器，重新配对版本后再运行。",
}

const RESULT_ERROR_LABELS: Record<string, string> = {
  kg_output_value_invalid: "模型输出的字段值不符合合同规则",
  kg_output_fields_missing: "模型输出缺少合同要求的字段",
  kg_output_identity_mismatch: "模型没有原样返回素材 ID",
  structured_output_schema_mismatch: "模型输出不符合提示词冻结的 JSON 结构",
  model_config_snapshot_drift: "该版本冻结的模型已过期（模型管理里的模型 ID 或端点已变），模型未被调用；请改选或重新生成机制版本",
  model_source_unavailable: "冻结的模型已停用或密钥不可用",
  model_real_execution_disabled: "管理员已关闭该模型的真实调用授权",
  "technical:operator_canceled": "本轮被人工取消",
  missing_level: "未形成 L1-L5 有效等级",
  no_authoritative_score: "未形成服务端权威分数",
  missing_quality_evidence: "未返回画质证据",
  missing_confidence: "未返回模型置信度",
  missing_precheck_scope_status: "调用 A 未返回评测范围字段，无法继续调用 B",
  missing_prompt_b_response: "调用 B 未返回结果",
  missing_aesthetic_result: "未形成八个美感维度结果",
}

export function baselineErrorMessage(error: string) {
  // 整串就是一个已知错误码时直接给人话（原生工作流的条目只存错误码，没有冒号后缀）
  const direct = RESULT_ERROR_LABELS[error.trim()]
  if (direct) return direct
  for (const [code, guidance] of Object.entries(WORKFLOW_ERROR_GUIDANCE)) {
    const codeIndex = error.indexOf(code)
    if (codeIndex < 0) continue
    const detail = error
      .slice(codeIndex + code.length)
      .replace(/^[\s:：-]+/, "")
      .trim()
    return detail ? `${guidance} 系统详情：${detail}` : guidance
  }

  const separatorIndex = error.indexOf(":")
  if (separatorIndex < 0) return error
  const reasons = error.slice(separatorIndex + 1)
  return reasons
    .split(",")
    .map((reason) => RESULT_ERROR_LABELS[reason] ?? reason)
    .join("；")
}

/**
 * 把被拒的模型输出压成一句话：优先只展示出错的那个字段（从原因文案里认出字段名），
 * 认不出时展示前几个非空字段，避免把整段 JSON 糊到列表上。
 */
export function rejectedOutputSummary(
  output: Record<string, unknown>,
  reason: string,
): string {
  const entries = Object.entries(output).filter(([, value]) => value !== "" && value !== null)
  const named = entries.filter(([key]) => reason.includes(key))
  const picked = (named.length ? named : entries).slice(0, named.length ? named.length : 3)
  return picked.map(([key, value]) => `${key} = ${JSON.stringify(value)}`).join("；")
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/baseline-regression/baseline-evidence-refresh.ts =====
import type { QueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query"

export type BaselineEvidenceRunState = {
  id: number
  status: "running" | "completed" | "partial_failed" | "failed"
  workflow_runtime: { pending: number }
}

export async function refreshCompletedBaselineEvidence(
  queryClient: QueryClient,
  run: BaselineEvidenceRunState,
) {
  if (run.status === "running" || run.workflow_runtime.pending > 0) return false

  await Promise.all([
    queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["baseline-field-metrics", run.id] }),
    queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["baseline-semantic-metrics", run.id] }),
  ])
  return true
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/baseline-regression/baseline-performance-summary.tsx =====
import type { ReactNode } from "react"
import type { BaselineRunMetrics, BaselineTruthFieldMetric } from "@/lib/types"

import { baselineRunMetricsView } from "./baseline-run-metrics"
import { LevelPerformanceSummary } from "./level-performance-summary"
import { structuredFieldLabel } from "./structured-truth"

const percentFormatter = new Intl.NumberFormat("zh-CN", {
  style: "percent",
  maximumFractionDigits: 2,
})

export function BaselinePerformanceSummary({
  metrics,
  actions,
}: {
  metrics: BaselineRunMetrics
  actions?: ReactNode
}) {
  const view = baselineRunMetricsView(metrics)
  const imageMetrics = "image_grade_metrics" in metrics ? metrics.image_grade_metrics : null
  if (imageMetrics) {
    const expected = imageMetrics.compared + imageMetrics.missing
    return (
      <section className="space-y-5" aria-label="图片维度评测表现">
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-3"><h3 className="visual-card-title">图片维度评测表现</h3>{actions}</div>
        <p className="text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">准确率仅统计已有预测且按图片身份对齐的图片；缺失图片不进入准确率分母。覆盖率按本轮已纳入比对的真值图片计算。</p>
        <div className="grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-4">
          <StructuredMetric label="逐图档位准确率" value={imageMetrics.compared ? formatPercent(imageMetrics.accuracy) : "—"} hint={imageMetrics.compared ? `${imageMetrics.correct}/${imageMetrics.compared} 张已对齐图片档位一致` : "暂无可比较图片"} />
          <StructuredMetric label="图片预测覆盖率" value={expected ? formatPercent(imageMetrics.compared / expected) : "—"} hint={`${imageMetrics.compared}/${expected} 张真值图片有可比较预测；缺失 ${imageMetrics.missing} 张`} />
          <StructuredMetric label="档位高估 / 低估" value={`${imageMetrics.over_graded} / ${imageMetrics.under_graded}`} hint="高估：模型档位优于人工；低估：模型档位低于人工" />
          <StructuredMetric label="缺失 / 额外图片" value={`${imageMetrics.missing} / ${imageMetrics.unexpected}`} hint={`本轮 ${view.validPredictions}/${view.total} 个案例产生可用结果`} />
        </div>
        <div className="baseline-matrix-frame overflow-x-auto rounded-md border border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-white">
          <table className="w-full text-left text-xs"><caption className="p-3 text-left font-bold">逐图档位混淆矩阵（行：人工真值；列：模型预测）</caption>
            <thead><tr><th className="p-3">人工 / 模型</th>{["L1", "L2", "L3", "L4"].map((level) => <th key={level} className="p-3">{level}</th>)}</tr></thead>
            <tbody>{["L1", "L2", "L3", "L4"].map((level) => <tr key={level} className="border-t border-[var(--line)]"><th className="p-3">{level}</th>{["L1", "L2", "L3", "L4"].map((predicted) => <td key={predicted} className="p-3">{imageMetrics.confusion[level]?.[predicted] ?? 0}</td>)}</tr>)}</tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      </section>
    )
  }
  if (view.levelMetrics) {
    return <LevelPerformanceSummary metrics={view.levelMetrics} actions={actions} />
  }
  // 等级回归但还没有任何成功结果：如实说明指标待生成，不要退回字段看板——
  // 那是知识图谱的口径，摆在灵感图页面上只会让人以为选错了类目。
  if (view.kind === "level") {
    return (
      <section className="space-y-5" aria-label="等级评测表现">
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-end justify-between gap-3">
          <div>
            <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-[var(--muted)]">当前回归任务</p>
            <h3 className="mt-1 visual-card-title">等级评测表现</h3>
          </div>
          <div className="space-y-2">{actions}<p className="text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">按 L1–L5 等级矩阵核对</p></div>
        </div>
        <div className="visual-panel border border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-white px-5 py-8 text-center">
          <p className="text-sm font-bold">本轮尚无可用结果，等级指标暂未生成</p>
          <p className="mt-2 text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">
            已完成 {view.validPredictions}/{view.total} 条
            {view.failed > 0 ? `，其中 ${view.failed} 条失败` : ""}
            。等级准确率与混淆矩阵需要至少一条成功结果才能计算；请先到下方逐条结果查看失败原因。
          </p>
        </div>
      </section>
    )
  }

  const aggregate = view.fieldAggregate
  const identity = "identity_aggregate" in metrics ? metrics.identity_aggregate : null
  const noSemanticTruth = !aggregate?.compared
  return (
    <section className="space-y-5" aria-label="字段评测表现">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-end justify-between gap-3">
        <div>
          <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-[var(--muted)]">当前回归任务</p>
          <h3 className="mt-1 visual-card-title">字段评测表现</h3>
        </div>
        <div className="space-y-2">{actions}<p className="text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">知识图谱按字段核对，不套用 L1–L5 等级矩阵</p></div>
      </div>

      <div className="grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-4">
        <StructuredMetric label="标签字段准确率" value={noSemanticTruth ? "待标注" : formatPercent(aggregate?.accuracy)} hint={noSemanticTruth ? "没有语义标签真值，无法评估标签质量" : `${aggregate!.correct}/${aggregate!.compared} 个标签字段完全一致`} />
        <StructuredMetric label="字段精确率" value={formatPercent(aggregate?.precision)} hint="预测出的标签中，有多少与真值一致" />
        <StructuredMetric label="字段召回率" value={formatPercent(aggregate?.recall)} hint="人工真值中的标签，有多少被成功找出" />
        <StructuredMetric label="有效覆盖率" value={formatPercent(view.coverage)} hint={`${view.validPredictions}/${view.total} 条已产生可用结果`} />
      </div>

      {identity && identity.compared > 0 && <p className="text-sm leading-6 text-[var(--muted)]">身份与路线一致率 {formatPercent(identity.accuracy)}（{identity.correct}/{identity.compared} 个字段），仅核对 ID / 整体与单体路线，不计入标签质量。</p>}

      <section className="visual-panel border border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-white" aria-label="逐字段表现">
        <div className="border-b border-[var(--line)] px-5 py-4">
          <p className="text-sm font-bold">逐字段表现</p>
          <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">先看准确率定位需要纠偏的字段，再进入下方逐条结果查看具体差异。</p>
        </div>
        <div className="grid sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-3">
          {Object.entries(view.fieldMetrics).map(([fieldKey, field]) => (
            <FieldMetric key={fieldKey} fieldKey={fieldKey} metric={field} />
          ))}
        </div>
      </section>
    </section>
  )
}

function StructuredMetric({
  label,
  value,
  hint,
}: {
  label: string
  value: string
  hint: string
}) {
  return (
    <div className="baseline-metric min-w-0 rounded-md border border-line bg-surface-sunken px-5 py-4">
      <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-[var(--muted)]">{label}</p>
      <p className="font-data mt-2 text-2xl font-bold">{value}</p>
      <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">{hint}</p>
    </div>
  )
}

function FieldMetric({
  fieldKey,
  metric,
}: {
  fieldKey: string
  metric: BaselineTruthFieldMetric
}) {
  return (
    <div className="border-b border-r border-[var(--line)] px-5 py-4">
      <div className="flex items-baseline justify-between gap-3">
        <p className="text-sm font-bold">{structuredFieldLabel(fieldKey)}</p>
        <p className="font-data text-lg font-bold">{formatPercent(metric.accuracy)}</p>
      </div>
      <p className="font-data mt-1 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">
        {metric.correct}/{metric.compared} 条一致
      </p>
    </div>
  )
}

function formatPercent(value: number | null | undefined) {
  return typeof value === "number" ? percentFormatter.format(value) : "—"
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/baseline-regression/baseline-regression-contract.ts =====
export type BaselineAcceptanceRow = {
  status: "queued" | "completed" | "failed"
  evaluation?: {
    human_review?: {
      decision?: "approved" | "corrected" | "rejected" | null
    } | null
  } | null
}

export type BaselineAcceptanceProgress = {
  reviewed: number
  total: number
  complete: boolean
}

import type { BaselineV3Revision, PromptVersion } from "@/lib/types"

export type BaselineV3Mode = "active" | "candidate"

export type CandidatePromptBindingResolution = {
  status: "available" | "unavailable" | "unbound"
  promptId: number | null
  requestedVersion: string | null
  reason?: "missing" | "stage" | "archived" | "pipeline_scope"
}

type CandidatePrompt = Pick<
  PromptVersion,
  "id" | "stage" | "version" | "status" | "pipeline_scope"
>

export function resolveCandidatePromptBinding(
  prompts: CandidatePrompt[],
  stage: "A" | "B",
  requestedVersion: string | null,
): CandidatePromptBindingResolution {
  const normalizedVersion = requestedVersion?.trim() || null
  if (!normalizedVersion) {
    return {
      status: "unbound",
      promptId: null,
      requestedVersion: null,
    }
  }

  const exactVersion = prompts.find(
    (prompt) => prompt.version === normalizedVersion && prompt.stage === stage,
  ) ?? prompts.find((prompt) => prompt.version === normalizedVersion)
  if (!exactVersion) {
    return {
      status: "unavailable",
      promptId: null,
      requestedVersion: normalizedVersion,
      reason: "missing",
    }
  }
  if (exactVersion.stage !== stage) {
    return {
      status: "unavailable",
      promptId: exactVersion.id,
      requestedVersion: normalizedVersion,
      reason: "stage",
    }
  }
  if (exactVersion.status === "archived") {
    return {
      status: "unavailable",
      promptId: exactVersion.id,
      requestedVersion: normalizedVersion,
      reason: "archived",
    }
  }
  if (exactVersion.pipeline_scope !== "baseline_regression" && exactVersion.pipeline_scope !== "shared") {
    return {
      status: "unavailable",
      promptId: exactVersion.id,
      requestedVersion: normalizedVersion,
      reason: "pipeline_scope",
    }
  }
  return {
    status: "available",
    promptId: exactVersion.id,
    requestedVersion: normalizedVersion,
  }
}

export function v3RevisionGroup(
  revision: Pick<BaselineV3Revision, "status" | "id">,
  projectedRevisionId: number,
): "active" | "candidate" | "history" {
  if (revision.id === projectedRevisionId || revision.status === "active") return "active"
  if (revision.status === "candidate") return "candidate"
  return "history"
}

export type V3CandidateLineage = "on_active_chain" | "diverged"

type LineageRevision = Pick<
  BaselineV3Revision,
  "id" | "status" | "parent_revision_id" | "category_key"
>

function ancestorIdsOf(
  startId: number,
  byId: Map<number, LineageRevision>,
): Set<number> {
  const chain = new Set<number>()
  let cursor = byId.get(startId)
  while (cursor && !chain.has(cursor.id)) {
    chain.add(cursor.id)
    if (cursor.parent_revision_id == null) break
    cursor = byId.get(cursor.parent_revision_id)
  }
  return chain
}

/**
 * Revisions are linked child -> parent (a new revision points at the one it
 * replaced), so the active revision sits at the *tip* of its chain and is never
 * an ancestor of a candidate. A candidate is therefore "on the active chain"
 * when it branches off any revision in the active revision's own ancestry.
 */
export function v3CandidateLineage(
  revision: LineageRevision,
  revisions: LineageRevision[],
  projectedRevisionId: number,
): V3CandidateLineage {
  const byId = new Map(revisions.map((item) => [item.id, item]))
  const activeChain = ancestorIdsOf(projectedRevisionId, byId)
  const seen = new Set<number>()
  let cursor: LineageRevision | undefined = byId.get(revision.id) ?? revision
  while (cursor) {
    if (activeChain.has(cursor.id)) return "on_active_chain"
    if (seen.has(cursor.id) || cursor.parent_revision_id == null) break
    seen.add(cursor.id)
    cursor = byId.get(cursor.parent_revision_id)
  }
  return "diverged"
}

/**
 * Selectability is intentionally limited to category + lifecycle status. Lineage
 * drift is surfaced as a note via {@link v3CandidateLineage} instead of blocking
 * the run, so operators keep full manual control over the graded matcher.
 */
export function isSelectableV3Candidate(
  revision: LineageRevision,
  _revisions: LineageRevision[],
  _projectedRevisionId: number,
): boolean {
  return revision.status === "candidate"
}

export function resolveV3PromptBinding(
  revision: Pick<BaselineV3Revision, "contract">,
  stage: "A" | "B",
): string | null {
  const bindings = revision.contract.prompt_bindings
  if (!bindings || typeof bindings !== "object") return null
  const key = stage === "A" ? "call_a_version" : "call_b_version"
  const value = (bindings as Record<string, unknown>)[key]
  return typeof value === "string" && value.trim() ? value : null
}

export type BaselineRunPayload = {
  workflow_version_id: number
  execution_mode?: "freeform" | "structured"
  model_execution_mode: "simulated" | "real"
  inference_mode: "online" | "batch"
  real_execution_confirmed: boolean
  category_context?: {
    source: "baseline_set"
    category_key: string
  }
}

export function baselineExecutionModeHelp(
  truthKind: "level" | "structured_fields",
  executionMode: "freeform" | "structured",
) {
  if (executionMode === "freeform") {
    return "自由实验不会要求固定 JSON、范围字段或八维输出；系统完整保留原始回答，能安全识别时自动计分，否则进入人工判断。"
  }
  if (truthKind === "structured_fields") {
    return "标准字段合同会逐项校验知识图谱字段、类型与当前路线；缺少必填字段或字段格式不合同时会停止并提示人工处理。"
  }
  return "标准评分合同沿用现有结构化协议；缺少范围、等级、分数或所选维度时会按合同失败。"
}

export function buildBaselineRunPayload(selection: {
  workflowVersionId: number
  executionMode?: "freeform" | "structured"
  categoryKey?: string | null
  modelExecutionMode?: "simulated" | "real"
  inferenceMode: "online" | "batch"
  realExecutionConfirmed?: boolean
}): BaselineRunPayload {
  const payload: BaselineRunPayload = {
    workflow_version_id: selection.workflowVersionId,
    model_execution_mode: selection.modelExecutionMode ?? "simulated",
    real_execution_confirmed: selection.realExecutionConfirmed ?? false,
    // 界面只有两种选择，且必须显式下发：省略等同于批量，但把它写清楚，
    // 服务端日志与冻结快照里就能直接看到这一轮是谁选的。
    inference_mode: selection.inferenceMode,
  }
  if (selection.executionMode) payload.execution_mode = selection.executionMode
  if (selection.categoryKey) {
    payload.category_context = {
      source: "baseline_set",
      category_key: selection.categoryKey,
    }
  }
  return payload
}

/**
 * The run the address pins, unless the page has just left that run.
 *
 * Picking another baseline set (or category) clears the run in local state at once,
 * but React Router applies the address change a render later. In that one render the
 * address still names the run that was left; treating it as pinned would select it
 * again, and its detail would then pull the page back to its own baseline set.
 *
 * ``leftRunId`` is the run of the address the page left, and only while that same
 * address is still the current one (the page compares the location key). Any later
 * navigation, to the same run included, is a new pin.
 */
export function baselinePinnedRunId(urlRunId: number, leftRunId: number): number {
  return urlRunId > 0 && urlRunId !== leftRunId ? urlRunId : 0
}

export function baselineRunIdAfterSetLoad(
  currentRunId: number,
  runs: Array<{ id: number }> | null,
  pinnedRunId = 0,
): number {
  if (pinnedRunId > 0) return pinnedRunId
  if (runs === null) return currentRunId
  if (runs.some((run) => run.id === currentRunId)) return currentRunId
  return runs[0]?.id ?? 0
}

export function baselineSetIdAfterListLoad(
  currentSetId: number,
  sets: Array<{ id: number }> | null,
  pinnedRunId = 0,
  awaitingListSetId = 0,
): number {
  // The run response selects its owning set; a category list must not clear
  // that run while its URL/category navigation is still settling.
  if (pinnedRunId > 0) return currentSetId
  if (sets === null) return currentSetId
  if (sets.some((set) => set.id === currentSetId)) return currentSetId
  // Chosen explicitly after this list was read: a set that was just created is
  // not in it yet. Missing from a list read before the choice does not mean the
  // set is gone; the next list read decides.
  if (awaitingListSetId > 0 && awaitingListSetId === currentSetId) return currentSetId
  return sets[0]?.id ?? 0
}

export function loadedBaselineSelectionIsCurrent(
  currentSelectionId: number,
  loadedSelectionId: number | null | undefined,
): boolean {
  return currentSelectionId > 0 && loadedSelectionId === currentSelectionId
}

export function loadedBaselineSetsMatchCategory(
  categoryKey: string,
  sets: Array<{ category_key: string }> | null,
): boolean {
  return sets !== null && sets.every((set) => set.category_key === categoryKey)
}

export function baselineRouteSearch(
  categoryKey: string,
  correction?: { runId: number; itemId: number },
): Record<string, string> {
  const params = { category_key: categoryKey }
  if (!correction) return params
  return {
    ...params,
    run: String(correction.runId),
    item: String(correction.itemId),
    mode: "correction",
  }
}

/** An explicit history selection replaces the old URL pin and its item. */
export function baselineSelectedRunSearch(categoryKey: string, runId: number): Record<string, string> {
  return { category_key: categoryKey, run: String(runId) }
}

export function baselineRunContextPatch(
  currentCategoryKey: string,
  currentBaselineSetId: number,
  target: { categoryKey: string; baselineSetId: number },
): { categoryKey?: string; baselineSetId?: number } {
  if (target.categoryKey !== currentCategoryKey) {
    return { categoryKey: target.categoryKey }
  }
  if (target.baselineSetId !== currentBaselineSetId) {
    return { baselineSetId: target.baselineSetId }
  }
  return {}
}

/** A loaded, URL-pinned run is authoritative; retain correction item/mode. */
export function baselineRunRouteSearch(
  search: URLSearchParams,
  selectedRunId: number,
  run: { id: number; category_key: string } | undefined,
): URLSearchParams | null {
  if (!run || run.id !== selectedRunId || Number(search.get("run")) !== run.id
    || !run.category_key || search.get("category_key") === run.category_key) return null
  const next = new URLSearchParams(search)
  next.set("category_key", run.category_key)
  return next
}

export function baselineAcceptanceProgressFromPages(
  pages: BaselineAcceptanceRow[][],
  runTerminal = true,
): BaselineAcceptanceProgress {
  return baselineAcceptanceProgress(pages.flat(), runTerminal)
}

export function baselineAcceptanceProgress(
  rows: BaselineAcceptanceRow[],
  runTerminal = true,
): BaselineAcceptanceProgress {
  const evaluable = rows.filter((row) => row.status === "completed" && row.evaluation)
  const reviewed = evaluable.filter((row) => (
    row.evaluation?.human_review?.decision === "approved"
    || row.evaluation?.human_review?.decision === "corrected"
    || row.evaluation?.human_review?.decision === "rejected"
  )).length
  const failedOrUnscored = rows.some((row) => row.status !== "completed" || !row.evaluation)
  return {
    reviewed,
    total: evaluable.length,
    complete: runTerminal && !failedOrUnscored && evaluable.length > 0 && reviewed === evaluable.length,
  }
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/baseline-regression/baseline-run-metrics.ts =====
import type {
  BaselineLevelMetrics,
  BaselineRunMetrics,
  BaselineTruthFieldMetric,
} from "@/lib/types"

export type BaselineRunMetricsView = {
  kind: "level" | "structured_fields"
  levelMetrics: BaselineLevelMetrics | null
  fieldAggregate: BaselineTruthFieldMetric | null
  fieldMetrics: Record<string, BaselineTruthFieldMetric>
  total: number
  validPredictions: number
  failed: number
  coverage: number
}

export function baselineRunMetricsView(
  metrics: BaselineRunMetrics,
): BaselineRunMetricsView {
  if (metrics.schema_version === "baseline-truth-metrics-v1") {
    // 口径以基准集为准，不靠「有没有算出等级指标」倒推：一轮全失败时
    // level_metrics 也会是 null，但它仍然是等级回归，不能显示成字段看板。
    // 历史 run 的 metrics 是当时算好存库的、没有 truth_kind，就退一步看字段
    // 统计：一个字段都没比较过（compared=0）说明基准集本来就没有字段真值。
    const comparedFields = metrics.field_aggregate?.compared ?? 0
    const kind = metrics.truth_kind
      ?? (metrics.level_metrics || comparedFields === 0
        ? "level"
        : "structured_fields")
    return {
      kind,
      levelMetrics: metrics.level_metrics,
      fieldAggregate: kind === "level" ? null : metrics.field_aggregate,
      fieldMetrics: kind === "level" ? {} : metrics.semantic_field_metrics ?? metrics.field_metrics,
      total: metrics.total,
      validPredictions: metrics.valid_predictions,
      failed: metrics.failed,
      coverage: metrics.coverage,
    }
  }

  return {
    kind: "level",
    levelMetrics: metrics,
    fieldAggregate: null,
    fieldMetrics: {},
    total: metrics.total,
    validPredictions: metrics.valid_predictions,
    failed: metrics.failed,
    coverage: metrics.total ? metrics.valid_predictions / metrics.total : 0,
  }
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/baseline-regression/candidate-release-panels.tsx =====
import { useState } from "react"
import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query"
import { ArrowClockwise, WarningCircle } from "@phosphor-icons/react"
import { toast } from "sonner"

import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { ApiError, baselineRegressionApi } from "@/lib/api"
import type { BaselineCandidateRebasePlan, BaselineV3Revision } from "@/lib/types"

type GateRegression = {
  code?: string
  message?: string
  field_key?: string
  accuracy_delta?: number
  recall_delta?: number
  failure_sample_ids?: number[]
}

function signedPercent(value: unknown): string | null {
  if (typeof value !== "number" || Number.isNaN(value)) return null
  const percent = value * 100
  const sign = percent > 0 ? "+" : ""
  return `${sign}${percent.toFixed(1)}%`
}

/**
 * Render why the release gate refused a candidate.
 *
 * The backend already reports every blocking reason, but a bare toast throws it
 * all away and leaves the operator with "rejected" and nothing to act on.
 */
export function CandidateGateRejection({ error }: { error: ApiError | null }) {
  if (!error) return null
  const detail = error.detail ?? {}
  const code = typeof detail.code === "string" ? detail.code : undefined
  const regressions = Array.isArray(detail.regressions)
    ? (detail.regressions as GateRegression[])
    : []
  const conflicts = Array.isArray(detail.conflicts)
    ? (detail.conflicts as Array<{ path?: string; reason?: string }>)
    : []

  return (
    <div
      className="mt-3 border border-alias-edge-036 bg-alias-surface-067 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-alias-content-041 rounded-md"
      data-testid="candidate-gate-rejection"
    >
      <div className="flex items-start gap-2">
        <WarningCircle className="mt-0.5 shrink-0" />
        <div className="min-w-0">
          <div className="font-semibold">{error.message || "候选启用被拒绝"}</div>
          {code ? <div className="font-data mt-0.5 text-xs">{code}</div> : null}

          {code === "candidate_ancestry_conflict" ? (
            <div className="mt-2 text-xs leading-5">
              该候选不是现役版本的直接子代，直接启用会丢弃现役版本引入的改动。
              可用下方「变基到现役版本」生成一个挂在现役之上的等效候选，再重新回归。
            </div>
          ) : null}

          {regressions.length ? (
            <ul className="mt-2 space-y-1.5 text-xs leading-5">
              {regressions.map((item, index) => {
                const accuracy = signedPercent(item.accuracy_delta)
                const recall = signedPercent(item.recall_delta)
                return (
                  <li key={`${item.code ?? "regression"}-${item.field_key ?? index}`}>
                    <span className="font-semibold">{item.message ?? item.code}</span>
                    {accuracy || recall ? (
                      <span className="font-data ml-2">
                        {accuracy ? `准确率 ${accuracy}` : ""}
                        {accuracy && recall ? " · " : ""}
                        {recall ? `召回率 ${recall}` : ""}
                      </span>
                    ) : null}
                    {item.failure_sample_ids?.length ? (
                      <span className="ml-2">失败样本 {item.failure_sample_ids.length} 个</span>
                    ) : null}
                  </li>
                )
              })}
            </ul>
          ) : null}

          {conflicts.length ? (
            <ul className="mt-2 space-y-1 text-xs leading-5">
              {conflicts.map((item) => (
                <li key={item.path}>
                  <span className="font-data break-all">{item.path}</span>
                  <span className="ml-2">{item.reason}</span>
                </li>
              ))}
            </ul>
          ) : null}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

/**
 * Offer a rebase for a candidate that branched away from the active revision.
 *
 * Preview first: the operator sees which of their changes carry over and any
 * conflicts before anything is written.  Rebasing appends a new candidate and
 * leaves the diverged one untouched.
 */
export function CandidateRebasePanel({
  categoryKey,
  candidate,
  activeRevision,
  onRebased,
}: {
  categoryKey: string
  candidate: BaselineV3Revision
  activeRevision: BaselineV3Revision
  onRebased?: (revision: BaselineV3Revision) => void
}) {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient()
  const [expanded, setExpanded] = useState(false)

  const preview = useQuery<BaselineCandidateRebasePlan>({
    queryKey: ["candidate-rebase-preview", categoryKey, candidate.revision, activeRevision.revision],
    queryFn: () => baselineRegressionApi.previewV3Rebase(categoryKey, candidate.revision),
    enabled: expanded,
  })

  const rebase = useMutation({
    mutationFn: () => baselineRegressionApi.rebaseV3Revision(categoryKey, candidate.revision, {
      expected_projected_revision: activeRevision.revision,
      expected_projected_contract_hash: activeRevision.contract_hash,
    }),
    onSuccess: async (created) => {
      await queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["baseline-v3-revisions", categoryKey] })
      toast.success(`已生成 Revision ${created.revision}，挂在现役 Revision ${activeRevision.revision} 之上`)
      onRebased?.(created)
    },
    onError: (error) => toast.error(error.message),
  })

  const plan = preview.data
  const conflicts = plan?.conflicts ?? []
  const rebaseError = rebase.error instanceof ApiError ? rebase.error : null

  return (
    <div className="mt-3 border border-alias-edge-039 bg-alias-surface-062 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-alias-content-030 rounded-md" data-testid="candidate-rebase-panel">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-3">
        <div className="min-w-0">
          <div className="font-semibold">该候选与现役版本已分叉，无法直接启用</div>
          <div className="mt-1 text-xs leading-5">
            Revision {candidate.revision} 挂在 Revision <span className="visual-code">{candidate.parent_revision_id ?? "—"}</span> 之上，
            而现役已是 Revision {activeRevision.revision}。变基会生成一个把本候选改动重放到现役之上的新候选，
            原候选保持不变。
          </div>
        </div>
        <Button variant="secondary" size="sm" onClick={() => setExpanded((value) => !value)}>
          <ArrowClockwise />{expanded ? "收起" : "查看变基影响"}
        </Button>
      </div>

      {expanded ? (
        <div className="mt-3 border-t border-alias-edge-054 pt-3">
          {preview.isLoading ? <div className="h-16 animate-pulse bg-alias-surface-034" /> : null}
          {preview.error ? (
            <div className="text-xs text-alias-content-041">变基预览失败：{preview.error.message}</div>
          ) : null}
          {plan && !plan.needed ? (
            <div className="text-xs">{plan.reason ?? "该候选无需变基。"}</div>
          ) : null}
          {plan && plan.needed ? (
            <div className="space-y-3">
              <div className="text-xs leading-5">
                共同祖先 Revision <span className="visual-code">{plan.base_revision ?? plan.base_revision_id}</span>；
                将把本候选的 {plan.adopted_changes.length} 处改动重放到 Revision {plan.onto_revision ?? activeRevision.revision} 之上。
              </div>
              {plan.adopted_changes.length ? (
                <details className="visual-native-details text-xs">
                  <summary className="font-semibold">带过来的改动</summary>
                  <ul className="font-data mt-1.5 space-y-0.5 break-all">
                    {plan.adopted_changes.map((change) => <li key={change}>{change}</li>)}
                  </ul>
                </details>
              ) : null}
              {conflicts.length ? (
                <div className="border border-alias-edge-036 bg-alias-surface-067 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-alias-content-041 rounded-md">
                  <div className="font-semibold">存在 {conflicts.length} 处冲突，需人工决定取舍后才能变基</div>
                  <ul className="mt-1 space-y-0.5">
                    {conflicts.map((conflict) => (
                      <li key={conflict.path}>
                        <span className="font-data break-all">{conflict.path}</span>
                        <span className="ml-2">{conflict.reason}</span>
                      </li>
                    ))}
                  </ul>
                </div>
              ) : (
                <Button
                  size="sm"
                  disabled={rebase.isPending}
                  onClick={() => {
                    if (window.confirm(`确认生成变基候选？将基于现役 Revision ${activeRevision.revision} 新建一个候选，原候选保持不变。`)) {
                      rebase.mutate()
                    }
                  }}
                >
                  <ArrowClockwise />{rebase.isPending ? "正在变基" : `变基到 Revision ${activeRevision.revision}`}
                </Button>
              )}
              <CandidateGateRejection error={rebaseError} />
            </div>
          ) : null}
        </div>
      ) : null}
    </div>
  )
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/baseline-regression/correction-analysis-panel.tsx =====
import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react"
import { ArrowClockwise, Check, CheckSquare, File, Play, Square } from "@phosphor-icons/react"
import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query"
import { toast } from "sonner"

import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { ImagePreviewButton, type ImagePreview } from "@/components/image-lightbox"
import { baselineRegressionApi } from "@/lib/api"
import { useAccess } from "@/lib/access"
import type { BaselineRegressionItem, BaselineRegressionRun } from "@/lib/types"
import { candidateRefreshPlan } from "@/features/correction-contract/candidate-refresh"
import { isImageMimeType } from "@/features/baseline-regression/asset-presentation"
import { correctionLevelDisplay } from "@/features/baseline-regression/correction-level-display"
import { correctionStageClassName, correctionStageLabel, correctionStageRunningMessage, correctionStageState, correctionStageStateName, correctionStages, correctionStatusName, correctionStatusTone } from "@/features/baseline-regression/correction-stage-meta"
import { levelExplanationSummary } from "@/features/baseline-regression/level-explanation"
import { Metric, percent } from "@/features/baseline-regression/regression-page-shared"
import { baselineRunMetricsView } from "@/features/baseline-regression/baseline-run-metrics"
import { nativeWorkflowCorrectionSummary } from "@/features/baseline-regression/native-correction-summary"
import { structuredFieldLabel } from "@/features/baseline-regression/structured-truth"
import { realExecutionCanStart } from "@/features/baseline-regression/real-model-execution"

export function CorrectionAnalysisPanel({
  run,
  items,
  loading,
  onPreview,
  canDecide,
  onContinueCorrection,
}: {
  run: BaselineRegressionRun
  items: BaselineRegressionItem[]
  loading: boolean
  onPreview: (preview: ImagePreview) => void
  canDecide: boolean
  onContinueCorrection: (itemId: number) => void
}) {
  const canDevelop = useAccess().can("drafts:write")
  const queryClient = useQueryClient()
  const deviations = useMemo(
    () => items.filter((item) => (
      item.status === "completed"
      && (item.deviation || hasHumanNodeCorrection(item))
    )),
    [items],
  )
  const deviationIds = useMemo(
    () => deviations
      .filter((item) => !run.selection.workflow || hasHumanNodeCorrection(item))
      .map((item) => item.id),
    [deviations, run.selection.workflow],
  )
  const [selectedIds, setSelectedIds] = useState<Set<number>>(new Set())
  const [realExecutionConfirmed, setRealExecutionConfirmed] = useState(false)
  const [retryRealExecutionConfirmed, setRetryRealExecutionConfirmed] = useState(false)
  const sourceUsesRealModel = run.selection.model_execution_mode === "real"
  const correctionRuns = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["baseline-correction-runs", run.id],
    queryFn: () => baselineRegressionApi.listCorrectionRuns(run.id),
    refetchInterval: (query) => (
      query.state.data?.items.some((item) => item.status === "processing") ? 2500 : false
    ),
  })
  const latest = correctionRuns.data?.items[0]

  useEffect(() => {
    setSelectedIds(new Set(deviationIds))
  }, [run.id, deviationIds.join(",")])

  useEffect(() => {
    setRealExecutionConfirmed(false)
    setRetryRealExecutionConfirmed(false)
  }, [run.id])

  useEffect(() => {
    setRetryRealExecutionConfirmed(false)
  }, [latest?.id, latest?.attempt_count])

  const createCorrection = useMutation({
    mutationFn: () => baselineRegressionApi.createCorrectionRun(
      run.id,
      Array.from(selectedIds),
      `baseline-correction-${run.id}-${Date.now()}`,
      realExecutionConfirmed,
    ),
    onSuccess: async () => {
      setRealExecutionConfirmed(false)
      await queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["baseline-correction-runs", run.id] })
      toast.success("纠偏分析已启动；系统会推荐组件版本并执行一次候选回归")
    },
    onError: (error) => toast.error(error.message),
  })
  const retryCorrection = useMutation({
    mutationFn: ({
      correctionRunId,
      realExecutionConfirmed,
    }: {
      correctionRunId: number
      realExecutionConfirmed: boolean
    }) => baselineRegressionApi.retryCorrectionRun(
      correctionRunId,
      realExecutionConfirmed,
    ),
    onSuccess: async () => {
      setRetryRealExecutionConfirmed(false)
      await queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["baseline-correction-runs", run.id] })
      toast.success("已重新启动纠偏分析")
    },
    onError: (error) => toast.error(error.message),
  })
  const decideCorrection = useMutation({
    mutationFn: ({
      correctionRunId,
      decision,
      note,
    }: {
      correctionRunId: number
      decision: "approved" | "rejected"
      note: string
    }) => baselineRegressionApi.decideCorrectionRun(correctionRunId, decision, note),
    onSuccess: async (result) => {
      const refreshPlan = result.mechanism_refresh
        ? candidateRefreshPlan(result.mechanism_refresh, run.id)
        : null
      await Promise.all([
        queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["baseline-correction-runs", run.id] }),
        ...(refreshPlan?.invalidate ?? [
          ["evaluation-categories"],
          ["prompts", run.category_key],
          ["baseline-v3-revisions", run.category_key],
        ]).map((queryKey) => queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey })),
      ])
      toast.success(result.status === "approved" ? "候选结论已采纳" : "本轮候选已放弃")
    },
    onError: (error) => toast.error(error.message),
  })

  const allSelected = deviationIds.length > 0 && selectedIds.size === deviationIds.length
  const report = latest?.report ?? {}
  const runMetricsView = baselineRunMetricsView(run.metrics)
  const runPrimaryAccuracy = runMetricsView.levelMetrics?.exact_accuracy
    ?? runMetricsView.fieldAggregate?.accuracy
  const nativeSummary = latest ? nativeWorkflowCorrectionSummary(latest) : null
  const promptSuggestions = recordArray(report.prompt_suggestions)
  const dimensionSuggestions = recordArray(report.dimension_suggestions)
  const risks = stringArray(report.risks)
  const candidateRegression = recordValue(report.candidate_regression)
  const baselineMetrics = recordValue(candidateRegression.baseline_metrics)
  const candidateMetrics = recordValue(candidateRegression.candidate_metrics)
  const regressions = recordArray(candidateRegression.regressions)
  const approvalAllowed = nativeSummary?.approvalAllowed ?? candidateRegression.approval_allowed === true
  const recommendation = nativeSummary?.recommendation ?? stringValue(candidateRegression.recommendation)
  const blockers = (latest?.blockers ?? []).map((blocker) => (
    typeof blocker === "string" ? blocker : readableRecord(blocker)
  )).filter(Boolean)
