  const latestLocked = latest?.status === "processing" || latest?.status === "awaiting_decision"

  const requestDecision = (decision: "approved" | "rejected") => {
    if (!latest || latest.status !== "awaiting_decision") return
    const approved = decision === "approved"
    const confirmed = window.confirm(
      approved
        ? nativeSummary
          ? "确认采纳本轮候选结论？候选完整工作流与回归证据会保留，但不会自动切换该类目的增量评测版本。"
          : "确认采纳本轮候选结论？旧链路会按既有规则应用候选，但不会发布标签事实。"
        : "确认放弃本轮候选？本次候选与回归证据会保留，结论提交后不可修改。",
    )
    if (!confirmed) return
    decideCorrection.mutate({
      correctionRunId: latest.id,
      decision,
      note: approved ? "人工采纳自动纠偏候选结论" : "人工放弃自动纠偏候选",
    })
  }

  const continueCorrection = () => {
    if (!latest || latest.status !== "awaiting_decision") return
    const nextItemId = Array.from(selectedIds)[0] ?? deviations[0]?.id
    if (!nextItemId) return
    const confirmed = window.confirm(
      "继续逐条纠偏会结束并保留本轮候选，再打开样本纠偏页。完成新的人工纠偏后，可重新发起下一轮分析与候选回归。",
    )
    if (!confirmed) return
    decideCorrection.mutate(
      {
        correctionRunId: latest.id,
        decision: "rejected",
        note: "运营选择继续逐条纠偏，保留并结束本轮候选",
      },
      { onSuccess: () => onContinueCorrection(nextItemId) },
    )
  }

  return (
    <section
      id="baseline-correction-panel"
      className="mt-6 visual-panel border border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-white"
      role="tabpanel"
      aria-labelledby="baseline-correction-tab"
    >
      <div className="grid gap-5 border-b border-[var(--line)] px-5 py-5 lg:grid-cols-[minmax(0,1fr)_auto] lg:items-end">
        <div>
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
            <h3 className="visual-card-title">纠偏分析与版本推荐</h3>
            <Badge tone="active">分析、推荐、试跑自动完成</Badge>
            <Badge tone="neutral">最终人工决策</Badge>
          </div>
          <p className="mt-2 max-w-3xl text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">
            启动后，系统会按人工证据定位受影响的步骤，推荐新的提示词或规则模块版本，再用推荐版本自动试跑一次。最后由运营选择采纳、继续纠偏或放弃。
          </p>
          <div className="mt-3 grid gap-2 text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)] sm:grid-cols-2">
            <p className="border-l-2 border-[var(--line-strong)] pl-3">
              结果都在当前页：先逐条纠偏，再进入“纠偏分析与版本推荐”。分析报告、版本建议、试跑指标和风险提示都会在这里展示。
            </p>
            <p className="border-l-2 border-primary pl-3">
              推荐版本试跑完成后，本区域会进入“等待人工决策”。采纳只记录本轮结论，不会自动切换增量评测版本。
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
          <Button
            variant="secondary"
            onClick={() => correctionRuns.refetch()}
            disabled={correctionRuns.isFetching}
          >
            <ArrowClockwise />刷新状态
          </Button>
          <Button
            onClick={() => createCorrection.mutate()}
            disabled={
              !canDevelop || run.status === "running"
              || !selectedIds.size
              || createCorrection.isPending
              || latestLocked
              || !realExecutionCanStart(
                sourceUsesRealModel ? "real" : "simulated",
                realExecutionConfirmed,
              )
            }
          >
            <Play weight="fill" />
            {createCorrection.isPending ? "正在启动" : `启动纠偏分析 (${selectedIds.size})`}
          </Button>
        </div>
        {sourceUsesRealModel && (
          <label className="visual-native-choice rounded-md lg:col-span-2 flex items-start gap-3 border border-alias-edge-049 bg-alias-surface-077 px-4 py-3 text-xs leading-5 text-alias-content-022">
            <input
              type="checkbox"
              className="visual-native-checkbox mt-0.5 size-4"
              checked={realExecutionConfirmed}
              onChange={(event) => setRealExecutionConfirmed(event.target.checked)}
            />
            <span>
              <strong className="block text-alias-content-018">真实模型候选回归会再次产生费用</strong>
              我确认使用推荐后的候选提示词版本重新调用真实模型，并知晓所选素材会再次发送至火山方舟。
            </span>
          </label>
        )}
      </div>

      <div className="grid min-w-0 lg:grid-cols-[minmax(320px,0.88fr)_minmax(0,1.12fr)]">
        <div className="min-w-0 border-b border-[var(--line)] lg:border-r lg:border-b-0">
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 border-b border-[var(--line)] bg-alias-surface-051 px-4 py-3">
            <div>
              <p className="text-sm font-bold">选择已完成人工纠偏的样本</p>
              <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">可分析 {deviationIds.length} / 共 {deviations.length} · 已选 {selectedIds.size}</p>
            </div>
            <Button
              variant="ghost"
              size="sm"
              disabled={!deviationIds.length || latestLocked}
              onClick={() => setSelectedIds(allSelected ? new Set() : new Set(deviationIds))}
            >
              {allSelected ? <CheckSquare weight="fill" /> : <Square />}
              {allSelected ? "取消全选" : "全选偏差"}
            </Button>
          </div>
          <div className="max-h-[620px] overflow-auto">
            {loading ? (
              <div className="h-64 animate-pulse bg-white" />
            ) : deviations.length ? (
              <div className="divide-y divide-[var(--line)]">
                {deviations.map((item) => {
                  const levelDisplay = correctionLevelDisplay(item)
                  const structuredTruth = item.truth_kind === "structured_fields"
                  const analysisReady = !run.selection.workflow || hasHumanNodeCorrection(item)
                  return (
                  <div key={item.id} className="grid grid-cols-[auto_52px_minmax(0,1fr)] gap-3 px-4 py-3 hover:bg-alias-surface-051">
                    <label className="visual-native-choice shrink-0 self-start"><input
                      type="checkbox"
                      aria-label={`选择偏差样本：${item.asset.name}`}
                      className="visual-native-checkbox mt-4 size-4"
                      checked={selectedIds.has(item.id)}
                      disabled={latestLocked || !analysisReady}
                      onChange={(event) => setSelectedIds((current) => {
                        const next = new Set(current)
                        if (event.target.checked) next.add(item.id)
                        else next.delete(item.id)
                        return next
                      })}
                    /></label>
                    {isImageMimeType(item.asset.mime_type) ? (
                      <ImagePreviewButton
                        src={item.image_url}
                        alt={item.asset.name}
                        imageClassName="size-12"
                        onPreview={onPreview}
                      />
                    ) : (
                      <div className="flex size-12 items-center justify-center border border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-alias-surface-029 text-[var(--muted)] rounded-md"><File size={21} /></div>
                    )}
                    <div className="min-w-0">
                      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                        <p className="file-name min-w-0 truncate text-sm">{item.asset.name}</p>
                        {structuredTruth ? (
                          <><Badge tone={item.deviation ? "danger" : "warning"}>{item.deviation ? `${item.field_deviations?.length ?? 0} 个字段有偏差` : "已保存人工纠偏"}</Badge><Badge tone="neutral">结构化字段真值</Badge></>
                        ) : (
                          <><Badge tone="danger">{levelDisplay.level ?? "—"} → {item.predicted_level ?? "—"}</Badge><Badge tone={levelDisplay.source === "human_correction" ? "warning" : "neutral"}>{levelDisplay.source === "human_correction" ? "人工纠偏等级" : "冻结预期等级"}</Badge></>
                        )}
                      </div>
                      {!structuredTruth && levelDisplay.source === "human_correction" && (
                        <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-4 text-alias-content-033">
                          原冻结预期 {item.expected_level} · 当前展示以已完成人工纠偏为准
                        </p>
                      )}
                      {!structuredTruth && levelDisplay.source === "frozen_expected" && (
                        <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-4 text-[var(--muted)]">
                          尚未保存人工纠偏等级 · 当前仅用于筛选偏差样本
                        </p>
                      )}
                      <p className="mt-1 line-clamp-2 text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">{structuredTruth ? `待确认字段：${(item.field_deviations ?? []).map(structuredFieldLabel).join("、") || "请查看节点明细"}` : levelExplanationSummary(item)}</p>
                      {!analysisReady && (
                        <Button className="mt-2" size="sm" variant="ghost" onClick={() => onContinueCorrection(item.id)}>
                          先逐条纠偏
                        </Button>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  )
                })}
              </div>
            ) : (
              <p className="px-5 py-12 text-center text-sm text-[var(--muted)]">
                当前运行没有偏差样本或已保存的人工纠偏证据。
              </p>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="min-w-0 px-5 py-5">
          {!latest ? (
            <div className="border-y border-[var(--line)] px-4 py-12 text-center">
              <p className="text-sm font-bold">尚未启动纠偏分析</p>
              <p className="mt-2 text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">选择左侧偏差样本后启动，系统会完成节点分析、组件版本推荐和一次候选回归，最后等待人工决策。</p>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <>
              <div className="flex flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-4 border-b border-[var(--line-strong)] pb-4">
                <div>
                  <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                    <h4 className="text-base font-bold">纠偏分析 #<span className="visual-code">{latest.id}</span></h4>
                    <Badge tone={correctionStatusTone(latest)}>{correctionStatusName(latest)}</Badge>
                    <Badge>第 {latest.attempt_count} 次尝试</Badge>
                  </div>
                  <p className="font-data mt-2 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">
                    {latest.selected_item_ids.length} 个冻结样本 · {latest.updated_at}
                  </p>
                </div>
                {latest.status === "failed" && latest.error?.retryable && (
                  <div className="flex max-w-md flex-col items-end gap-2">
                    {sourceUsesRealModel && (
                      <label className="visual-native-choice rounded-md flex items-start gap-2 border border-alias-edge-049 bg-alias-surface-077 px-3 py-2 text-left text-xs leading-4 text-alias-content-022">
                        <input
                          type="checkbox"
                          className="visual-native-checkbox mt-0.5 size-4 shrink-0"
                          checked={retryRealExecutionConfirmed}
                          onChange={(event) => setRetryRealExecutionConfirmed(event.target.checked)}
                        />
                        <span>
                          <strong className="block">本次重新执行会再次产生费用</strong>
                          我确认再次调用真实模型，并知晓冻结素材会再次发送至火山方舟。
                        </span>
                      </label>
                    )}
                    <Button
                      variant="secondary"
                      size="sm"
                      disabled={
                        !canDevelop || retryCorrection.isPending
                        || (sourceUsesRealModel && !retryRealExecutionConfirmed)
                      }
                      onClick={() => retryCorrection.mutate({
                        correctionRunId: latest.id,
                        realExecutionConfirmed: retryRealExecutionConfirmed,
                      })}
                    >
                      <ArrowClockwise />{retryCorrection.isPending ? "正在重新执行" : "重新执行"}
                    </Button>
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>

              <div className="mt-4 grid grid-cols-2 border-y border-[var(--line)] bg-alias-surface-051 2xl:grid-cols-5">
                {correctionStages.map((stage, index) => {
                  const state = correctionStageState(latest, stage.key)
                  return (
                    <div
                      key={stage.key}
                      className="min-w-0 border-r border-[var(--line)] px-3 py-3 last:border-r-0"
                    >
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                        <span className={`flex size-6 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-md border text-xs font-bold ${correctionStageClassName(state)}`}>
                          {state === "completed" ? <Check weight="bold" /> : index + 1}
                        </span>
                        <p className="text-xs font-bold leading-4">{stage.label}</p>
                      </div>
                      <p className="mt-1 pl-8 text-xs leading-4 text-[var(--muted)]">
                        {correctionStageStateName(state)}
                      </p>
                    </div>
                  )
                })}
              </div>

              {latest.status === "processing" && (
                <div className="mt-4 border border-alias-edge-035 bg-alias-surface-044 px-4 py-3 rounded-md">
                  <div className="flex justify-between gap-4 text-xs font-bold">
                    <span>{correctionStageRunningMessage(latest.stage)}</span>
                    <span className="font-data">{latest.progress}%</span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="mt-2 h-1.5 overflow-hidden bg-white">
                    <div className="h-full w-full origin-left bg-brand transition-transform duration-[var(--visual-ease-out-ms)] ease-[var(--visual-ease-out)]" style={{ transform: `scaleX(${latest.progress / 100})` }} />
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}

              {latest.status === "failed" && (
                <div className="mt-4 border border-alias-edge-048 bg-alias-surface-066 px-4 py-3 text-xs leading-5 text-alias-content-041 rounded-md">
                  <p className="font-bold">{correctionStageLabel(latest.stage)}失败{latest.error?.code ? ` · ${latest.error.code}` : ""}</p>
                  <p className="mt-1">{latest.error?.message || "未返回具体失败原因，可重新执行本次冻结样本。"}</p>
                  <p className="mt-1 text-alias-content-024">
                    重新执行会沿用本次冻结样本；真实模型重试仍需再次确认费用和素材外发。
                  </p>
                </div>
              )}

              {(blockers.length > 0) && (
                <div className="mt-4 border border-alias-edge-049 bg-alias-surface-077 px-4 py-3 text-xs leading-5 text-alias-content-033 rounded-md">
                  <p className="font-bold">自动处理提示</p>
                  {blockers.map((blocker, index) => <p key={`${blocker}-${index}`} className="mt-1">{blocker}</p>)}
                </div>
              )}

              {(latest.status === "awaiting_decision"
                || latest.status === "approved"
                || latest.status === "rejected") && (
                <>
                  <div className="visual-panel-group mt-5 grid gap-px visual-panel border border-[var(--line)] bg-[var(--line)] sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-4">
                    <Metric label={nativeSummary?.accuracyKind === "field" ? "基准字段准确率" : nativeSummary?.accuracyKind === "level" ? "基准等级准确率" : "基准准确率"} value={formatPercent(nativeSummary?.baselineAccuracy ?? numberValue(baselineMetrics.exact_accuracy) ?? runPrimaryAccuracy ?? null)} />
                    <Metric label={nativeSummary?.accuracyKind === "field" ? "候选字段准确率" : nativeSummary?.accuracyKind === "level" ? "候选等级准确率" : "候选准确率"} value={formatPercent(nativeSummary?.candidateAccuracy ?? numberValue(candidateMetrics.exact_accuracy))} />
                    <Metric label="准确率变化" value={formatSignedPercent(nativeSummary?.accuracyDelta ?? numberValue(candidateRegression.exact_accuracy_delta))} />
                    <Metric label={nativeSummary ? "增量版本" : "相邻准确率变化"} value={nativeSummary ? "保持不变" : formatSignedPercent(numberValue(candidateRegression.adjacent_accuracy_delta))} />
                  </div>
                  <div className="visual-panel-group mt-5 grid gap-px visual-panel border border-[var(--line)] bg-[var(--line)] sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-4">
                    <ReportFact label={nativeSummary ? "原完整工作流" : "机制候选"} value={nativeSummary?.baselineWorkflowVersionId ? `Workflow #${nativeSummary.baselineWorkflowVersionId}` : latest.candidate_revision_id ? `Revision #${latest.candidate_revision_id}` : "—"} />
                    <ReportFact label={nativeSummary ? "候选完整工作流" : "候选提示词"} value={nativeSummary?.candidateWorkflowVersionId ? `Workflow #${nativeSummary.candidateWorkflowVersionId}` : latest.orchestration.candidate_prompt?.version ?? "—"} />
                    <ReportFact label="候选回归" value={latest.regression_run_id ? `Run #${latest.regression_run_id}` : "—"} />
                    <ReportFact label="回归建议" value={recommendation === "approve" ? "建议采纳" : "建议放弃"} />
                  </div>
                  {nativeSummary ? (
                    <div className="mt-6 space-y-5">
                      <section>
                        <h5 className="visual-item-title">受影响节点</h5>
                        <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">只根据本轮已保存的人工纠偏证据定位，不会修改未受影响路线。</p>
                        <div className="mt-3 divide-y divide-[var(--line)] border-y border-[var(--line)]">
                          {nativeSummary.affectedNodes.map((node) => (
                            <div key={node.nodeInstanceId} className="grid gap-2 py-3 text-xs sm:grid-cols-[minmax(150px,0.7fr)_minmax(0,1fr)]">
                              <span className="font-bold"><span className="visual-code">{node.nodeInstanceId}</span></span>
                              <span className="leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">{node.componentKind === "prompt" ? "提示词节点" : "规则模块节点"} · {node.assetKey || "未记录调用标识"} · {node.fields.length ? `纠偏字段：${node.fields.map(structuredFieldLabel).join("、")}` : "按节点证据分析"} · {node.evidenceCount} 条证据</span>
                            </div>
                          ))}
                        </div>
                      </section>
                      <section>
                        <h5 className="visual-item-title">组件版本建议</h5>
                        <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">提示词与规则模块一视同仁，按节点选择新版本，再组成候选完整工作流。</p>
                        <div className="mt-3 divide-y divide-[var(--line)] border-y border-[var(--line)]">
                          {nativeSummary.recommendations.map((item) => (
                            <div key={`${item.nodeInstanceId}-${item.candidateVersionId}`} className="grid gap-2 py-3 text-xs sm:grid-cols-[minmax(150px,0.7fr)_minmax(0,1fr)]">
                              <span className="font-bold"><span className="visual-code">{item.nodeInstanceId}</span></span>
                              <span className="leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">{item.assetKey || "组件"} · 版本 #<span className="visual-code">{item.sourceVersionId}</span> → 推荐版本 #<span className="visual-code">{item.candidateVersionId}</span>{item.mode === "generated" ? "（根据人工纠偏生成）" : "（复用已有独立版本）"}</span>
                            </div>
                          ))}
                        </div>
                      </section>
                    </div>
                  ) : (
                  <div className="mt-6">
                    <h5 className="visual-item-title">分析与候选变更摘要</h5>
                    <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">系统已把以下分析落入统一机制候选，并使用同一基准集完成验证。</p>
                    <div className="mt-3 divide-y divide-[var(--line)] border-y border-[var(--line)]">
                      {[...promptSuggestions, ...dimensionSuggestions].map((suggestion, index) => (
                        <div key={`${stringValue(suggestion.code) || stringValue(suggestion.dimension_key) || "suggestion"}-${index}`} className="grid gap-2 py-3 text-xs sm:grid-cols-[120px_minmax(0,1fr)]">
                          <span className="font-bold">
                            {stringValue(suggestion.dimension_key) || "提示词建议"}
                            {stringValue(suggestion.priority) ? ` · ${priorityName(stringValue(suggestion.priority))}` : ""}
                          </span>
                          <span className="leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">{stringValue(suggestion.message) || readableRecord(suggestion)}</span>
                        </div>
                      ))}
                      {!promptSuggestions.length && !dimensionSuggestions.length && (
                        <p className="py-5 text-center text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">本次未形成可展示的改进建议。</p>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  )}
                  {risks.length > 0 && (
                    <div className="mt-5 border border-alias-edge-049 bg-alias-surface-077 px-4 py-3 text-xs leading-5 text-alias-content-033 rounded-md">
                      <p className="font-bold">报告风险提示</p>
                      {risks.map((risk) => <p key={risk} className="mt-1">{risk}</p>)}
                    </div>
                  )}
                  {regressions.length > 0 && (
                    <div className="mt-5 border border-alias-edge-048 bg-alias-surface-066 px-4 py-3 text-xs leading-5 text-alias-content-041 rounded-md">
                      <p className="font-bold">候选回归未通过</p>
                      {regressions.map((regression, index) => (
                        <p key={`${stringValue(regression.code)}-${index}`} className="mt-1">
                          {stringValue(regression.message) || readableRecord(regression)}
                        </p>
                      ))}
                    </div>
                  )}
                  {latest.status === "awaiting_decision" && canDecide && (
                    <div className="mt-5 border-l-2 border-primary bg-alias-surface-048 px-4 py-4">
                      <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-5">
                        <div>
                          <p className="text-sm font-bold">等待人工决策</p>
                          <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">
                            中间步骤已全部完成。采纳会保留候选完整工作流作为人工认可结论，但不会自动切换增量评测版本；正式增量仍需回到工作流管理器单独设置。
                          </p>
                        </div>
                        <div className="flex shrink-0 flex-wrap justify-end gap-2">
                          <Button
                            variant="danger"
                            disabled={decideCorrection.isPending}
                            onClick={() => requestDecision("rejected")}
                          >
                            放弃候选
                          </Button>
                          <Button
                            variant="secondary"
                            disabled={decideCorrection.isPending}
                            onClick={continueCorrection}
                          >
                            继续逐条纠偏
                          </Button>
                          <Button
                            disabled={!approvalAllowed || decideCorrection.isPending}
                            title={approvalAllowed ? undefined : "候选回归未通过，不能采纳"}
                            onClick={() => requestDecision("approved")}
                          >
                            <Check weight="bold" />采纳候选结论
                          </Button>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  )}
                  {latest.status === "awaiting_decision" && !canDecide && (
                    <div className="mt-5 border-l-2 border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-alias-surface-051 px-4 py-4">
                      <p className="text-sm font-bold">等待系统管理员决策</p>
                      <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">
                        候选与回归证据已就绪。只有系统管理员可以采纳或放弃候选；运营仍可继续逐条纠偏。
                      </p>
                    </div>
                  )}
                  {(latest.status === "approved" || latest.status === "rejected") && (
                    <div className={`mt-5 border-l-2 px-4 py-4 ${latest.status === "approved" ? "border-primary bg-alias-surface-048" : "border-danger bg-alias-surface-066"}`}>
                      <p className="text-sm font-bold">
                        人工结论：{latest.status === "approved" ? "已采纳候选结论" : "已放弃候选"}
                      </p>
                      <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">
                        {latest.decided_by || "管理员"} · {latest.decided_at || latest.updated_at}
                        {latest.decision_note ? ` · ${latest.decision_note}` : ""}
                      </p>
                      <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">该人工结论不可修改，候选、回归与决策证据均已保留。</p>
                    </div>
                  )}
                </>
              )}
            </>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
  )
}

export function recordValue(value: unknown): Record<string, unknown> {
  return value && typeof value === "object" && !Array.isArray(value)
    ? value as Record<string, unknown>
    : {}
}

function hasHumanNodeCorrection(item: BaselineRegressionItem) {
  return (item.evaluation?.correction_history ?? []).some((event) => {
    const metadata = event as unknown as {
      corrector_confidence?: unknown
      corrector_policy?: unknown
    }
    return metadata.corrector_confidence == null && !metadata.corrector_policy
  })
}

export function recordArray(value: unknown): Array<Record<string, unknown>> {
  return Array.isArray(value) ? value.map(recordValue).filter((item) => Object.keys(item).length) : []
}

export function stringArray(value: unknown): string[] {
  return Array.isArray(value) ? value.filter((item): item is string => typeof item === "string") : []
}

export function stringValue(value: unknown): string {
  return typeof value === "string" ? value : ""
}

export function numberValue(value: unknown): number | null {
  return typeof value === "number" && Number.isFinite(value) ? value : null
}

export function readableRecord(value: unknown): string {
  const record = recordValue(value)
  return stringValue(record.message) || stringValue(record.title) || stringValue(record.code)
}

export function formatSignedPercent(value: number | null) {
  if (value === null) return "—"
  const sign = value > 0 ? "+" : ""
  return `${sign}${(value * 100).toFixed(1)}%`
}

export function formatPercent(value: number | null) {
  return value === null ? "—" : percent(value)
}

export function priorityName(priority: string) {
  if (priority === "high") return "高优先"
  if (priority === "medium") return "中优先"
  if (priority === "low") return "低优先"
  return priority
}

export function ReportFact({ label, value }: { label: string; value: string }) {
  return (
    <div className="border-y border-[var(--line)] px-4 py-3">
      <p className="text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">{label}</p>
      <p className="font-data mt-1 text-lg font-bold">{value}</p>
    </div>
  )
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/baseline-regression/correction-autosave.ts =====
import { createIdempotencyToken } from "../../lib/idempotency-token.ts"
import { correctionDraftFromView, correctionNodeChanged, correctionSubmissionPayload, updateCorrectionDraft } from "../correction-contract/correction-view-state.ts"
import type { CorrectionDraft, CorrectionSubmissionRequest, CorrectionView } from "../correction-contract/types.ts"

type PendingEdit = { key: string; value: unknown; payload?: CorrectionSubmissionRequest }
const same = (a: unknown, b: unknown) => JSON.stringify(a) === JSON.stringify(b)

/** One serial queue per material; retries retain the original idempotency key. */
export class CorrectionAutosave {
  view: CorrectionView
  draft: CorrectionDraft
  queue: PendingEdit[] = []
  busy = false
  error = ""
  failedStatus = 0
  saved = new Set<string>()
  private timers = new Map<string, ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>>()
  private composing = new Set<string>()
  private notify: () => void
  private send: (payload: CorrectionSubmissionRequest) => Promise<CorrectionView>
  private accept: (view: CorrectionView) => void
  private confirmRerun: () => boolean

  constructor(view: CorrectionView, options: {
    notify: () => void; send: (payload: CorrectionSubmissionRequest) => Promise<CorrectionView>
    accept: (view: CorrectionView) => void; confirmRerun: () => boolean
  }) {
    this.view = view
    this.draft = correctionDraftFromView(view)
    this.notify = options.notify; this.send = options.send; this.accept = options.accept
    this.confirmRerun = options.confirmRerun
  }

  get dirty() { return this.busy || this.queue.length > 0 || Object.values(this.draft).some(node => node.dirty) }

  edit(key: string, value: unknown) {
    this.draft = updateCorrectionDraft(this.draft, key, { value })
    this.saved.delete(key)
    this.notify()
  }

  schedule(key: string, delay = 800) {
    const timer = this.timers.get(key)
    if (timer) clearTimeout(timer)
    this.timers.delete(key)
    if (!this.composing.has(key)) this.timers.set(key, setTimeout(() => this.commit(key), delay))
  }

  composition(key: string, active: boolean) {
    if (active) this.composing.add(key)
    else this.composing.delete(key)
    this.schedule(key)
  }

  cancelScheduled() {
    this.timers.forEach(timer => clearTimeout(timer))
    this.timers.clear()
  }

  commit(key: string) {
    const timer = this.timers.get(key)
    if (timer) clearTimeout(timer)
    this.timers.delete(key)
    if (this.composing.has(key)) return
    const node = this.view.nodes.find(node => node.node_key === key)
    const edited = this.draft[key]
    if (!node || !edited?.dirty) return
    const last = this.queue.filter(task => task.key === key).at(-1)
    if (last && same(last.value, edited.value)) return
    if (!last && !correctionNodeChanged(node, edited)) {
      this.draft[key] = { ...edited, dirty: false }; this.notify(); return
    }
    // A rejected input did not commit: replace it with the user's corrected input.
    if (this.error && this.failedStatus === 422 && this.queue[0]?.key === key) {
      this.queue.shift(); this.error = ""; this.failedStatus = 0
    }
    this.queue.push({ key, value: structuredClone(edited.value) })
    this.notify()
    void this.flush()
  }

  retry() { this.error = ""; this.failedStatus = 0; void this.flush() }

  /** Explicit conflict resolution only; preserve edits while taking a fresh revision. */
  refresh(view: CorrectionView) {
    this.view = view
    for (const task of this.queue) delete task.payload
    this.retry()
  }

  private async flush() {
    if (this.busy || this.error || !this.queue.length) return
    this.busy = true; this.notify()
    try {
      while (this.queue.length) {
        const task = this.queue[0]
        if (!task.payload) {
          const node = this.view.nodes.find(node => node.node_key === task.key)
          if (!node || this.view.read_only) throw new Error("字段当前不可编辑，请刷新核对")
          const paid = node.metadata?.paid_rerun_required === true
          if (paid && !this.confirmRerun()) {
            this.queue.shift()
            this.draft[task.key] = correctionDraftFromView(this.view)[task.key]
            continue
          }
          const reason = "运营在表格中手动修正字段（未补充人工备注）"
          const change = updateCorrectionDraft(correctionDraftFromView(this.view), task.key, {
            value: task.value, reason,
            evidence: [{ source: "operator_table_edit", text: "人工直接填写的字段修正值", field: task.key, value: task.value }],
          })
          task.payload = correctionSubmissionPayload(change, this.view,
            `table:${this.view.run_id}:${this.view.item_id}:${createIdempotencyToken()}`, { realExecutionConfirmed: paid })
        }
        const response = await this.send(task.payload)
        this.view = response
        const fresh = correctionDraftFromView(response)
        for (const [key, edited] of Object.entries(this.draft)) {
          if (edited.dirty && (key !== task.key || !same(edited.value, task.value))) fresh[key] = edited
        }
        this.draft = fresh
        this.queue.shift()
        this.saved.add(task.key)
        this.accept(response)
        this.notify()
      }
    } catch (error) {
      this.error = error instanceof Error ? error.message : "保存失败，请重试"
      this.failedStatus = Number((error as { status?: number })?.status ?? 0)
    } finally {
      this.busy = false; this.notify()
    }
  }
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/baseline-regression/correction-cache.ts =====
import type { QueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query"
import type { BaselineRegressionDetail, BaselineRegressionItem } from "../../lib/types"

/** Refresh the saved material, preserving every unrelated row and page. */
export async function refreshCorrectionItem(
  client: QueryClient, runId: number, itemId: number, revision: number,
  fetchItem: () => Promise<BaselineRegressionItem>,
) {
  const item = await fetchItem()
  if (item.id !== itemId || (item.evaluation?.review_revision ?? 0) < revision) return
  const replace = (rows: BaselineRegressionItem[]) => rows.map(previous =>
    previous.id === itemId && (previous.evaluation?.review_revision ?? 0) <= (item.evaluation?.review_revision ?? 0)
      ? item : previous)
  client.setQueriesData<BaselineRegressionDetail>({
    queryKey: ["baseline-regression", runId],
    predicate: query => typeof query.queryKey[2] === "number",
  }, old => old ? { ...old, items: replace(old.items) } : old)
  client.setQueriesData<BaselineRegressionItem[][]>({ queryKey: ["baseline-acceptance", runId] },
    old => old?.map(replace))
  void client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["baseline-acceptance-summary", runId] })
  // Full evidence remains a distinct payload and is fetched when reopened.
  await client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["baseline-regression", runId, "item", itemId], refetchType: "none" })
  await client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["baseline-correction-view", runId, itemId], exact: true, refetchType: "none" })
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/baseline-regression/correction-draft-store.ts =====
import type { CorrectionAutosave } from "./correction-autosave"

type DraftEntry = {
  editor: CorrectionAutosave | null
  note: string
  noteBase: string
  noteOpen: boolean
  noteRequest: { text: string; idempotency_key: string } | null
  noteBusy: boolean
  active: boolean
  listeners: Set<() => void>
}
const drafts = new Map<string, DraftEntry>()
let account: number | null = null

export function correctionDraftEntry(runId: number, itemId: number): DraftEntry {
  const key = `${runId}:${itemId}`
  let entry = drafts.get(key)
  if (!entry) {
    entry = { editor: null, note: "", noteBase: "", noteOpen: false, noteRequest: null, noteBusy: false, active: true, listeners: new Set() }
    drafts.set(key, entry)
  }
  return entry
}
export function correctionNoteDirty(entry: Pick<DraftEntry, "note" | "noteBase">) {
  return entry.note.trim() !== entry.noteBase.trim()
}
export function openCorrectionNote(entry: DraftEntry, notes: { id: number; text: string }[]) {
  if (!entry.noteOpen && !correctionNoteDirty(entry)) {
    entry.noteBase = notes.reduce((latest, note) => note.id > latest.id ? note : latest, { id: -1, text: "" }).text
    entry.note = entry.noteBase
  }
  entry.noteOpen = true
  entry.listeners.forEach(notify => notify())
}
export function resetCorrectionNote(entry: DraftEntry) {
  entry.note = ""; entry.noteBase = ""; entry.noteOpen = false; entry.noteRequest = null
  entry.listeners.forEach(notify => notify())
}
export function hasUnsavedCorrectionDrafts() {
  return [...drafts.values()].some(entry => entry.editor?.dirty || correctionNoteDirty(entry))
}
export function correctionDraftsBusy() {
  return [...drafts.values()].some(entry => entry.editor?.busy || entry.noteBusy)
}
export function scopeCorrectionDrafts(userId: number | null) {
  if (account === userId) return
  for (const entry of drafts.values()) { entry.active = false; entry.editor?.cancelScheduled() }
  drafts.clear()
  account = userId
}
export function releaseCleanDraft(runId: number, itemId: number) {
  const key = `${runId}:${itemId}`
  const entry = drafts.get(key)
  // StrictMode replays mount effects. Only release after an actual unmount,
  // when no subscriber reattached and no in-flight note can still update it.
  queueMicrotask(() => {
    if (entry && drafts.get(key) === entry && !entry.listeners.size && !entry.editor?.dirty && !correctionNoteDirty(entry) && !entry.noteBusy) drafts.delete(key)
  })
}
// Keep the guard alive while another page is open. Draft values are confined
// to this signed-in tab; no credentials or correction evidence go into storage.
if (typeof window !== "undefined") window.addEventListener("beforeunload", event => {
  if (hasUnsavedCorrectionDrafts()) { event.preventDefault(); event.returnValue = "" }
})

===== FILE frontend/src/features/baseline-regression/correction-level-display.ts =====
import type { BaselineLevel, BaselineRegressionItem } from "@/lib/types"
import type { NodeCorrectionHistoryItem } from "@/lib/node-correction"

const BASELINE_LEVELS = new Set<BaselineLevel>(["L1", "L2", "L3", "L4", "L5"])

function isBaselineLevel(value: string | null | undefined): value is BaselineLevel {
  return typeof value === "string" && BASELINE_LEVELS.has(value as BaselineLevel)
}

export type CorrectionLevelDisplay = {
  level: BaselineLevel | null
  source: "human_correction" | "frozen_expected"
}

function isHumanNodeCorrection(event: NodeCorrectionHistoryItem): boolean {
  const metadata = event as typeof event & {
    corrector_confidence?: unknown
    corrector_policy?: unknown
  }
  return metadata.corrector_confidence == null && !metadata.corrector_policy
}

export function humanNodeCorrectionCount(
  item: Pick<BaselineRegressionItem, "evaluation">,
): number {
  return (item.evaluation?.correction_history ?? []).filter(isHumanNodeCorrection).length
}

function latestHumanCorrectedLevel(
  item: Pick<BaselineRegressionItem, "evaluation">,
): BaselineLevel | null {
  const history = item.evaluation?.correction_history ?? []
  for (let index = history.length - 1; index >= 0; index -= 1) {
    const event = history[index]
    if (!event || !isHumanNodeCorrection(event)) continue
    const finalLevelNode = (
      event.node_type === "final_level"
      || (
        event.module_type_key === "score_level_matcher"
        && event.module_field_key === "level"
      )
    )
    if (finalLevelNode && isBaselineLevel(event.new_value as string | null | undefined)) {
      return event.new_value as BaselineLevel
    }
  }
  return null
}

/**
 * The frozen expected level remains the regression truth. This helper only
 * chooses which level the correction-analysis UI should show as its human
 * reference after a completed manual correction.
 */
export function correctionLevelDisplay(
  item: Pick<BaselineRegressionItem, "expected_level" | "evaluation">,
): CorrectionLevelDisplay {
  const evaluation = item.evaluation
  const humanReview = evaluation?.human_review
  const nativeHumanLevel = latestHumanCorrectedLevel(item)
  if (nativeHumanLevel) {
    return { level: nativeHumanLevel, source: "human_correction" }
  }
  const humanLevel = humanReview?.corrected_level
  if (
    isBaselineLevel(humanLevel)
    && evaluation?.review_truth_status === "completed"
    && humanReview?.decision === "corrected"
  ) {
    return { level: humanLevel, source: "human_correction" }
  }
  return { level: item.expected_level, source: "frozen_expected" }
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/baseline-regression/correction-navigation.ts =====
export type CorrectionNavigationItem = {
  id: number
  status: "queued" | "completed" | "failed"
  evaluation: {
    review_stage: string | null
  } | null
}

/** Keep only rows that have a finished model result and can be opened in correction. */
export function correctionQueueItems<T extends CorrectionNavigationItem>(
  items: readonly T[],
): T[] {
  return items.filter((item) => item.status === "completed" && item.evaluation)
}

/** Return the next item after current that still needs an initial decision. */
export function nextPendingCorrectionId(
  items: readonly CorrectionNavigationItem[],
  currentId: number,
): number | null {
  const currentIndex = items.findIndex((item) => item.id === currentId)
  const candidates = currentIndex >= 0 ? items.slice(currentIndex + 1) : items
  return candidates.find((item) => (
    item.status === "completed"
    && item.evaluation
    && item.evaluation.review_stage !== "completed"
  ))?.id ?? null
}

/** Return the immediately preceding item in the current correction list. */
export function previousCorrectionId(
  items: readonly CorrectionNavigationItem[],
  currentId: number,
): number | null {
  const currentIndex = items.findIndex((item) => item.id === currentId)
  if (currentIndex <= 0) return null
  return items[currentIndex - 1]?.id ?? null
}

/**
 * Save a dirty node draft before approving the item, and navigate only after
 * both writes succeed. Injected callbacks keep failure ordering testable.
 */
export async function runStructuredCorrectionApproval<Saved>({
  hasDirtyDraft,
  saveDraft,
  submitReview,
  navigateNext,
}: {
  hasDirtyDraft: boolean
  saveDraft: () => Promise<Saved>
  submitReview: (saved: Saved | null) => Promise<void>
  navigateNext?: () => void
}): Promise<void> {
  const saved = hasDirtyDraft ? await saveDraft() : null
  await submitReview(saved)
  navigateNext?.()
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/baseline-regression/correction-pagination.tsx =====
import { useEffect, useId, useState } from "react"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input"
import { pageRange } from "@/lib/paging"

export function CorrectionPagination({ page, pageSize, total, loading, onPageChange, onPageSizeChange }: {
  page: number; pageSize: number; total: number; loading: boolean
  onPageChange: (page: number) => void
  onPageSizeChange: (size: number) => void
}) {
  const id = useId()
  const pageCount = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(total / pageSize))
  const [jump, setJump] = useState(String(page + 1))
  useEffect(() => setJump(String(page + 1)), [page])
  const range = pageRange({ total, offset: page * pageSize, limit: pageSize })
  const pages = [...new Set([0, page - 1, page, page + 1, pageCount - 1])]
    .filter(value => value >= 0 && value < pageCount).sort((a, b) => a - b)
  return <nav aria-label="纠偏分页" className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 border-y border-[var(--line)] bg-white px-3 py-3">
    <p className="text-xs text-[var(--muted)]" aria-live="polite">
      {loading ? "正在加载素材…" : `第 ${range.from}–${range.to} 张 / 共 ${total} 张`} · 第 {page + 1}/{pageCount} 页
    </p>
    <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
      <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs">每页
        <select aria-label="每页素材数" value={pageSize} disabled={loading}
          className="visual-native-select" onChange={event => onPageSizeChange(Number(event.target.value))}>
          {[10, 25, 50].map(size => <option key={size} value={size}>{size} 张</option>)}
        </select>
      </label>
      <Button size="sm" variant="secondary" disabled={loading || page <= 0} onClick={() => onPageChange(page - 1)}>上一页</Button>
      {pages.map((value, index) => <span key={value} className="inline-flex items-center gap-2">
        {index > 0 && value > pages[index - 1] + 1 && <span aria-hidden="true" className="text-[var(--muted)]">…</span>}
        <Button size="sm" variant={value === page ? "primary" : "secondary"} aria-label={`第 ${value + 1} 页`}
          aria-current={value === page ? "page" : undefined} disabled={loading || value === page}
          onClick={() => onPageChange(value)}>{value + 1}</Button>
      </span>)}
      <Button size="sm" variant="secondary" disabled={loading || page + 1 >= pageCount} onClick={() => onPageChange(page + 1)}>下一页</Button>
      <form className="flex items-center gap-2" onSubmit={event => {
        event.preventDefault()
        const target = Number(jump)
        if (!loading && Number.isInteger(target) && target >= 1 && target <= pageCount) onPageChange(target - 1)
      }}>
        <label htmlFor={id} className="text-xs">跳至</label>
        <Input id={id} aria-label="跳至页码" type="number" min={1} max={pageCount} step={1} required
          value={jump} onChange={event => setJump(event.target.value)} disabled={loading} className="w-20"/>
        <Button type="submit" size="sm" variant="secondary" disabled={loading || total === 0}>跳转</Button>
      </form>
    </div>
  </nav>
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/baseline-regression/correction-presentation.ts =====
import { scopeFromIsSingle } from "./kg-route-codes.ts"

export type CorrectionDetailMode = "aesthetic_level" | "structured_fields"

export type CorrectionWorkspaceLayout = {
  panelOrder: Array<"queue" | "context" | "correction">
  queueWeight: number
  contextWeight: number
  correctionWeight: number
  gridTemplateColumns: string
}

export type CorrectionQueueState = {
  key: "completed" | "different" | "pending" | "ready"
  label: "已完成" | "有差异" | "待填写" | "待确认"
  tone: "success" | "danger" | "neutral" | "warning"
}

export type BaselineWorkspaceLayout = {
  gridClassName: string
  showBaselineSetSidebar: boolean
}

export function baselineWorkspaceLayout(
  correctionItemId: number,
): BaselineWorkspaceLayout {
  if (correctionItemId > 0) {
    return {
      gridClassName: "mx-auto grid max-w-[1920px] grid-cols-1",
      showBaselineSetSidebar: false,
    }
  }
  return {
    gridClassName: "mx-auto grid max-w-[1720px] lg:grid-cols-[310px_minmax(0,1fr)]",
    showBaselineSetSidebar: true,
  }
}

export type KnowledgeGraphTruthRoute = {
  source: "国内" | "海外"
  scope: "单体" | "整体"
  label: string
}

export function correctionDetailMode(
  truthKind: "level" | "structured_fields",
): CorrectionDetailMode {
  return truthKind === "structured_fields" ? "structured_fields" : "aesthetic_level"
}

export function correctionWorkspaceLayout(
  mode: CorrectionDetailMode,
): CorrectionWorkspaceLayout {
  if (mode === "structured_fields") {
    return {
      panelOrder: ["queue", "context", "correction"],
      queueWeight: 0.45,
      contextWeight: 0.95,
      correctionWeight: 1.3,
      gridTemplateColumns: "minmax(200px, 0.45fr) minmax(280px, 0.95fr) minmax(360px, 1.3fr)",
    }
  }
  return {
    panelOrder: ["context", "correction"],
    queueWeight: 0,
    contextWeight: 0.85,
    correctionWeight: 1.15,
    gridTemplateColumns: "minmax(320px, 0.85fr) minmax(0, 1.15fr)",
  }
}

export function correctionQueueItemState({
  reviewStage,
  deviationCount,
  pendingTruthCount,
}: {
  reviewStage: string | null | undefined
  deviationCount: number
  pendingTruthCount: number
}): CorrectionQueueState {
  if (reviewStage === "completed") {
    return { key: "completed", label: "已完成", tone: "success" }
  }
  if (deviationCount > 0) {
    return { key: "different", label: "有差异", tone: "danger" }
  }
  if (pendingTruthCount > 0) {
    return { key: "pending", label: "待填写", tone: "neutral" }
  }
  return { key: "ready", label: "待确认", tone: "warning" }
}

export function structuredCorrectionPrimaryAction(
  hasNext: boolean,
  pending: boolean,
): string {
  if (pending) return "正在保存"
  return hasNext ? "保存纠偏并查看下一条" : "保存本条纠偏"
}

export function knowledgeGraphTruthRoute(
  workflowInput: Record<string, unknown> | null | undefined,
): KnowledgeGraphTruthRoute | null {
  if (!workflowInput) return null
  const source = workflowInput.source === "domestic"
    ? "国内"
    : workflowInput.source === "overseas"
      ? "海外"
      : null
  const scopeValue = scopeFromIsSingle(workflowInput.source as string, workflowInput.is_single)
  const scope = scopeValue === "single" ? "单体" : scopeValue === "whole" ? "整体" : null
  if (!source || !scope) return null
  return { source, scope, label: `${source} · ${scope}` }
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/baseline-regression/correction-stage-meta.tsx =====
import type { BaselineCorrectionRun, BaselineRegressionItem } from "@/lib/types"

export const correctionStages: Array<{
  key: BaselineCorrectionRun["stage"]
  label: string
}> = [
  { key: "analysis", label: "自动分析纠偏样本" },
  { key: "candidate_generation", label: "生成统一机制候选" },
  { key: "candidate_validation", label: "校验并冻结候选" },
  { key: "regression", label: "执行候选回归" },
  { key: "decision", label: "等待人工决策" },
]

export type CorrectionStageState = "pending" | "active" | "completed" | "failed"

export function correctionStageState(
  run: BaselineCorrectionRun,
  stage: BaselineCorrectionRun["stage"],
): CorrectionStageState {
  const currentIndex = correctionStages.findIndex((item) => item.key === run.stage)
  const stageIndex = correctionStages.findIndex((item) => item.key === stage)
  if (run.status === "approved" || run.status === "rejected") return "completed"
  if (stageIndex < currentIndex) return "completed"
  if (stageIndex > currentIndex) return "pending"
  if (run.status === "failed") return "failed"
  return "active"
}

export function correctionStageClassName(state: CorrectionStageState) {
  if (state === "completed") return "border-alias-control-002 bg-primary text-active-text"
  if (state === "active") return "border-alias-control-002 bg-active-surface text-active-text"
  if (state === "failed") return "border-danger bg-alias-surface-063 text-alias-content-041"
  return "border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-white text-[var(--muted)]"
}

export function correctionStageStateName(state: CorrectionStageState) {
  if (state === "completed") return "已完成"
  if (state === "active") return "进行中"
  if (state === "failed") return "执行失败"
  return "等待自动执行"
}

export function correctionStageLabel(stage: BaselineCorrectionRun["stage"]) {
  return correctionStages.find((item) => item.key === stage)?.label ?? "自动纠偏"
}

export function correctionStageRunningMessage(stage: BaselineCorrectionRun["stage"]) {
  if (stage === "analysis") return "AI 正在分析纠偏样本与偏差方向"
  if (stage === "candidate_generation") return "AI 正在生成统一机制候选"
  if (stage === "candidate_validation") return "系统正在校验并冻结候选版本"
  if (stage === "regression") return "系统正在执行候选回归"
  return "系统正在整理最终决策证据"
}

export function correctionStatusName(run: BaselineCorrectionRun) {
  if (run.status === "processing") return correctionStageLabel(run.stage)
  if (run.status === "awaiting_decision") return "等待人工决策"
  if (run.status === "approved") return "已启用候选"
  if (run.status === "rejected") return "已拒绝候选"
  return "执行失败"
}

export function correctionStatusTone(run: BaselineCorrectionRun): "active" | "success" | "danger" {
  if (run.status === "processing") return "active"
  if (run.status === "awaiting_decision" || run.status === "approved") return "success"
  return "danger"
}

export function reviewStatus(evaluation: BaselineRegressionItem["evaluation"]) {
  if (!evaluation) return null
  if (evaluation.review_panel && evaluation.review_panel.status !== "completed") {
    return {
      label: `审核中 ${evaluation.review_panel.submitted_count}/${evaluation.review_panel.required_reviewers}`,
      tone: "active" as const,
    }
  }
  const decision = evaluation.human_review?.decision
  if (decision === "approved") return { label: "已确认", tone: "success" as const }
  if (decision === "corrected") {
    return {
      label: `已纠偏 · 人工 ${evaluation.final_level ?? "维度"}`,
      tone: "warning" as const,
    }
  }
  if (decision === "rejected") return { label: "已退回", tone: "danger" as const }
  if (evaluation.review_panel) {
    return {
      label: `审核中 ${evaluation.review_panel.submitted_count}/${evaluation.review_panel.required_reviewers}`,
      tone: "active" as const,
    }
  }
  return { label: "待审核", tone: "neutral" as const }
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/baseline-regression/correction-table.tsx =====
import { createIdempotencyToken } from "@/lib/idempotency-token"
import { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from "react"
import { useMutation, useQueries, useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query"
import { toast } from "sonner"
import { baselineRegressionApi } from "@/lib/api"
import type { BaselineRegressionItem } from "@/lib/types"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { TablePreferences, orderedColumns } from "@/components/table-preferences"
import { useRememberedPage } from "@/lib/preferences"
import { Textarea } from "@/components/ui/textarea"
import { ImagePreviewButton, type ImagePreview } from "@/components/image-lightbox"
import { renderCorrectionNode } from "@/features/correction-contract/contract-renderer.tsx"
import { correctionNodeEditable } from "@/features/correction-contract/contract-renderer"
import { correctionValueFromNode } from "@/features/correction-contract/correction-view-state"
import type { CorrectionContractNode, CorrectionView } from "@/features/correction-contract/types"
import { CorrectionAutosave } from "./correction-autosave"
import { refreshCorrectionItem } from "./correction-cache"
import { correctionDraftEntry, correctionNoteDirty, openCorrectionNote, resetCorrectionNote, releaseCleanDraft } from "./correction-draft-store"

const fieldKey = (node: CorrectionContractNode) => node.metadata?.field_identity?.field_path ?? node.node_key

export function CorrectionTable({ runId, items, disabled, onPreview, onDirtyChange }: {
  runId: number; items: BaselineRegressionItem[]; disabled: boolean
  onPreview: (preview: ImagePreview) => void; onDirtyChange: (dirty: boolean) => void
}) {
  const remembered = useRememberedPage("baseline-regression")
  const [field, setField] = useState("")
  const [dirtyRows, setDirtyRows] = useState<Set<number>>(new Set())
  const headerScroll = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null)
  const bodyScroll = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null)
  const client = useQueryClient()
  const completedIds = items.filter(item => item.status === "completed").map(item => item.id)
  const batch = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["baseline-correction-page", runId, "table", completedIds],
    queryFn: async () => {
      const response = await baselineRegressionApi.getCorrectionViews(runId, completedIds, "table")
      response.items.forEach(view => {
        const key = ["baseline-correction-view", runId, view.item_id, "table"]
        const current = client.getQueryData<CorrectionView>(key)
        if (!current || current.review_revision <= view.review_revision) {
          view.notes = [...new Map([...(view.notes ?? []), ...(current?.notes ?? [])].map(note => [note.id, note])).values()].sort((a, b) => a.id - b.id)
          client.setQueryData(key, view)
        }
      })
      return response
    },
    enabled: completedIds.length > 0, staleTime: 60_000,
  })
  const views = useQueries({ queries: items.map(item => ({
    queryKey: ["baseline-correction-view", runId, item.id, "table"],
    queryFn: () => baselineRegressionApi.getCorrectionView(runId, item.id, "table"),
    enabled: false, staleTime: 60_000,
  })) })
  const columns = useMemo(() => {
    const fields = new Map<string, string>()
    views.forEach(query => query.data?.nodes.filter(correctionNodeEditable).forEach(node => fields.set(fieldKey(node), node.label)))
    return [...fields].map(([key, label]) => ({ key, label }))
  }, [views])
  const catalog = [{ key: "material", label: "素材", width: 176, fixed: true },
    ...columns.map((column) => ({ ...column, key: `field:${column.key}`, width: 260 })),
    { key: "notes", label: "保存状态与人工备注", width: 288, fixed: true }]
  const ordered = orderedColumns(catalog, remembered.table)
  const visible = ordered.filter(column => column.key.startsWith("field:") && column.visible && (!field || column.key === `field:${field}`))
    .map(column => ({ ...column, key: column.key.slice(6) }))
  const widths = [ordered.find(column => column.key === "material")?.width ?? 176, ...visible.map(column => column.width), ordered.find(column => column.key === "notes")?.width ?? 288]
  const tableStyle = { minWidth: widths.reduce((sum, width) => sum + width, 0) }
  const colgroup = <colgroup>{widths.map((width, index) => <col key={index} style={{ width }} />)}</colgroup>
  useEffect(() => { onDirtyChange(dirtyRows.size > 0) }, [dirtyRows, onDirtyChange])
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!dirtyRows.size) return
    const warn = (event: BeforeUnloadEvent) => { event.preventDefault(); event.returnValue = "" }
    window.addEventListener("beforeunload", warn)
    return () => window.removeEventListener("beforeunload", warn)
  }, [dirtyRows])
  return <section className={`mt-5 ${remembered.table.density === "compact" ? "[&_td]:py-2 [&_th]:py-2" : ""}`} aria-label="素材字段纠偏表格">
    <div className="mb-3 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
      <h3 className="text-xl font-bold">表格纠偏</h3>
      <label className="text-sm">查看字段 <select aria-label="查看字段" value={field} onChange={event => setField(event.target.value)} className="visual-native-select ml-2">
        <option value="">全部字段</option>{columns.map(column => <option key={column.key} value={column.key}>{column.label}</option>)}
      </select></label>
      <TablePreferences columns={catalog} value={remembered.table} onChange={remembered.setTable} disabled={!remembered.ready} />
      <p className="text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">字段停止输入后自动保存，离开单元格或按 Enter 也会立即保存；无需逐条确认。人工备注可基于上次内容反复补充或修改，单独提交；图片可点击放大。切换页面或轮次会保留未保存草稿，返回该素材可继续。</p>
    </div>
    <div className="sticky top-28 z-10 rounded-t-md border border-[var(--line)] bg-alias-surface-029 lg:top-12" data-testid="correction-table-sticky-header">
      <div ref={headerScroll} className="overflow-x-auto rounded-t-md" onScroll={event => {
        if (bodyScroll.current) bodyScroll.current.scrollLeft = event.currentTarget.scrollLeft
      }}>
        <table className="w-full table-fixed border-collapse text-sm" style={tableStyle}>{colgroup}
          <thead><tr><th className="sticky left-0 z-10 bg-alias-surface-029 p-3 text-left">素材</th>
            {visible.map(column => <th key={column.key} className="p-3 text-left">{column.label}</th>)}
            <th className="p-3 text-left">保存状态与人工备注</th></tr></thead>
        </table>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div ref={bodyScroll} className="overflow-x-auto rounded-b-md border-x border-b border-[var(--line)]" onScroll={event => {
      if (headerScroll.current) headerScroll.current.scrollLeft = event.currentTarget.scrollLeft
    }}>
      <table className="w-full table-fixed border-collapse text-sm" style={tableStyle} aria-label="素材字段纠偏内容">{colgroup}
        <thead className="sr-only"><tr><th>素材</th>{visible.map(column => <th key={column.key}>{column.label}</th>)}<th>保存状态与人工备注</th></tr></thead>
        <tbody>{items.map((item, index) => <CorrectionRow key={`${runId}:${item.id}`} runId={runId} item={item}
          view={views[index].data} error={batch.error?.message ?? views[index].error?.message} columns={visible} disabled={disabled}
          onPreview={onPreview} onDirtyChange={dirty => setDirtyRows(previous => {
            if (previous.has(item.id) === dirty) return previous
            const next = new Set(previous); if (dirty) next.add(item.id); else next.delete(item.id); return next
          })} />)}</tbody>
      </table>
    </div>
  </section>
}

function CorrectionRow({ runId, item, view: latestView, error, columns, disabled, onPreview, onDirtyChange }: {
  runId: number; item: BaselineRegressionItem; view?: CorrectionView; error?: string
  columns: {key: string; label: string}[]; disabled: boolean
  onPreview: (preview: ImagePreview) => void; onDirtyChange: (dirty: boolean) => void
}) {
  const client = useQueryClient()
  const [, render] = useState(0)
  const entry = useMemo(() => correctionDraftEntry(runId, item.id), [runId, item.id])
  const composingFields = useRef(new Set<string>())
  const editor = { get current() { return entry.editor }, set current(value: CorrectionAutosave | null) { entry.editor = value } }
  const note = entry.note
  const setNote = (value: string) => { entry.note = value; entry.listeners.forEach(notify => notify()) }
  const noteOpen = entry.noteOpen
  const noteRequest = { get current() { return entry.noteRequest }, set current(value: typeof entry.noteRequest) { entry.noteRequest = value } }
  useEffect(() => {
    const notify = () => render(version => version + 1)
    entry.listeners.add(notify)
    return () => { entry.listeners.delete(notify); releaseCleanDraft(runId, item.id) }
  }, [entry, runId, item.id])
  const view = editor.current?.dirty ? editor.current.view : latestView ?? editor.current?.view
  const edit = (nodeKey: string, value: unknown) => {
    if (!view || disabled) return
    if (!editor.current || !editor.current.dirty) editor.current = new CorrectionAutosave(latestView ?? view, {
      notify: () => entry.listeners.forEach(notify => notify()),
      send: payload => entry.active ? baselineRegressionApi.submitCorrectionNodes(runId, item.id, payload, "table") : Promise.reject(new Error("登录账号已变化，请重新打开纠偏页面")),
      accept: response => {
        if (!entry.active) return
        const current = client.getQueryData<CorrectionView>(["baseline-correction-view", runId, item.id, "table"])
        response.notes = [...new Map([...(response.notes ?? []), ...(current?.notes ?? [])].map(note => [note.id, note])).values()].sort((a, b) => a.id - b.id)
        client.setQueryData(["baseline-correction-view", runId, item.id, "table"], response)
        void refreshCorrectionItem(client, runId, item.id, response.review_revision,
          () => baselineRegressionApi.getRunItem(runId, item.id, "list"))
          .catch(() => toast.error("字段已保存，结果摘要刷新失败；可稍后刷新页面"))
      },
      confirmRerun: () => window.confirm("此字段修改需要重跑后续模型节点并消耗调用额度，是否继续？"),
    })
    editor.current.edit(nodeKey, value)
    editor.current.composition(nodeKey, composingFields.current.has(nodeKey))
  }
  const pending = Boolean(editor.current?.busy)
  const dirty = Boolean(editor.current?.dirty || correctionNoteDirty(entry))
  useEffect(() => { onDirtyChange(dirty) }, [dirty, onDirtyChange])
  const saveNote = useMutation({
    mutationFn: () => {
      if (disabled) return Promise.reject(new Error("当前角色或类目仅可查看"))
      if (!entry.active) return Promise.reject(new Error("登录账号已变化，请重新打开纠偏页面"))
      if (noteRequest.current?.text !== note.trim()) noteRequest.current = {
        text: note.trim(), idempotency_key: `note:${runId}:${item.id}:${createIdempotencyToken()}`,
      }
      entry.noteBusy = true
      entry.listeners.forEach(notify => notify())
      return baselineRegressionApi.addCorrectionNote(runId, item.id, noteRequest.current!).finally(() => {
        entry.noteBusy = false; entry.listeners.forEach(notify => notify())
      })
    },
    onSuccess: response => {
      if (!entry.active) return
      client.setQueryData(["baseline-correction-view", runId, item.id, "table"], (current: CorrectionView | undefined) => current ? { ...current, notes: response.notes } : current)
      if (editor.current) editor.current.view = { ...editor.current.view, notes: response.notes }
      resetCorrectionNote(entry)
      toast.success("人工备注已保存")
    },
    onError: error => toast.error(error.message),
  })
  const locked = disabled || view?.read_only
  return <tr className="border-t border-[var(--line)] align-top">
    <td className="sticky left-0 z-[1] bg-white p-3"><ImagePreviewButton src={item.image_url} alt={item.asset.name} imageClassName="h-24 w-32 object-contain bg-white" onPreview={onPreview}/>
      <p className="mt-2 break-all text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">{item.asset.name}</p><p className="text-xs">素材 #<span className="visual-code">{item.asset.asset_id}</span></p>
      {item.status !== "completed" && <p className="text-xs text-red-700">未完成评测，请先处理失败</p>}</td>
    {columns.map(column => {
      const node = view?.nodes.find(node => fieldKey(node) === column.key && correctionNodeEditable(node))
      const savedValue = node ? correctionValueFromNode(node) : undefined
      const draft = node ? editor.current?.draft[node.node_key] : undefined
      return <td key={column.key} className="p-3" onCompositionStart={() => { if (node) { composingFields.current.add(node.node_key); editor.current?.composition(node.node_key, true) } }} onCompositionEnd={() => { if (node) { composingFields.current.delete(node.node_key); editor.current?.composition(node.node_key, false) } }} onBlur={event => {
        if (node && !event.currentTarget.contains(event.relatedTarget as Node | null)) editor.current?.commit(node.node_key)
      }} onKeyDown={event => {
        if (event.key === "Enter" && !event.nativeEvent.isComposing && event.target instanceof HTMLInputElement) event.target.blur()
      }}>{node ? <>
        <p className="mb-2 whitespace-pre-wrap break-words text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">{view?.route_field_confirmation ? node.human_value !== undefined ? "新路线人工真值" : "新路线待确认" : node.human_value !== undefined ? "人工纠偏" : "当前结果"}：{savedValue === null ? "已清空" : JSON.stringify(savedValue ?? "")}</p>
        {view?.route_field_confirmation && <p className="mb-2 whitespace-pre-wrap break-words text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">旧路线模型：{JSON.stringify(node.model_value ?? "")}</p>}
        {renderCorrectionNode({...node, node_key: `${item.id}.${node.node_key}`}, draft ? draft.value : savedValue,
          value => edit(node.node_key, value), Boolean(locked))}
        <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]" aria-live="polite">{editor.current?.queue.some(task => task.key === node.node_key)
          ? editor.current.error ? "保存失败，修改仍保留" : "保存中…" : editor.current?.draft[node.node_key]?.dirty ? "等待自动保存…" : editor.current?.saved.has(node.node_key) ? "已保存" : ""}</p>
      </> : <span className="text-[var(--muted)]">—</span>}</td>
    })}
    <td className="p-3">{error ? <p className="text-red-700">{error}</p> : !view && item.status === "completed" ? <p>加载字段中…</p> : <>
      <p role="status" className="mb-2 text-xs">{pending ? "正在保存字段…" : editor.current?.error ? "字段保存失败" : editor.current?.dirty ? "有待保存字段" : editor.current?.saved.size ? view?.route_field_confirmation ? "已保存为新路线人工真值" : "字段纠偏已保存，可用于分析" : !view ? "素材完成评测后可纠偏" : locked ? "当前结果只读，不能修改字段或备注" : !view.nodes.some(correctionNodeEditable) ? "当前结果没有可编辑字段，可补充人工备注" : "编辑字段即可纠偏"}</p>
      {view?.route_field_confirmation && <p className="mb-3 text-xs text-amber-800">整体/单体已修改，可继续纠偏其他字段。人工真值供新路线使用；本轮模型结果仍是旧路线，请重新回归后分析模型表现。</p>}
      {editor.current?.error && <div className="mb-3 text-xs text-red-700"><p>{editor.current.error}</p>
        <Button size="sm" variant="secondary" onClick={async () => {
          if (editor.current?.failedStatus === 409) {
            if (!window.confirm("审核版本已变化。保留你的字段修改，读取最新结果后重新提交？")) return
            try { editor.current.refresh(await baselineRegressionApi.getCorrectionView(runId, item.id, "table")) } catch (error) { toast.error((error as Error).message) }
          } else editor.current?.retry()
        }}>{editor.current.failedStatus === 409 ? "刷新后重试" : "重试保存"}</Button>
      </div>}
      {dirty && <Button variant="ghost" size="sm" disabled={pending || entry.noteBusy} onClick={async () => {
        if (!window.confirm("放弃此素材未保存的字段与备注？已经保存的纠偏不会撤销。")) return
        entry.editor?.cancelScheduled(); entry.editor = null; resetCorrectionNote(entry)
        render(version => version + 1)
        try { client.setQueryData(["baseline-correction-view", runId, item.id, "table"], await baselineRegressionApi.getCorrectionView(runId, item.id, "table")) }
        catch (error) { toast.error((error as Error).message) }
      }}>放弃未保存修改</Button>}
      {(view?.notes ?? []).slice(-1).map(note => <p key={note.id} className="mb-2 whitespace-pre-wrap break-words text-xs">{note.text}<span className="ml-2 text-[var(--muted)]">{note.actor}</span></p>)}
      {(view?.notes?.length ?? 0) > 1 && <details className="visual-native-details mb-2 text-xs"><summary className="">历史备注（{view!.notes!.length - 1}）</summary>{view!.notes!.slice(0, -1).map(note => <p key={note.id} className="mt-2 whitespace-pre-wrap break-words">{note.text}<span className="ml-2 text-[var(--muted)]">{note.actor}</span></p>)}</details>}
      {noteOpen ? <>
        <Textarea rows={3} aria-label={`素材 ${item.asset.asset_id} 人工备注`} value={note} maxLength={2000} onChange={event => setNote(event.target.value)} placeholder="在上次备注基础上补充或修改（选填）" disabled={Boolean(locked) || entry.noteBusy}/>
        <Button className="mt-2" size="sm" disabled={Boolean(locked) || entry.noteBusy || !note.trim() || !correctionNoteDirty(entry)} onClick={() => saveNote.mutate()}>{entry.noteBusy ? "保存备注中…" : "提交人工备注"}</Button>
      </> : <Button variant="secondary" size="sm" disabled={Boolean(locked) || !view} onClick={() => openCorrectionNote(entry, view?.notes ?? [])}>补充人工备注</Button>}
    </>}</td>
  </tr>
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/baseline-regression/correction-workbench.tsx =====
import { scopeFromIsSingle, scopeLabel } from "./kg-route-codes"
import { type CSSProperties, type ReactNode } from "react"
import { ArrowLeft, CaretLeft, CaretRight } from "@phosphor-icons/react"

import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { ImagePreviewButton } from "@/components/image-lightbox"
import { NodeCorrectionEditor } from "@/pages/node-correction-editor"
import type { BaselineRegressionItem, Evaluation } from "@/lib/types"
import { CorrectionContractRenderer } from "@/features/correction-contract/contract-renderer.tsx"
import { NativeEvaluationDetail } from "@/features/correction-contract/native-evaluation-detail"
import { nativeEvaluationPresentation } from "@/features/correction-contract/native-evaluation-presentation"
import type { CorrectionDraft, CorrectionView } from "@/features/correction-contract/types"
import { RealModelEvidence } from "@/features/baseline-regression/real-model-evidence"
import {
  TruthRevisionPanel,
  type KnowledgeGraphRouteRevision,
  type TruthFieldConfirmation,
} from "@/features/baseline-regression/truth-revision-panel"
import {
  correctionQueueItemState,
  correctionWorkspaceLayout,
  knowledgeGraphTruthRoute,
} from "@/features/baseline-regression/correction-presentation"

export function CorrectionWorkbench({
  item,
  queueItems = [],
  onSelectQueueItem,
  runSequence,
  reviewedCount,
  reviewTotal,
  hasUnsavedChanges = false,
  onBack,
  corrector,
  onCorrected,
  onPreview,
  onPrevious,
  hasPrevious,
  onNext,
  hasNext,
  correctionView,
  correctionDraft,
  onCorrectionChange,
  onCorrectionSubmit,
  correctionSubmitPending,
  correctionSubmitDisabled,
  correctionSubmitBlockReason,
  correctionDisabled,
  correctionViewLoading = false,
  correctionViewError,
  onConfirmTruthField,
  truthFieldPending,
  onReviseKnowledgeGraphRoute,
  routeRevisionPending,
  decisionActions,
  children,
}: {
  item: BaselineRegressionItem
  queueItems?: BaselineRegressionItem[]
  onSelectQueueItem?: (itemId: number) => void
  runSequence?: number
  reviewedCount?: number
  reviewTotal?: number
  hasUnsavedChanges?: boolean
  onBack: () => void
  corrector: string
  onCorrected: () => Promise<void> | void
  onPreview: (preview: { src: string; alt: string }) => void
  onPrevious?: () => void
  hasPrevious?: boolean
  onNext?: () => void
  hasNext?: boolean
  correctionView?: CorrectionView | null
  correctionDraft?: CorrectionDraft
  onCorrectionChange?: (nodeKey: string, patch: Partial<CorrectionDraft[string]>) => void
  onCorrectionSubmit?: () => void
  correctionSubmitPending?: boolean
  correctionSubmitDisabled?: boolean
  correctionSubmitBlockReason?: string | null
  correctionDisabled?: boolean
  correctionViewLoading?: boolean
  correctionViewError?: string | null
  onConfirmTruthField?: (confirmation: TruthFieldConfirmation) => Promise<void> | void
  truthFieldPending?: boolean
  onReviseKnowledgeGraphRoute?: (revision: KnowledgeGraphRouteRevision) => Promise<void> | void
  routeRevisionPending?: boolean
  decisionActions?: ReactNode
  children?: ReactNode
}) {
  const evaluation = item.evaluation as Evaluation | null
  const structured = item.truth_kind === "structured_fields"
  const nativePresentation = !structured && correctionView?.contract
    ? nativeEvaluationPresentation(correctionView.nodes)
    : null
  const layout = correctionWorkspaceLayout(structured ? "structured_fields" : "aesthetic_level")
  const position = queueItems.findIndex((entry) => entry.id === item.id) + 1
  const requestNavigation = (navigate?: () => void) => {
    if (!navigate) return
    if (
      !hasUnsavedChanges
      || window.confirm("当前纠偏还没有保存。离开后会丢失这些修改，是否继续？")
    ) navigate()
  }

  const truthPanel = correctionView ? (
        <TruthRevisionPanel
          view={correctionView}
          onConfirmField={onConfirmTruthField}
          onReviseRoute={onReviseKnowledgeGraphRoute}
          fieldPending={truthFieldPending}
          routePending={routeRevisionPending}
          disabled={correctionDisabled}
          frozenTruthContractKey={item.truth_contract_key}
        />
  ) : null

  const correctionContent = (
    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4">
      {structured && truthPanel}
      {correctionView && correctionDraft && onCorrectionChange ? (
        <>
          {nativePresentation ? <NativeEvaluationDetail
            key={`${correctionView.run_id}:${correctionView.item_id}:${correctionView.evaluation_id}`}
            presentation={nativePresentation}
            view={correctionView}
            draft={correctionDraft}
            onChange={onCorrectionChange}
            onSubmit={onCorrectionSubmit}
            pending={correctionSubmitPending}
            submitDisabled={correctionSubmitDisabled}
            submitBlockReason={correctionSubmitBlockReason}
            disabled={correctionDisabled}
          /> : <CorrectionContractRenderer
            presentation={structured ? "supplementary" : "primary"}
            view={correctionView}
            draft={correctionDraft}
            onChange={onCorrectionChange}
            onSubmit={onCorrectionSubmit}
            pending={correctionSubmitPending}
            submitDisabled={correctionSubmitDisabled}
            submitBlockReason={correctionSubmitBlockReason}
            disabled={correctionDisabled}
          />}
          <details className="visual-native-details border-y border-[var(--line)] bg-alias-surface-051">
            <summary className="font-semibold">查看模型运行证据</summary>
            <RealModelEvidence nodes={correctionView.nodes} />
          </details>
        </>
      ) : evaluation && evaluation.scoring?.v3_context ? (
        <NodeCorrectionEditor
          key={`${evaluation.id}-${evaluation.review_revision}`}
          evaluation={evaluation}
          corrector={corrector}
          onCorrected={onCorrected}
        />
      ) : null}
      {children}
    </div>
  )

  return (
    <section className="rounded-lg border border-[var(--line)] bg-white" aria-label="逐条确认与纠偏">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-4 border-b border-[var(--line)] px-5 py-4 md:px-7">
        <div>
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
            <Button variant="ghost" size="sm" onClick={() => requestNavigation(onBack)}><ArrowLeft />返回回归结果</Button>
            <h2 className="visual-card-title">
              {structured ? "知识图谱 · 存量回归纠偏" : "逐条确认与纠偏"}
            </h2>
            {runSequence != null && <Badge tone="active">第 {runSequence} 轮</Badge>}
            {position > 0 && <span className="font-data text-sm text-muted-foreground">{position} / {queueItems.length}</span>}
          </div>
          <p className="mt-2 break-words text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">
            当前素材：{item.asset.name}
            {structured
              ? ` · ${(item.field_deviations ?? []).length} 个字段与人工真值不同`
              : ` · 期望 ${item.expected_level ?? "—"} · 预测 ${item.predicted_level ?? "—"}`}
            {reviewedCount != null && reviewTotal != null ? ` · 人工已确认 ${reviewedCount} / ${reviewTotal}` : ""}
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>

      {structured ? (
        <div
          className="structured-correction-grid grid min-w-0"
          style={{ "--correction-workspace-columns": layout.gridTemplateColumns } as CSSProperties}
        >
          <CorrectionQueue
            items={queueItems.length ? queueItems : [item]}
            currentId={item.id}
            onSelect={(itemId) => {
              if (itemId === item.id) return
              requestNavigation(onSelectQueueItem ? () => onSelectQueueItem(itemId) : undefined)
            }}
            reviewedCount={reviewedCount}
            reviewTotal={reviewTotal}
          />
          <StructuredAssetContext
            item={item}
            view={correctionView ?? null}
            loading={correctionViewLoading}
            errorMessage={correctionViewError}
            onPreview={onPreview}
          />
          <section className="structured-correction-evidence min-w-0" aria-label="纠偏主面板" tabIndex={0}>
            <header className="flex flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-3 border-b border-[var(--line)] pb-5">
              <div>
                <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-[var(--muted)]">本次模型结果</p>
                <h3 className="mt-1 text-xl font-bold">核对差异并写入人工真值</h3>
                <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">优先处理有差异和待填写字段；节点运行证据仅在需要排查时展开。</p>
              </div>
              <Badge tone={(item.field_deviations ?? []).length ? "danger" : "success"}>
                {(item.field_deviations ?? []).length ? `${item.field_deviations?.length} 项不同` : "待人工确认"}
              </Badge>
            </header>
            {correctionViewLoading && (
              <CorrectionViewStatus tone="loading" message="正在读取本条素材的人工真值与节点结果…" />
            )}
            {correctionViewError && (
              <CorrectionViewStatus tone="error" message={correctionViewError} />
            )}
            {correctionContent}
          </section>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div
          className="correction-workspace-grid grid min-w-0"
          style={{ "--correction-workspace-columns": layout.gridTemplateColumns } as CSSProperties}
        >
          <AssetPreview item={item} onPreview={onPreview}>{truthPanel}</AssetPreview>
          <div role="region" aria-label="评测依据与纠偏" tabIndex={0} className="min-w-0 border-t border-[var(--line-strong)] xl:border-l xl:border-t-0 focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-inset focus-visible:ring-primary">
            {correctionContent}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
      <footer data-testid="correction-decision-dock" className="correction-decision-dock flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-4 border-t border-[var(--line)] px-5 py-4 md:px-7">
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
          {position > 0 && <span className="font-data text-sm text-muted-foreground">{position} / {queueItems.length}</span>}
          {onPrevious && <Button variant="secondary" onClick={() => requestNavigation(onPrevious)} disabled={!hasPrevious}><CaretLeft />上一条</Button>}
          {onNext && <Button variant="secondary" onClick={() => requestNavigation(onNext)} disabled={!hasNext}>下一条<CaretRight /></Button>}
        </div>
        <div className="min-w-0">{decisionActions}</div>
      </footer>
    </section>
  )
}

function CorrectionQueue({
  items,
  currentId,
  onSelect,
  reviewedCount,
  reviewTotal,
}: {
  items: BaselineRegressionItem[]
  currentId: number
  onSelect?: (itemId: number) => void
  reviewedCount?: number
  reviewTotal?: number
}) {
  const differentCount = items.filter((item) => (item.field_deviations ?? []).length > 0).length
