  const completedCount = items.filter((item) => item.evaluation?.review_stage === "completed").length
  return (
    <aside className="correction-queue flex min-w-0 flex-col" aria-label="待纠偏素材">
      <div className="correction-queue-heading border-b border-[var(--line)] px-4 py-4">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
          <h3 className="text-base font-bold">待纠偏素材</h3>
          <Badge tone="neutral">{Math.max(0, items.length - completedCount)} 条</Badge>
        </div>
        <div className="mt-3 flex flex-wrap gap-2 text-xs">
          <span className="border border-active-border bg-alias-surface-027 px-2 py-1 rounded-md">全部 {items.length}</span>
          <span className="border border-alias-edge-056 bg-alias-surface-063 px-2 py-1 text-alias-content-041 rounded-md">有差异 {differentCount}</span>
          <span className="border border-[var(--line)] bg-workspace px-2 py-1 rounded-md">已完成 {completedCount}</span>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div className="correction-queue-items min-h-0 flex-1 p-3">
        {items.map((queueItem) => {
          const pendingTruthCount = countBlankLeafValues(queueItem.expected_result)
          const state = correctionQueueItemState({
            reviewStage: queueItem.evaluation?.review_stage,
            deviationCount: queueItem.field_deviations?.length ?? 0,
            pendingTruthCount,
          })
          const active = queueItem.id === currentId
          return (
            <button
              key={queueItem.id}
              type="button"
              className={"visual-native-button correction-queue-item flex items-start gap-3 " + (`w-full rounded-md border p-3 text-left ${
                active ? "border-active-border bg-alias-surface-027" : "border-[var(--line)] bg-white hover:bg-alias-surface-051"
              }`)}
              aria-current={active ? "true" : undefined}
              onClick={() => onSelect?.(queueItem.id)}
            >
              <span className="size-12 shrink-0 overflow-hidden rounded-md border border-line bg-white">
                <img src={queueItem.image_url} alt="" loading="lazy" className="size-full rounded-none object-contain" />
              </span>
              <span className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                <strong className="block break-all text-xs leading-5">{queueItem.asset.name}</strong>
                <Badge className="mt-1" tone={state.tone}>{state.label}</Badge>
                <span className="mt-2 block text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">
                  {routeFromItem(queueItem) || (pendingTruthCount ? `${pendingTruthCount} 个字段待填写` : "等待人工核对")}
                </span>
              </span>
            </button>
          )
        })}
      </div>
      <div className="correction-queue-progress mt-auto border-t border-[var(--line)] px-4 py-3 text-xs leading-5">
        <p className="font-bold">人工确认进度 {reviewedCount ?? completedCount} / {reviewTotal ?? items.length}</p>
        <p className="mt-1 text-[var(--muted)]">每次纠偏都会追加一版新的有效人工真值；历史版本保留，下一轮使用最新版本。</p>
      </div>
    </aside>
  )
}

function StructuredAssetContext({
  item,
  view,
  loading,
  errorMessage,
  onPreview,
}: {
  item: BaselineRegressionItem
  view: CorrectionView | null
  loading: boolean
  errorMessage?: string | null
  onPreview: (preview: { src: string; alt: string }) => void
}) {
  const truth = view?.effective_truth
  const route = knowledgeGraphTruthRoute(truth?.workflow_input)
  const routeLabel = loading
    ? "正在读取路线"
    : errorMessage
      ? "路线读取失败"
      : route?.label ?? "路线待人工确认"
  const known = view?.known_truth_fields?.length ?? 0
  const pending = view?.pending_truth_fields?.length ?? 0
  return (
    <div className="structured-asset-context min-w-0">
      <div className="shrink-0 px-5 pt-5">
        <div className="min-w-0">
          <h3 className="break-all text-sm font-bold">{item.asset.name}</h3>
          <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">人工真值路线：{routeLabel}</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div className="correction-asset-stage shrink-0 p-5">
        <ImagePreviewButton
          src={item.image_url}
          alt={item.asset.name}
          imageClassName="structured-asset-image w-full rounded-none bg-white object-contain"
          matte="white"
          onPreview={onPreview}
        />
      </div>
      <section className="structured-truth-summary min-h-0 space-y-4 border-t border-line px-5 py-5" aria-label="当前人工真值摘要" tabIndex={0}>
        <div>
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
            <h3 className="text-lg font-bold">当前人工真值</h3>
            <Badge tone={view?.truth_revision != null ? "success" : "neutral"}>
              {view?.truth_revision != null
                ? `${view.truth_source === "revision" ? "人工真值" : "导入真值"} v${view.truth_revision}`
                : loading ? "正在读取" : errorMessage ? "读取失败" : "尚未建立"}
            </Badge>
          </div>
          <p className="mt-2 text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">这里用于核对上下文；具体差异与修改集中在右侧纠偏主面板。</p>
        </div>
        <div className="border border-active-border bg-alias-surface-027 px-3 py-3 rounded-md">
          <p className="text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">素材路线</p>
          <p className="mt-1 text-base font-bold">{routeLabel}</p>
        </div>
        <div className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-1 2xl:grid-cols-2">
          <ContextFact label="已确认字段" value={view ? `${known} 项` : "—"} />
          <ContextFact label="待补充字段" value={view ? `${pending} 项` : "—"} />
          {Object.entries(truth?.expected_result ?? {}).slice(0, 4).map(([key, value]) => (
            <ContextFact key={key} label={key} value={displayCompactValue(value)} />
          ))}
        </div>
      </section>
    </div>
  )
}

function CorrectionViewStatus({
  tone,
  message,
}: {
  tone: "loading" | "error"
  message: string
}) {
  return (
    <div
      role={tone === "error" ? "alert" : "status"}
      className={`mx-5 mt-4 border rounded-md px-4 py-3 text-xs leading-5 md:mx-6 ${
        tone === "error"
          ? "border-alias-edge-041 bg-alias-surface-063 text-alias-content-041"
          : "border-[var(--line)] bg-workspace text-[var(--muted)]"
      }`}
    >
      {message}
    </div>
  )
}

function AssetPreview({
  item,
  onPreview,
  children,
}: {
  item: BaselineRegressionItem
  children?: ReactNode
  onPreview: (preview: { src: string; alt: string }) => void
}) {
  return (
    <div className="correction-asset-column min-w-0 px-5 py-5 md:px-7">
      <div className="correction-asset-context sticky top-4">
        <div className="correction-asset-stage flex items-center justify-center bg-white">
          <ImagePreviewButton
            src={item.image_url}
            alt={item.asset.name}
            imageClassName="correction-asset-image w-full max-w-full rounded-none bg-white object-contain"
            matte="white"
            onPreview={onPreview}
          />
        </div>
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 border-t border-[var(--line)] px-4 py-3">
          <p className="min-w-0 break-all text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">{item.asset.name}</p>
        </div>
        {children}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

function ContextFact({ label, value }: { label: string; value: string }) {
  return (
    <div className="min-w-0">
      <p className="break-words text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">{label}</p>
      <p className="mt-1 break-words text-sm font-semibold [overflow-wrap:anywhere]">{value}</p>
    </div>
  )
}

function countBlankLeafValues(value: unknown): number {
  if (!value || typeof value !== "object" || Array.isArray(value)) return 0
  return Object.values(value).reduce((sum, child) => {
    if (child && typeof child === "object" && !Array.isArray(child)) {
      return sum + countBlankLeafValues(child)
    }
    return sum + (child === "" || child == null ? 1 : 0)
  }, 0)
}

function routeFromItem(item: BaselineRegressionItem): string {
  const result = item.expected_result ?? {}
  const source = result.source === "overseas" || result.site_scope === "overseas"
    ? "海外"
    : result.source === "domestic" || result.site_scope === "domestic"
      ? "国内"
      : ""
  const site = result.source === "overseas" || result.site_scope === "overseas" ? "overseas" : "domestic"
  const scope = result.asset_scope === "single" || result.asset_scope === "whole"
    ? scopeLabel(result.asset_scope)
    : scopeLabel(scopeFromIsSingle(site, result.is_single))
  return source && scope ? `${source} · ${scope}` : ""
}

function displayCompactValue(value: unknown): string {
  if (value === "" || value == null) return "暂未填写"
  if (typeof value === "string" || typeof value === "number" || typeof value === "boolean") return String(value)
  return JSON.stringify(value)
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/baseline-regression/export-button.tsx =====
import { useEffect, useState } from "react"
import { DownloadSimple } from "@phosphor-icons/react"
import { useMutation, useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query"
import { toast } from "sonner"

import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { baselineRegressionApi } from "@/lib/api"
import type { BaselineExportJob } from "@/lib/types"

/** 生成中每 2 秒问一次；这个任务通常几十秒，问太密没意义、太疏运营会以为卡住。 */
const POLL_MS = 2000

function sizeText(bytes: number): string {
  if (bytes <= 0) return ""
  const mb = bytes / 1024 / 1024
  return mb >= 1 ? `${mb.toFixed(1)} MB` : `${Math.round(bytes / 1024)} KB`
}

/**
 * 存量回归结果导出：一张表带缩略图和全部返回字段，模型输出与人工真值并排。
 *
 * 嵌缩略图要逐张读 NAS 原图（400 条约百秒），做不成同步下载，所以点完先建
 * 后台任务，这里轮询到完成再给下载。
 */
export function BaselineExportButton({ runId }: { runId: number }) {
  const [jobId, setJobId] = useState<number | null>(null)

  // 进页面先看有没有现成的：运营可能上次导完没下载，不该让他再等一轮。
  const existing = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["baseline-exports", runId],
    queryFn: () => baselineRegressionApi.listExports(runId),
  })

  const active = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["baseline-export", jobId],
    queryFn: () => baselineRegressionApi.getExport(jobId as number),
    enabled: jobId != null,
    refetchInterval: (query) => {
      const status = query.state.data?.status
      return status === "queued" || status === "running" ? POLL_MS : false
    },
  })

  const latest: BaselineExportJob | undefined =
    active.data ?? existing.data?.items?.[0]
  const running = latest?.status === "queued" || latest?.status === "running"

  // 页面打开时若已有任务在跑，接管它继续轮询
  useEffect(() => {
    const pending = existing.data?.items?.find(
      (item) => item.status === "queued" || item.status === "running",
    )
    if (pending && jobId == null) setJobId(pending.id)
  }, [existing.data, jobId])

  useEffect(() => {
    if (active.data?.status === "succeeded") {
      toast.success(
        `导出完成：${active.data.row_count} 条`
        + (active.data.missing_image_count
          ? `，${active.data.missing_image_count} 张缩略图读取失败`
          : ""),
      )
    }
    if (active.data?.status === "failed") {
      toast.error(active.data.error_message || "导出失败")
    }
  }, [active.data?.status])

  const start = useMutation({
    mutationFn: () => baselineRegressionApi.createExport(runId, true),
    onSuccess: (job) => {
      setJobId(job.id)
      if (job.status === "queued" || job.status === "running") {
        toast.info("正在生成，可以先做别的，好了会提示")
      }
    },
    onError: (error: Error) => toast.error(error.message),
  })

  const download = useMutation({
    mutationFn: (job: BaselineExportJob) =>
      baselineRegressionApi.downloadExport(job.id, job.file_name),
    onError: (error: Error) => toast.error(error.message),
  })

  const ready = latest?.status === "succeeded" && latest.file_available
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2" data-testid="baseline-export">
      <Button
        variant="secondary"
        size="sm"
        disabled={running || start.isPending}
        onClick={() => start.mutate()}
      >
        <DownloadSimple />
        {running ? "正在生成…" : "导出 Excel"}
      </Button>
      {ready && (
        <Button
          size="sm"
          disabled={download.isPending}
          onClick={() => download.mutate(latest)}
        >
          下载（{latest.row_count} 条{latest.file_bytes ? ` · ${sizeText(latest.file_bytes)}` : ""}）
        </Button>
      )}
      {running && (
        <span className="text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">
          带缩略图，几十秒到一两分钟
        </span>
      )}
      {latest?.status === "failed" && (
        <span className="text-xs text-[var(--danger)]">
          {latest.error_message || "导出失败"}
        </span>
      )}
      {latest?.status === "succeeded" && !latest.file_available && (
        <span className="text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">
          上次的文件已被新导出取代，请重新导出
        </span>
      )}
      {ready && latest.missing_image_count > 0 && (
        <span className="text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">
          {latest.missing_image_count} 张缩略图读取失败，已在「导出说明」页标注
        </span>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/baseline-regression/field-metrics-evidence.tsx =====
import type { BaselineFieldMetrics, BaselineRegressionRun } from "@/lib/types"
import { baselineRunMetricsView } from "@/features/baseline-regression/baseline-run-metrics"
import { Metric, percent } from "@/features/baseline-regression/regression-page-shared"
import { structuredFieldLabel } from "@/features/baseline-regression/structured-truth"

const truthPercentFormatter = new Intl.NumberFormat("zh-CN", {
  style: "percent",
  maximumFractionDigits: 2,
})

export function FieldMetricsEvidence({
  data,
  loading,
  error,
  levelMetrics,
}: {
  data?: BaselineFieldMetrics
  loading: boolean
  error: Error | null
  levelMetrics: BaselineRegressionRun["metrics"]
}) {
  const runMetricsView = baselineRunMetricsView(levelMetrics)
  const workflow = data?.versions.workflow
  const promptNodes = data?.versions.components?.prompt_nodes ?? []
  const ruleModules = data?.versions.components?.rule_modules ?? []
  if (loading) {
    return <div className="h-52 animate-pulse bg-alias-surface-042" />
  }
  if (error) {
    return (
      <div className="border border-alias-edge-036 bg-alias-surface-067 px-4 py-4 text-sm text-alias-content-041 rounded-md">
        字段质量证据加载失败：{error.message}
      </div>
    )
  }
  if (!data) {
    return <p className="text-sm text-[var(--muted)]">当前轮次尚未形成字段证据。</p>
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      <section className="visual-panel-group grid gap-px visual-panel border border-[var(--line)] bg-[var(--line)] grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-4">
        <Metric label="宏平均准确率" value={percent(data.aggregates.macro.accuracy)} />
        <Metric label="宏平均召回率" value={percent(data.aggregates.macro.recall)} />
        <Metric label="微平均准确率" value={percent(data.aggregates.micro.accuracy)} />
        <Metric label="失败样本" value={String(data.failure_sample_ids.length)} />
      </section>

      <section className="visual-panel border border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-white">
        <div className="grid grid-cols-[minmax(210px,1fr)_90px_110px_110px_100px] gap-3 border-b border-[var(--line)] bg-alias-surface-051 px-4 py-3 text-xs font-semibold text-[var(--muted)]">
          <span>字段</span><span>支持数</span><span>准确率</span><span>召回率</span><span>失败数</span>
        </div>
        {data.field_metrics.map((item) => (
          <details
            key={fieldMetricIdentityKey(item)}
            className="visual-native-details border-b border-[var(--line)] last:border-0"
            data-testid={`baseline-field-metric-${item.field_key}`}
          >
            <summary className="grid grid-cols-[minmax(210px,1fr)_90px_110px_110px_100px] gap-3 hover:bg-alias-surface-054">
              <span className="font-data break-all font-semibold">{fieldMetricLabel(item.field_key)}</span>
              <span className="font-data">{item.support}</span>
              <span className="font-data">{percent(item.accuracy)}</span>
              <span className="font-data">{percent(item.recall)}</span>
              <span className="font-data">{item.failure_sample_ids.length}</span>
            </summary>
            <div className="space-y-4 border-t border-[var(--line)] bg-alias-surface-058 px-4 py-4">
              {item.comparison_status === "incompatible_contracts" ? (
                <p className="border-l-2 border-alias-edge-026 bg-alias-surface-069 px-3 py-2 text-xs leading-5 text-alias-content-031">
                  同名字段来自多个不兼容输出合同，本轮只展示证据，不与其他版本做自动升降判断。
                </p>
              ) : item.field_identity ? (
                <p className="break-all text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">
                  字段身份：节点 <span className="visual-code">{item.field_identity.node_instance_id}</span> · 合同 <span className="visual-code">{item.field_identity.output_contract_hash.slice(0, 12)}</span> · 路径 {item.field_identity.field_path}
                  {item.field_identity.item_key ? ` · 子项 ${item.field_identity.item_key}` : ""}
                </p>
              ) : null}
              <div className="grid gap-3 grid-cols-3">
                <EvidenceMetric label="TP" value={item.tp} />
                <EvidenceMetric label="FP" value={item.fp} />
                <EvidenceMetric label="FN" value={item.fn} />
              </div>
              <div
                className="overflow-x-auto border border-[var(--line)] bg-white rounded-md"
                data-testid={item.field_key === "level" ? "baseline-five-level-confusion-matrix" : undefined}
              >
                <table className="w-full min-w-[520px] border-collapse text-left text-xs">
                  <thead><tr className="border-b border-[var(--line)] bg-alias-surface-051"><th className="px-3 py-2">人工真值</th><th className="px-3 py-2">模型输出</th><th className="px-3 py-2">样本数</th></tr></thead>
                  <tbody>
                    {Object.entries(item.confusion_matrix).flatMap(([expected, predictions]) => (
                      Object.entries(predictions).map(([predicted, count]) => (
                        <tr key={`${expected}:${predicted}`} className="border-b border-[var(--line)] last:border-0">
                          <td className="font-data px-3 py-2">{fieldMetricValue(expected)}</td>
                          <td className="font-data px-3 py-2">{fieldMetricValue(predicted)}</td>
                          <td className="font-data px-3 py-2">{count}</td>
                        </tr>
                      ))
                    ))}
                  </tbody>
                </table>
              </div>
              <p className="text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">
                失败样本：{item.failure_sample_ids.length ? item.failure_sample_ids.map((id) => `#${id}`).join("、") : "无"}
              </p>
            </div>
          </details>
        ))}
      </section>

      <section className="grid gap-4 border-y border-[var(--line)] bg-alias-surface-042 px-4 py-4 text-xs leading-5 grid-cols-2">
        <div>
          <p className="font-semibold">本轮版本</p>
          {workflow ? (
            <>
              <p className="mt-1 text-[var(--muted)]">
                完整评测机制 <span className="visual-code">{workflow.version || `#${workflow.workflow_version_id ?? "未记录"}`}</span>
              </p>
              {promptNodes.map((node) => (
                <div key={node.node_instance_id ?? node.asset_key} className="mt-2 border-l border-[var(--line-strong)] pl-3 text-[var(--muted)]">
                  <p className="font-medium text-[var(--ink)]">{node.display_name || "提示词步骤"}</p>
                  <p>版本 <span className="visual-code">{node.version || `#${node.version_id ?? "未记录"}`}</span> · 模型 <span className="visual-code">{node.model_id || "未记录"}</span></p>
                  <p>最大输出 {node.max_tokens ?? "未设置"} Token · {thinkingLevelLabel(node.thinking_level)}</p>
                </div>
              ))}
              {ruleModules.length ? (
                <p className="mt-2 text-[var(--muted)]">
                  规则模块 {ruleModules.map((node) => `${node.display_name || "未命名模块"} ${node.version || `#${node.version_id ?? "未记录"}`}`).join(" / ")}
                </p>
              ) : null}
            </>
          ) : (
            <>
              <p className="mt-1 text-[var(--muted)]">模型 {data.versions.model.join(" / ") || "未记录"}</p>
              <p className="text-[var(--muted)]">Prompt A {data.versions.prompt.a.join(" / ") || "未记录"}</p>
              <p className="text-[var(--muted)]">Prompt B {data.versions.prompt.b.join(" / ") || "未使用"}</p>
              <p className="text-[var(--muted)]">机制 {data.versions.mechanism.spec_version || "历史版本未记录"}</p>
            </>
          )}
        </div>
        <div>
          <p className="font-semibold">证据覆盖</p>
          <p className="mt-1 text-[var(--muted)]">
            素材 {data.versions.asset.count} 条 · {workflow ? "冻结字段真值" : "黄金真值匹配"} {data.versions.truth.matched_asset_count} 条
          </p>
          {workflow ? (
            <p className="break-all text-[var(--muted)]">
              字段合同 {(data.versions.truth.contract_keys ?? []).join(" / ") || "未记录"}
            </p>
          ) : (
            <p className="text-[var(--muted)]">真值修订 V{data.versions.truth.revision_min}–V{data.versions.truth.revision_max}</p>
          )}
          <p className="text-[var(--muted)]">
            {runMetricsView.kind === "structured_fields"
              ? `结构化字段准确率 ${runMetricsView.fieldAggregate ? truthPercentFormatter.format(runMetricsView.fieldAggregate.accuracy) : "—"} · 逐字段证据按当前冻结输出合同展示`
              : `等级准确率 ${runMetricsView.levelMetrics ? percent(runMetricsView.levelMetrics.exact_accuracy) : "—"} · 等级矩阵继续作为字段 level 的详细证据`}
          </p>
        </div>
      </section>

      <div className="border-l-2 border-primary bg-alias-surface-044 px-4 py-3 text-xs leading-5">
        指标仅作为人工决策证据，不会自动采纳或启用候选评测机制。人工需结合失败样本、黄金集门禁和回归结果另行决定。
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

function fieldMetricIdentityKey(item: BaselineFieldMetrics["field_metrics"][number]) {
  const identity = item.field_identity
  if (!identity) return `${item.field_key}:${item.comparison_status ?? "legacy"}`
  return [
    identity.node_instance_id,
    identity.output_contract_hash,
    identity.field_path,
    identity.item_key ?? "",
  ].join(":")
}

function thinkingLevelLabel(value: string | null) {
  const labels: Record<string, string> = {
    none: "不启用思考",
    low: "轻量思考",
    medium: "标准思考",
    high: "深度思考",
    auto: "自动选择思考强度",
  }
  return value ? labels[value] ?? value : "思考强度未设置"
}

export function EvidenceMetric({ label, value }: { label: string; value: number }) {
  return <div className="border border-[var(--line)] bg-white px-3 py-3 rounded-md"><p className="text-[var(--muted)]">{label}</p><p className="font-data mt-1 text-lg font-bold">{value}</p></div>
}

export function fieldMetricLabel(fieldKey: string) {
  const labels: Record<string, string> = {
    level: "正式等级",
    scope_status: "范围判定",
    primary_category: "主类目",
    quality_severity: "画质严重度",
  }
  if (fieldKey.startsWith("dimensions.")) return `维度 · ${fieldKey.slice("dimensions.".length)}`
  return labels[fieldKey] ?? structuredFieldLabel(fieldKey)
}

export function fieldMetricValue(value: string) {
  if (value === "__missing__") return "缺失"
  if (value === "__empty__") return "空值"
  return value
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/baseline-regression/form-selects.tsx =====
import type { BaselineLevel, PromptVersion } from "@/lib/types"
import { levels } from "@/features/baseline-regression/regression-page-shared"

export const levelNames: Record<BaselineLevel, string> = {
  L1: "好",
  L2: "中等",
  L3: "中差",
  L4: "极差",
  L5: "过滤",
}

export function LevelSelect({
  label,
  value,
  onChange,
}: {
  label: string
  value: BaselineLevel
  onChange: (value: BaselineLevel) => void
}) {
  return (
    <label>
      <span className="mb-2 block text-xs font-semibold">{label}</span>
      <select
        className="visual-native-select w-full"
        value={value}
        onChange={(event) => onChange(event.target.value as BaselineLevel)}
      >
        {levels.map((level) => (
          <option key={level} value={level}>{level} · {levelNames[level]}</option>
        ))}
      </select>
    </label>
  )
}

export function PromptSelect({
  label,
  value,
  options,
  published,
  disabled,
  onChange,
}: {
  label: string
  value: number
  options: PromptVersion[]
  published: PromptVersion | undefined
  disabled: boolean
  onChange: (value: number) => void
}) {
  return (
    <label>
      <span className="mb-2 block text-xs font-semibold">{label}</span>
      <select
        className="visual-native-select w-full disabled:border-[var(--line)] disabled:bg-alias-surface-014 disabled:text-[var(--muted)]"
        value={disabled ? (published?.id ?? "") : (value || "")}
        disabled={disabled}
        onChange={(event) => onChange(Number(event.target.value))}
      >
        {!published && disabled && (
          <option value="">当前发布版本未配置</option>
        )}
        {!options.length && !disabled && (
          <option value="">暂无可选版本</option>
        )}
        {options.map((prompt) => (
          <option key={prompt.id} value={prompt.id}>
            {prompt.version} · {prompt.name} · {promptStatusName(prompt.status)} · {promptScopeName(prompt.pipeline_scope)}
          </option>
        ))}
      </select>
    </label>
  )
}

export function promptStatusName(status: PromptVersion["status"]) {
  if (status === "published") return "已发布"
  if (status === "archived") return "已归档"
  return "草稿"
}

export function promptScopeName(scope: PromptVersion["pipeline_scope"]) {
  if (scope === "baseline_regression") return "基准回归专用"
  if (scope === "full_pipeline") return "完整流水线专用"
  return "共用"
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/baseline-regression/image-grade-drawer.tsx =====
import { useState } from "react"
import { ImagePreviewButton, type ImagePreview } from "@/components/image-lightbox"
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { SecondaryDrawer } from "@/components/workspace-page"
import {
  IMAGE_GRADE_LEVELS,
  type ImageGradeDraft,
  type ImageGradeLevel,
} from "@/features/baseline-regression/truth-edit"

const CASE_LEVELS = ["L1", "L2", "L3", "L4", "L5"] as const
const GRADE_LABEL: Record<string, string> = {
  L1: "好",
  L2: "中等",
  L3: "中差",
  L4: "极差",
  L5: "过滤",
}

/**
 * 图片维度真值编辑：案例一档，加上冻结清单里每一张图一档。
 *
 * 图片没有过滤档——运营的过滤是案例级判断，单张图片只分好 / 中等 / 中差 / 极差。
 * 冻结清单里的图片一张都不能漏：少一张就说明这份真值和要跑的图不是同一批，
 * 后端会拒绝，这里用未选计数把它提前暴露出来。
 */
export function ImageGradeDrawer({
  open,
  onOpenChange,
  caseName,
  draft,
  onChange,
}: {
  open: boolean
  onOpenChange: (open: boolean) => void
  caseName: string
  draft: ImageGradeDraft
  onChange: (draft: ImageGradeDraft) => void
}) {
  const [preview, setPreview] = useState<ImagePreview | null>(null)
  const pending = draft.images.filter((image) => !image.level).length
  const distribution = IMAGE_GRADE_LEVELS.map((level) => ({
    level,
    count: draft.images.filter((image) => image.level === level).length,
  })).filter((entry) => entry.count)

  function setImageLevel(cloudId: string, level: ImageGradeLevel) {
    onChange({
      ...draft,
      images: draft.images.map((image) =>
        image.cloudId === cloudId ? { ...image, level } : image,
      ),
    })
  }

  function setAllRemaining(level: ImageGradeLevel) {
    onChange({
      ...draft,
      images: draft.images.map((image) =>
        image.level ? image : { ...image, level },
      ),
    })
  }

  return (
    <>
    <SecondaryDrawer
      open={open}
      onOpenChange={onOpenChange}
      size="wide"
      title="填写逐图档位真值"
      description={`案例：${caseName || "当前案例"}。案例一档，冻结清单里每张图各一档；档位以人工判断为准，不从模型分推导。`}
      footer={(
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-4">
          <p className="text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">
            {pending
              ? `还有 ${pending} 张没有选档位，保存会被拒绝。`
              : "案例档位与全部逐图档位会一起追加为新一版真值，下一轮回归生效。"}
          </p>
          <Button onClick={() => onOpenChange(false)}>完成</Button>
        </div>
      )}
    >
      <div className="space-y-6">
        <section className="border-y border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-alias-surface-051 p-4">
          <h3 className="text-sm font-bold">案例档位</h3>
          <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">
            与样本目录的 好 / 中等 / 中差 / 极差 / 过滤 一一对应。过滤档只在案例级存在。
          </p>
          <div className="mt-3 flex flex-wrap gap-2" role="radiogroup" aria-label="案例档位">
            {CASE_LEVELS.map((level) => (
              <button
                key={level}
                type="button"
                role="radio"
                aria-checked={draft.caseLevel === level}
                className={"visual-native-button " + ("border px-3 " +
                  (draft.caseLevel === level
                    ? "border-alias-edge-016 bg-brand text-black"
                    : "border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-white hover:bg-alias-surface-009"))}
                onClick={() => onChange({ ...draft, caseLevel: level })}
              >
                {GRADE_LABEL[level]}（{level}）
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        </section>

        <section>
          <div className="mb-3 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
            <div>
              <h3 className="visual-card-title">逐图档位</h3>
              <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">
                共 {draft.images.length} 张
                {distribution.length
                  ? `：${distribution.map((entry) => `${GRADE_LABEL[entry.level]} ${entry.count}`).join(" · ")}`
                  : ""}
                {pending ? `；未选 ${pending} 张` : ""}
              </p>
            </div>
            {pending ? (
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <span className="text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">把未选的都设为</span>
                {IMAGE_GRADE_LEVELS.map((level) => (
                  <Button
                    key={level}
                    variant="secondary"
                    size="sm"
                    onClick={() => setAllRemaining(level)}
                  >
                    {GRADE_LABEL[level]}
                  </Button>
                ))}
              </div>
            ) : null}
          </div>
          <div className="space-y-2">
            {draft.images.map((image, index) => (
              <div
                key={image.cloudId}
                className={
                  "flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 border rounded-md p-3 "
                  + (image.level
                    ? "border-[var(--line)] bg-white"
                    : "border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-alias-surface-061")
                }
              >
                {image.imageUrl ? (
                  <ImagePreviewButton src={image.imageUrl} alt={`${caseName} · 图片 ${image.cloudId}`} imageClassName="h-24 w-32 object-contain bg-white" onPreview={setPreview} />
                ) : <span className="text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">暂无图片上下文</span>}
                <p className="font-data text-xs">
                  <span className="text-[var(--muted)]">#{index + 1}</span>{" "}
                  <span className="visual-code">{image.cloudId}</span>
                  {image.level ? null : <Badge tone="active">未选</Badge>}
                </p>
                <div
                  className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1"
                  role="radiogroup"
                  aria-label={`图片 ${image.cloudId} 档位`}
                >
                  {IMAGE_GRADE_LEVELS.map((level) => (
                    <button
                      key={level}
                      type="button"
                      role="radio"
                      aria-checked={image.level === level}
                      className={"visual-native-button " + ("border px-2 " +
                        (image.level === level
                          ? "border-alias-edge-016 bg-brand text-black"
                          : "border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-white hover:bg-alias-surface-009"))}
                      onClick={() => setImageLevel(image.cloudId, level)}
                    >
                      {GRADE_LABEL[level]}
                    </button>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </section>
      </div>
    </SecondaryDrawer>
    <SecondaryDrawer open={open && preview !== null} onOpenChange={(value) => { if (!value) setPreview(null) }} size="wide" title="图片原图" description={preview?.alt}>
      {preview && <img src={preview.src} alt={preview.alt} className="mx-auto h-auto max-w-full bg-white object-contain" />}
    </SecondaryDrawer>
    </>
  )
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/baseline-regression/inference-mode-field.tsx =====
import { inferenceModeFromOnlineSwitch } from "./inference-mode"

/** 存量回归启动配置里的「使用在线推理」开关。
 *
 * 只有开与关两种状态：关＝本批全程批量，开＝本批全程在线。不再提供「默认策略」这一档
 * ——运营在按下启动之前必须知道这一轮到底走哪条路，费用差一倍。
 */
export function InferenceModeField({
  useOnlineInference,
  onChange,
  disabled = false,
}: {
  useOnlineInference: boolean
  onChange: (useOnlineInference: boolean) => void
  disabled?: boolean
}) {
  const mode = inferenceModeFromOnlineSwitch(useOnlineInference)
  return (
    <div className="space-y-2" data-testid="inference-mode-field">
      <label className="visual-native-choice flex items-start gap-3 text-sm font-semibold">
        <input
          type="checkbox"
          role="switch"
          className="visual-native-checkbox mt-1 size-4"
          aria-label="使用在线推理"
          data-testid="use-online-inference"
          checked={useOnlineInference}
          disabled={disabled}
          onChange={(event) => onChange(event.target.checked)}
        />
        <span>
          使用在线推理
          <span className="mt-1 block text-xs font-normal leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">
            默认使用在线推理；本地 Qwen 仍使用本地模型。批量接入暂时空置，
            如需恢复批量，须先重新配置接入点。
          </span>
        </span>
      </label>
      <p className="text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]" data-testid="inference-mode-summary">
        本轮推理方式：{mode === "online" ? "在线推理" : "批量推理"}。启动后冻结，重试、恢复和断点续跑
        都沿用本轮冻结的这一种；之后改绑接入点不影响本轮。
      </p>
    </div>
  )
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/baseline-regression/inference-mode.ts =====
import type { RealExecutionPreview } from "./real-model-execution"

/** 存量回归一轮只有两种推理方式；界面上就是一个「使用在线推理」开关。 */
export type RegressionInferenceMode = "batch" | "online"

/** 云端默认在线；本地模型仍由冻结模型配置直接调用。 */
export const DEFAULT_USE_ONLINE_INFERENCE = true

export function inferenceModeFromOnlineSwitch(
  useOnlineInference: boolean,
): RegressionInferenceMode {
  return useOnlineInference ? "online" : "batch"
}

export function inferenceModeLabel(mode: RegressionInferenceMode): string {
  return mode === "online"
    ? "在线推理：即时返回，按在线价目计费"
    : "批量推理：费用较低，完成时间较长"
}

/** 一次预估请求的身份。世代号在每次入参变化时自增，决不回退。 */
export type EstimateRequest = {
  generation: number
  baselineSetId: number
  workflowVersionId: number
  mode: RegressionInferenceMode
}

export type EstimateState =
  | { status: "idle" }
  | { status: "pending"; request: EstimateRequest }
  | { status: "ready"; request: EstimateRequest; preview: RealExecutionPreview }
  | { status: "error"; request: EstimateRequest; message: string }

export type EstimateVerdict =
  | { accepted: true }
  | { accepted: false; reason: "stale_generation" }
  | { accepted: false; reason: "mode_mismatch"; returned: string }

/** 一条预估响应能不能用。
 *
 * 只比较响应里的 `inference_route.mode` 是不够的：批量→在线→批量地连点开关，第一次
 * 批量请求的迟到响应和当前这次批量请求的响应长得一模一样，比较 mode 会让过期报价冒充
 * 当前报价（ABA）。世代号只增不减，所以过期响应一定对不上当前世代。两条都要过。
 */
export function estimateAcceptance(
  request: EstimateRequest,
  currentGeneration: number,
  preview: RealExecutionPreview,
): EstimateVerdict {
  if (request.generation !== currentGeneration) {
    return { accepted: false, reason: "stale_generation" }
  }
  const returned = preview.inference_route?.mode
  if (returned !== undefined && returned !== request.mode) {
    return { accepted: false, reason: "mode_mismatch", returned }
  }
  return { accepted: true }
}

export function estimateModeMismatchMessage(
  request: EstimateRequest,
  returned: string,
): string {
  const name = (mode: string) => (mode === "batch" ? "批量推理" : "在线推理")
  return `服务端按${name(returned)}给出了报价，与当前选择的${name(request.mode)}不一致，已作废；请重新预估后再启动。`
}

/** 报价与启动按钮都只认这一个判定：当前世代已经拿到可用预估。 */
export function estimateIsCurrent(
  state: EstimateState,
  currentGeneration: number,
): state is { status: "ready"; request: EstimateRequest; preview: RealExecutionPreview } {
  return state.status === "ready" && state.request.generation === currentGeneration
}

export function displayableEstimate(
  state: EstimateState,
  currentGeneration: number,
): RealExecutionPreview | null {
  return estimateIsCurrent(state, currentGeneration) ? state.preview : null
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/baseline-regression/kg-route-codes.ts =====
/**
 * 知识图谱 is_single 的站点编码（与后端 knowledge_graph_contracts.IS_SINGLE_CODES 一致）。
 * asset_scope（整体/单体）才是正式口径，is_single 只是投影：
 * 国内 整体=0、单体=1；海外 整体=1、单体=2（2026-09-02 老大定，海外历史 0/1 由服务端自愈）。
 */
export type KnowledgeGraphSite = "domestic" | "overseas"
export type KnowledgeGraphScope = "whole" | "single"

const CODES: Record<KnowledgeGraphSite, Record<KnowledgeGraphScope, number>> = {
  domestic: { whole: 0, single: 1 },
  overseas: { whole: 1, single: 2 },
}

export function isSingleCode(site: KnowledgeGraphSite, scope: KnowledgeGraphScope): number {
  return CODES[site][scope]
}

export function scopeFromIsSingle(site: KnowledgeGraphSite | string | null | undefined, value: unknown): KnowledgeGraphScope | null {
  if (site !== "domestic" && site !== "overseas") return null
  const code = value === true ? 1 : value === false ? 0 : value
  if (typeof code !== "number") return null
  for (const scope of ["whole", "single"] as const) {
    if (CODES[site][scope] === code) return scope
  }
  return null
}

export function scopeLabel(scope: KnowledgeGraphScope | null): string {
  return scope === "single" ? "单体" : scope === "whole" ? "整体" : ""
}

export function isSingleOptions(site: KnowledgeGraphSite): Array<{ value: number; label: string; scope: KnowledgeGraphScope }> {
  return (["whole", "single"] as const).map((scope) => ({
    value: CODES[site][scope],
    scope,
    label: `${scope === "whole" ? "整体图" : "单体图"}（${CODES[site][scope]}）`,
  }))
}

/** 换站点时把整体/单体保持不变，只换编码。 */
export function remapIsSingle(from: KnowledgeGraphSite, to: KnowledgeGraphSite, value: number): number {
  const scope = scopeFromIsSingle(from, value) ?? "whole"
  return CODES[to][scope]
}

/**
 * 整体/单体判错时，运营直接在 is_single 字段里填对的值即可，平台会把它翻译成
 * 一次路线改判（合同、身份、可迁移字段一起切，下一轮按新分支重跑）。
 * 2026-09-03 老大定：纠偏页要能改，不能只读、也不能拿合同写死挡住运营。
 */
export function isSingleChangeHint(
  site: KnowledgeGraphSite | string | null | undefined,
  current: unknown,
): string | null {
  if (site !== "domestic" && site !== "overseas") return null
  const siteLabel = site === "overseas" ? "海外" : "国内"
  const scope = scopeFromIsSingle(site, current)
  const other = scope === "single" ? "whole" : "single"
  const otherCode = CODES[site][other]
  const otherLabel = other === "single" ? "单体" : "整体"
  return `${siteLabel}链路：${isSingleOptions(site).map((o) => `${o.value}=${o.scope === "whole" ? "整体" : "单体"}`).join("，")}。`
    + `改成 ${otherCode} 就是把这条素材改判为${siteLabel}${otherLabel}，下一轮按新分支重跑，本轮结果不改写。`
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/baseline-regression/level-explanation.tsx =====
import type { BaselineRegressionItem } from "@/lib/types"
import { percent } from "@/features/baseline-regression/regression-page-shared"
import { structuredFieldLabel } from "@/features/baseline-regression/structured-truth"

/**
 * 等级类真值（旧豆包灵感图 / 案例链路）本轮模型输出的业务字段。
 * 2026-09-04 运营反馈：这些字段（image_defects、tags、trait、seo_title…）
 * 一直存在库里，但结果页只展示六维评分与理由，运营看不到具体内容。
 * 等级/分数类字段由上方评分区展示，这里只补业务字段，避免重复。
 */
const LEVEL_SUMMARY_FIELDS = new Set([
  "score", "level", "aesthetic_score", "dimensions", "confidence",
  "recommendation_calibration",
])

function displayFieldValue(value: unknown): string {
  if (value === null || value === undefined) return "—"
  if (typeof value === "string") return value.trim() === "" ? "（空）" : value
  if (Array.isArray(value)) return value.length ? value.map((v) => typeof v === "object" && v !== null ? JSON.stringify(v) : String(v)).join("、") : "（空）"
  if (typeof value === "object") return JSON.stringify(value)
  return String(value)
}

export function ModelOutputFields({ item }: { item: BaselineRegressionItem }) {
  const raw = (item.normalized_result ?? {}) as Record<string, unknown>
  // 历史结果里同一字段名可能带尾随空格（1.8/2.0 关思考时返回过 "image_defects "），
  // 按字节精确匹配会漏渲染，这里统一去空格归一；同名冲突时保留非空值。
  const result: Record<string, unknown> = {}
  for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(raw)) {
    const normalized = key.trim()
    if (!normalized) continue
    const existing = result[normalized]
    const existingEmpty = existing === undefined || existing === null || existing === ""
    if (existingEmpty) result[normalized] = value
  }
  // 素材维度摘要既有结构化计数又有可读文本；表里只显示文本那一份，
  // 说法与运营样本表完全一致（「82分有3张,88分有4张」），不再糊一串 JSON。
  const fields = Object.keys(result)
    .filter((key) => !LEVEL_SUMMARY_FIELDS.has(key))
    .filter((key) => !(key.endsWith("_text") && `${key.slice(0, -5)}` in result))
    .filter((key) => key !== "material_summary_schema_version" && key !== "feature_texts")
    .sort()
  if (!fields.length) return null
  return (
    <div className="mt-4 border-t border-[var(--line)] pt-4" data-testid="model-output-fields">
      <p className="text-xs font-semibold">本轮模型输出字段</p>
      <div className="mt-2 overflow-x-auto visual-panel border border-[var(--line)] bg-white">
        <table className="w-full min-w-[420px] text-left text-xs">
          <thead className="bg-alias-surface-029 text-[var(--muted)]">
            <tr><th className="px-3 py-2">字段</th><th className="px-3 py-2">本轮输出</th></tr>
          </thead>
          <tbody className="divide-y divide-[var(--line)]">
            {fields.map((field) => {
              const readable = result[`${field}_text`]
              const featureTexts = field === "feature_counts" ? result.feature_texts : undefined
              const text = displayFieldValue(
                typeof readable === "string" && readable.trim()
                  ? readable
                  : featureTexts && typeof featureTexts === "object"
                    ? Object.values(featureTexts as Record<string, unknown>)
                        .filter((value) => typeof value === "string" && value.trim())
                        .join("；")
                    : result[field],
              )
              return (
                <tr key={field}>
                  <td className="px-3 py-2 font-semibold">
                    {structuredFieldLabel(field)}
                    <span className="ml-2 font-data text-xs font-normal text-[var(--muted)]">{field}</span>
                  </td>
                  <td className={`px-3 py-2 ${text === "（空）" ? "text-[var(--muted)]" : ""}`}>{text}</td>
                </tr>
              )
            })}
          </tbody>
        </table>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

export function LevelExplanation({ item, collapseModelFields = false }: { item: BaselineRegressionItem; collapseModelFields?: boolean }) {
  const modelFields = collapseModelFields ? <details className="visual-native-details mt-3 border-t border-[var(--line)]"><summary className="font-semibold">查看原始评分结果</summary><ModelOutputFields item={item} /></details> : <ModelOutputFields item={item} />
  const explanation = item.level_explanation
  const interpretation = item.interpretation
  const calibration = item.normalized_result?.recommendation_calibration
  const labels = Object.fromEntries(
    item.evaluation?.dimension_schema?.definition?.dimensions.map((dimension) => [
      dimension.key,
      dimension.label,
    ]) ?? [],
  )
  if (explanation.status === "unavailable_historical") {
    return (
      <div className="border-t border-[var(--line)] px-4 py-4">
        <p className="text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">
          {explanation.message ?? "历史结果未冻结评测理由"}
        </p>
        {modelFields}
      </div>
    )
  }
  const dimensionRows = (explanation.all_dimensions.length
    ? explanation.all_dimensions
    : [
        ...explanation.strong_dimensions,
        ...explanation.weak_dimensions,
      ].filter(
        (dimension, index, all) =>
          all.findIndex((candidate) => candidate.key === dimension.key) === index,
      )
  ).map((dimension) => ({
    ...dimension,
    kind: dimension.grade >= 4
      ? "主要优势"
      : dimension.grade <= 2
        ? "主要短板"
        : "中性维度",
  }))
  return (
    <div className="border-t border-[var(--line)] px-4 py-4">
      {interpretation?.status === "manual_required" ? (
        <div className="border-l-2 border-alias-edge-027 bg-alias-surface-077 px-3 py-2 text-xs leading-5 text-alias-content-033">
          <strong>自由输出已正常完成，等待人工判断。</strong>
          {interpretation.message ? ` ${interpretation.message}` : " 本次未形成可安全比较的 L1–L5 等级，因此不进入自动准确率分母。"}
        </div>
      ) : interpretation?.status === "rule_decided" ? (
        <div className="border-l-2 border-alias-edge-001 bg-alias-surface-017 px-3 py-2 text-xs leading-5 text-alias-content-002">
          <strong>服务端规则直接定级：{explanation.predicted_level ?? "—"}。</strong>
          {explanation.message ? ` ${explanation.message}` : " 命中短路规则后跳过了美感打分，没有服务端分数。"}
        </div>
      ) : (
        <p className="text-sm font-semibold">
          服务端结论：{explanation.predicted_level ?? "未形成等级"} ·
          {" "}{explanation.authoritative_score ?? "无有效分数"} 分
        </p>
      )}
      {calibration && (
        <p className="mt-2 text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">
          视觉证据校准：原始评分 {calibration.original_level} · {calibration.original_score} 分，
          校准后 {explanation.predicted_level ?? "—"} · {explanation.authoritative_score ?? "—"} 分。
          原始模型输出保留，可展开核对。
        </p>
      )}
      {(interpretation?.raw_text_a || interpretation?.raw_text_b) && (
        <div className="mt-4 grid gap-3 lg:grid-cols-2">
          {interpretation.raw_text_a && (
            <RawModelOutput label="调用 A 原始输出" value={interpretation.raw_text_a} />
          )}
          {interpretation.raw_text_b && (
            <RawModelOutput label="调用 B 原始输出" value={interpretation.raw_text_b} />
          )}
        </div>
      )}
      {explanation.status === "out_of_scope" && (
        <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-alias-content-041">素材超出评测范围，未形成正式美感等级。</p>
      )}
      {dimensionRows.length > 0 && (
        <div className="mt-4 divide-y divide-[var(--line)] border-y border-[var(--line)]">
          {dimensionRows.map((dimension) => (
            <div
              key={dimension.key}
              className="grid gap-2 py-3 text-xs sm:grid-cols-[88px_120px_minmax(0,1fr)]"
            >
              <span className="font-semibold text-[var(--muted)]">{dimension.kind}</span>
              <span className="font-semibold">
                {labels[dimension.key] ?? dimension.key} · {dimension.grade} 级
              </span>
              <span className="leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">
                {[...dimension.evidence, ...dimension.defects].join("；") || "未返回可展示证据"}
              </span>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
      <div className="mt-4 grid gap-3 border-y border-[var(--line)] py-3 text-xs sm:grid-cols-2">
        <p>
          <span className="font-semibold text-[var(--muted)]">模型置信度：</span>
          {item.confidence === null || item.confidence === undefined
            ? "未返回"
            : percent(item.confidence)}
        </p>
        <p>
          <span className="font-semibold text-[var(--muted)]">复核标记：</span>
          {item.needs_review === true
            ? "建议人工复核"
            : item.needs_review === false
              ? "未触发复核"
              : "未记录"}
        </p>
        <p>
          <span className="font-semibold text-[var(--muted)]">画质：</span>
          {explanation.image_quality.status === "available"
            ? `${explanation.image_quality.severity_label || explanation.image_quality.severity || "已返回"}${explanation.image_quality.confidence === null || explanation.image_quality.confidence === undefined ? "" : ` · ${percent(explanation.image_quality.confidence)} 置信度`}`
            : "未返回画质证据"}
        </p>
        <p>
          <span className="font-semibold text-[var(--muted)]">版本：</span>
          {[item.versions.model, item.versions.rubric, item.versions.engine]
            .filter(Boolean)
            .join(" · ") || "历史结果未记录"}
        </p>
      </div>
      {explanation.image_quality.evidence.length > 0 && (
        <p className="mt-2 text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">
          画质证据：{explanation.image_quality.evidence.join("；")}
        </p>
      )}
      {explanation.caps.length > 0 && (
        <p className="mt-3 text-xs leading-5 text-alias-content-033">
          等级限制：{explanation.caps.map((cap) => {
            const level = typeof cap.cap === "string" ? cap.cap : ""
            const reason = typeof cap.reason === "string" ? cap.reason : "触发等级限制"
            return [level, reason].filter(Boolean).join(" · ")
          }).join("；")}
        </p>
      )}
      {explanation.review_reasons.length > 0 && (
        <p className="mt-2 text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">
          建议人工复核：{explanation.review_reasons.join("；")}
        </p>
      )}
      {modelFields}
    </div>
  )
}

export function levelExplanationSummary(item: BaselineRegressionItem) {
  if (item.interpretation?.status === "manual_required") {
    return "自由输出已完整保存；未强制转换为八维或等级，等待人工判断"
  }
  if (item.interpretation?.status === "rule_decided") {
    return item.level_explanation.message ?? "命中短路规则，由服务端直接定级"
  }
  const explanation = item.level_explanation
  if (explanation.status === "unavailable_historical") {
    return explanation.message ?? "历史结果未冻结评测理由"
  }
  if (explanation.status === "out_of_scope") {
    return "超出评测范围，未形成正式等级"
  }
  const weakest = explanation.weak_dimensions[0]
  const weakEvidence = weakest
    ? [...weakest.defects, ...weakest.evidence][0]
    : null
  const cap = explanation.caps[0]
  const capReason = cap && typeof cap.reason === "string" ? cap.reason : null
  return capReason
    ? `等级受限：${capReason}`
    : weakEvidence
      ? `主要短板：${weakEvidence}`
      : `服务端按 ${explanation.authoritative_score ?? "—"} 分判定为 ${explanation.predicted_level ?? "未定级"}`
}

export function RawModelOutput({ label, value }: { label: string; value: string }) {
  return (
    <div className="min-w-0 visual-panel border border-[var(--line)] bg-white px-3 py-3">
      <p className="text-xs font-bold text-[var(--muted)]">{label}</p>
      <pre className="font-data mt-2 max-h-64 overflow-auto whitespace-pre-wrap break-words text-xs leading-5 text-foreground">{value}</pre>
    </div>
  )
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/baseline-regression/level-metrics.ts =====
import type { BaselineLevel, BaselineLevelMetrics } from "../../lib/types"

export const BASELINE_LEVELS: readonly BaselineLevel[] = ["L1", "L2", "L3", "L4", "L5"]

export type BaselineLevelMatrixMetrics = {
  levels: readonly BaselineLevel[]
  rowTotals: Record<BaselineLevel, number>
  columnTotals: Record<BaselineLevel, number>
  recallByLevel: Record<BaselineLevel, number | null>
  precisionByLevel: Record<BaselineLevel, number | null>
  cells: Array<{ expected: BaselineLevel; predicted: BaselineLevel; count: number }>
}

export function computeBaselineLevelMatrixMetrics(
  metrics: Pick<BaselineLevelMetrics, "confusion_matrix">,
): BaselineLevelMatrixMetrics {
  const rowTotals = emptyLevelRecord()
  const columnTotals = emptyLevelRecord()
  const diagonal = emptyLevelRecord()
  const cells: BaselineLevelMatrixMetrics["cells"] = []

  for (const expected of BASELINE_LEVELS) {
    for (const predicted of BASELINE_LEVELS) {
      const count = metrics.confusion_matrix?.[expected]?.[predicted] ?? 0
      cells.push({ expected, predicted, count })
      rowTotals[expected] += count
      columnTotals[predicted] += count
      if (expected === predicted) diagonal[expected] += count
    }
  }

  const recallByLevel = emptyNullableLevelRecord()
  const precisionByLevel = emptyNullableLevelRecord()
  for (const level of BASELINE_LEVELS) {
    recallByLevel[level] = rowTotals[level] ? diagonal[level] / rowTotals[level] : null
    precisionByLevel[level] = columnTotals[level] ? diagonal[level] / columnTotals[level] : null
  }

  return { levels: BASELINE_LEVELS, rowTotals, columnTotals, recallByLevel, precisionByLevel, cells }
}

function emptyLevelRecord(): Record<BaselineLevel, number> {
  return { L1: 0, L2: 0, L3: 0, L4: 0, L5: 0 }
}

function emptyNullableLevelRecord(): Record<BaselineLevel, number | null> {
  return { L1: null, L2: null, L3: null, L4: null, L5: null }
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/baseline-regression/level-performance-summary.tsx =====
import type { ReactNode } from "react"
import type { BaselineLevel, BaselineLevelMetrics } from "@/lib/types"
import { BASELINE_LEVELS, computeBaselineLevelMatrixMetrics } from "./level-metrics"

export type BaselineLevelBucketMetric = {
  key: "recommended" | "regular" | "filtered"
  label: "推荐档" | "常规档" | "过滤档"
  levels: readonly BaselineLevel[]
  truePositive: number
  predicted: number
  expected: number
  precision: number | null
  recall: number | null
}

type BucketDefinition = Pick<
  BaselineLevelBucketMetric,
  "key" | "label" | "levels"
>

const buckets: readonly BucketDefinition[] = [
  { key: "recommended", label: "推荐档", levels: ["L1", "L2"] },
  { key: "regular", label: "常规档", levels: ["L3", "L4"] },
  { key: "filtered", label: "过滤档", levels: ["L5"] },
]

const percentFormatter = new Intl.NumberFormat("zh-CN", {
  style: "percent",
  maximumFractionDigits: 2,
})

export function computeBaselineLevelBucketMetrics(
  metrics: BaselineLevelMetrics,
): BaselineLevelBucketMetric[] {
  return buckets.map((bucket) => {
    const bucketLevels = new Set<BaselineLevel>(bucket.levels)
    let truePositive = 0
    let predicted = 0
    let expected = 0

    for (const expectedLevel of metrics.levels) {
      for (const predictedLevel of metrics.levels) {
        const count = metrics.confusion_matrix[expectedLevel]?.[predictedLevel] ?? 0
        if (bucketLevels.has(predictedLevel)) predicted += count
        if (bucketLevels.has(expectedLevel)) expected += count
        if (bucketLevels.has(expectedLevel) && bucketLevels.has(predictedLevel)) {
          truePositive += count
        }
      }
    }

    return {
      ...bucket,
      truePositive,
      predicted,
      expected,
      precision: predicted ? truePositive / predicted : null,
      recall: expected ? truePositive / expected : null,
    }
  })
}

export function LevelPerformanceSummary({ metrics, actions }: { metrics: BaselineLevelMetrics; actions?: ReactNode }) {
  const bucketMetrics = computeBaselineLevelBucketMetrics(metrics)
  const matrixMetrics = computeBaselineLevelMatrixMetrics(metrics)
  // 准确率的分母是有效预测数，不是样本总数。不显示分母时，一个 2/100 有效的
  // 回归会呈现成"准确率 100%"，看不出这个数字只由 2 条样本得出。
  const unscored = metrics.unscored ?? 0
  const basisHint =
    metrics.valid_predictions < metrics.total
      ? `基于 ${metrics.valid_predictions}/${metrics.total} 条有效预测` +
        (unscored ? `，${unscored} 条未评级` : "")
      : `基于全部 ${metrics.total} 条`

  return (
    <section className="space-y-5" aria-label="等级表现">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-end justify-between gap-3">
        <div>
          <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-[var(--muted)]">当前回归任务</p>
          <h3 className="mt-1 visual-card-title">等级表现</h3>
        </div>
        <div className="space-y-2">{actions}<p className="text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">三档指标为 L1–L5 五档矩阵的附加聚合，不替代原矩阵</p></div>
      </div>

      <div className="grid items-stretch gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2">
        <MetricCard
          label="精确等级准确率"
          value={formatPercent(metrics.exact_accuracy)}
          testId="baseline-exact-accuracy"
          hint={basisHint}
        />
        <MetricCard
          label="相邻等级准确率"
          value={formatPercent(metrics.adjacent_accuracy)}
          testId="baseline-adjacent-accuracy"
          hint={basisHint}
        />
      </div>

      <div className="grid items-stretch gap-3 md:grid-cols-3">
        {bucketMetrics.map((bucket) => (
          <div
            key={bucket.key}
            className="baseline-metric min-w-0 rounded-md border border-line bg-surface-sunken px-5 py-4"
            data-testid={`baseline-bucket-${bucket.key}`}
          >
            <div className="flex items-baseline justify-between gap-3">
              <p className="text-sm font-bold">{bucket.label}</p>
              <p className="font-data text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">{bucket.levels.join("–")}</p>
            </div>
            <div className="mt-4 grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
              <BucketValue label="精确率" value={formatPercent(bucket.precision)} />
              <BucketValue label="召回率" value={formatPercent(bucket.recall)} />
            </div>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>

      <section className="space-y-3" aria-label="L1–L5 等级分布矩阵" data-testid="baseline-level-matrix">
        <div>
          <p className="text-sm font-bold">L1–L5 等级分布矩阵</p>
          <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">行是人工真值，列是模型预测；行末为召回率，底部为单档准确率（Precision）。</p>
        </div>
        <div className="overflow-x-auto border border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-white rounded-md">
          <div className="min-w-[620px]">
            <div className="grid grid-cols-[88px_repeat(5,minmax(62px,1fr))_96px] border-b border-[var(--line)] bg-alias-surface-051 text-xs font-semibold text-[var(--muted)]">
              <div className="px-3 py-3">真值＼预测</div>
              {BASELINE_LEVELS.map((level) => <div key={`header-${level}`} className="px-3 py-3 text-center">{level}</div>)}
              <div className="px-3 py-3 text-center">召回率</div>
            </div>
            {BASELINE_LEVELS.map((expected) => (
              <div key={`row-${expected}`} className="grid grid-cols-[88px_repeat(5,minmax(62px,1fr))_96px] border-b border-[var(--line)] last:border-0">
                <div className="flex items-center px-3 py-3 text-xs font-semibold">{expected}</div>
                {BASELINE_LEVELS.map((predicted) => {
                  const cell = matrixMetrics.cells.find((item) => item.expected === expected && item.predicted === predicted)
                  const count = cell?.count ?? 0
                  return (
                    <div
                      key={`${expected}-${predicted}`}
                      className={`flex items-center justify-center border-l border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-3 font-data text-sm ${expected === predicted && count > 0 ? "bg-alias-surface-016 font-bold" : ""}`}
                      data-testid={`baseline-level-cell-${expected}-${predicted}`}
                      aria-label={`${expected} 真值、${predicted} 预测：${count} 条`}
                    >
                      {count}
                    </div>
                  )
                })}
                <div className="flex items-center justify-center border-l border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-3 font-data text-sm" data-testid={`baseline-level-recall-${expected}`}>
                  {formatPercent(matrixMetrics.recallByLevel[expected])}
                </div>
              </div>
            ))}
            <div className="grid grid-cols-[88px_repeat(5,minmax(62px,1fr))_96px] bg-alias-surface-051 text-xs">
              <div className="px-3 py-3 font-semibold">准确率（Precision）</div>
              {BASELINE_LEVELS.map((level) => <div key={`precision-${level}`} className="border-l border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-3 text-center font-data" data-testid={`baseline-level-precision-${level}`}>{formatPercent(matrixMetrics.precisionByLevel[level])}</div>)}
              <div className="border-l border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-3 text-center text-[var(--muted)]">—</div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>
    </section>
  )
}

function MetricCard({
  label,
  value,
  testId,
  hint,
}: {
  label: string
  value: string
  testId: string
  hint?: string
}) {
  return (
    <div className="baseline-metric min-w-0 rounded-md border border-line bg-surface-sunken px-5 py-4">
      <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-[var(--muted)]">{label}</p>
      <p className="font-data mt-2 text-2xl font-bold" data-testid={testId}>{value}</p>
      {hint ? (
        <p className="font-data mt-1 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">{hint}</p>
      ) : null}
    </div>
  )
}

function BucketValue({ label, value }: { label: string; value: string }) {
  return (
    <div>
      <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-[var(--muted)]">{label}</p>
      <p className="font-data mt-1 text-xl font-bold">{value}</p>
    </div>
  )
}

function formatPercent(value: number | null) {
  return value === null ? "—" : percentFormatter.format(value)
}
