===== FILE frontend/src/features/baseline-regression/native-correction-summary.ts =====
export type NativeCorrectionAffectedNode = {
  nodeInstanceId: string
  assetKey: string
  componentKind: string
  fields: string[]
  evidenceCount: number
}

export type NativeCorrectionRecommendation = {
  nodeInstanceId: string
  assetKey: string
  sourceVersionId: number
  candidateVersionId: number
  mode: string
}

export type NativeWorkflowCorrectionSummary = {
  baselineWorkflowVersionId: number | null
  candidateWorkflowVersionId: number | null
  affectedNodes: NativeCorrectionAffectedNode[]
  recommendations: NativeCorrectionRecommendation[]
  baselineAccuracy: number | null
  candidateAccuracy: number | null
  accuracyDelta: number | null
  accuracyKind: "level" | "field" | null
  recommendation: string
  approvalAllowed: boolean
  incrementalPointerChanged: boolean | null
}

type CorrectionLike = {
  candidate_workflow_version_id?: unknown
  report?: unknown
  orchestration?: unknown
}

export function nativeWorkflowCorrectionSummary(
  correction: CorrectionLike,
): NativeWorkflowCorrectionSummary | null {
  const report = recordValue(correction.report)
  if (!String(report.schema_version ?? "").startsWith("native-workflow-")) return null
  const groupsByNode = new Map<string, NativeCorrectionAffectedNode>()
  for (const group of recordArray(report.groups)) {
    const nodeInstanceId = stringValue(group.node_instance_id)
    if (!nodeInstanceId) continue
    const current = groupsByNode.get(nodeInstanceId) ?? {
      nodeInstanceId,
      assetKey: stringValue(group.asset_key),
      componentKind: stringValue(group.component_kind),
      fields: [],
      evidenceCount: 0,
    }
    const fieldPath = stringValue(group.field_path)
    if (fieldPath && !current.fields.includes(fieldPath)) current.fields.push(fieldPath)
    current.evidenceCount += numberValue(group.evidence_count) ?? 0
    groupsByNode.set(nodeInstanceId, current)
  }
  const recommendations = recordArray(report.recommendations).flatMap(
    (recommendation): NativeCorrectionRecommendation[] => {
      const nodeInstanceId = stringValue(recommendation.node_instance_id)
      const sourceVersionId = numberValue(recommendation.source_version_id)
      const candidateVersionId = numberValue(recommendation.candidate_version_id)
      if (!nodeInstanceId || sourceVersionId === null || candidateVersionId === null) return []
      return [{
        nodeInstanceId,
        assetKey: stringValue(recommendation.asset_key),
        sourceVersionId,
        candidateVersionId,
        mode: stringValue(recommendation.mode),
      }]
    },
  )
  const regression = recordValue(report.candidate_regression)
  const orchestration = recordValue(correction.orchestration)
  const nativeCandidate = recordValue(orchestration.native_candidate)
  const baselineAccuracy = primaryAccuracy(regression.baseline_metrics)
  const candidateAccuracy = primaryAccuracy(regression.candidate_metrics)
  return {
    baselineWorkflowVersionId: numberValue(
      regression.baseline_workflow_version_id ?? report.baseline_workflow_version_id,
    ),
    candidateWorkflowVersionId: numberValue(
      correction.candidate_workflow_version_id
      ?? regression.candidate_workflow_version_id
      ?? nativeCandidate.candidate_workflow_version_id,
    ),
    affectedNodes: [...groupsByNode.values()],
    recommendations,
    baselineAccuracy,
    candidateAccuracy,
    accuracyDelta: baselineAccuracy !== null && candidateAccuracy !== null
      ? candidateAccuracy - baselineAccuracy
      : numberValue(regression.primary_accuracy_delta),
    accuracyKind: primaryAccuracyKind(
      regression.baseline_metrics,
      regression.candidate_metrics,
    ),
    recommendation: stringValue(regression.recommendation),
    approvalAllowed: regression.approval_allowed === true,
    incrementalPointerChanged: typeof regression.incremental_pointer_changed === "boolean"
      ? regression.incremental_pointer_changed
      : null,
  }
}

function primaryAccuracy(value: unknown): number | null {
  const metrics = recordValue(value)
  const levelMetrics = recordValue(metrics.level_metrics)
  const levelAccuracy = numberValue(levelMetrics.exact_accuracy)
  if (levelAccuracy !== null) return levelAccuracy
  const fieldAggregate = recordValue(metrics.field_aggregate)
  const fieldAccuracy = numberValue(fieldAggregate.accuracy)
  if (fieldAccuracy !== null) return fieldAccuracy
  return numberValue(metrics.exact_accuracy)
}

function primaryAccuracyKind(
  baselineValue: unknown,
  candidateValue: unknown,
): "level" | "field" | null {
  for (const value of [baselineValue, candidateValue]) {
    const metrics = recordValue(value)
    const levelMetrics = recordValue(metrics.level_metrics)
    if (numberValue(levelMetrics.exact_accuracy) !== null) return "level"
    const fieldAggregate = recordValue(metrics.field_aggregate)
    if (numberValue(fieldAggregate.accuracy) !== null) return "field"
  }
  return null
}

function recordValue(value: unknown): Record<string, unknown> {
  return value && typeof value === "object" && !Array.isArray(value)
    ? value as Record<string, unknown>
    : {}
}

function recordArray(value: unknown): Array<Record<string, unknown>> {
  return Array.isArray(value) ? value.map(recordValue).filter((item) => Object.keys(item).length) : []
}

function stringValue(value: unknown): string {
  return typeof value === "string" ? value : ""
}

function numberValue(value: unknown): number | null {
  return typeof value === "number" && Number.isFinite(value) ? value : null
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/baseline-regression/real-model-evidence.tsx =====
import type { CorrectionContractNode } from "@/features/correction-contract/types"

type ModelInvocationEvidence = {
  schema_version?: string
  provider?: string
  model_id?: string
  request_correlation_id?: string | null
  upstream_status_code?: number | null
  input_tokens?: number
  output_tokens?: number
  total_tokens?: number
  attempt_count?: number
  output_budget?: number
  thinking_mode?: string
  reasoning_effort?: string | null
  component_hash?: string
  model_snapshot_hash?: string
  raw_payload?: unknown
  parsed_output?: unknown
}

type NodeInvocationEvidence = {
  nodeInstanceId: string
  label: string
  runtimeStatus: string
  invocation: ModelInvocationEvidence
}

function record(value: unknown): Record<string, unknown> {
  return value && typeof value === "object" && !Array.isArray(value)
    ? value as Record<string, unknown>
    : {}
}

export function modelInvocationEvidenceFromNodes(
  nodes: CorrectionContractNode[],
): NodeInvocationEvidence[] {
  const byNode = new Map<string, NodeInvocationEvidence>()
  for (const node of nodes) {
    const execution = record(node.execution_evidence)
    const invocation = record(execution.model_invocation)
    const nodeInstanceId = typeof execution.node_instance_id === "string"
      ? execution.node_instance_id
      : ""
    if (!nodeInstanceId || !Object.keys(invocation).length || byNode.has(nodeInstanceId)) continue
    byNode.set(nodeInstanceId, {
      nodeInstanceId,
      label: node.label || nodeInstanceId,
      runtimeStatus: typeof execution.runtime_status === "string"
        ? execution.runtime_status
        : "unknown",
      invocation: invocation as ModelInvocationEvidence,
    })
  }
  return [...byNode.values()]
}

function valueOrDash(value: unknown) {
  return value === null || value === undefined || value === "" ? "—" : String(value)
}

function shortHash(value: unknown) {
  if (typeof value !== "string" || !value) return "—"
  return value.length > 18 ? `${value.slice(0, 10)}…${value.slice(-6)}` : value
}

export function RealModelEvidence({ nodes }: { nodes: CorrectionContractNode[] }) {
  const invocations = modelInvocationEvidenceFromNodes(nodes)
  if (!invocations.length) return null

  return (
    <section className="visual-panel border border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-white px-5 py-5 md:px-7" aria-label="真实模型调用证据">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-3">
        <div>
          <h3 className="visual-card-title">真实模型调用证据</h3>
          <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">
            以下内容来自本轮冻结节点，只用于核对实际调用的模型、版本与返回结果；敏感凭据不会展示。
          </p>
        </div>
        <span className="border border-alias-edge-035 bg-alias-surface-044 px-2 py-1 text-xs font-semibold text-alias-content-012 rounded-md">
          {invocations.length} 个真实提示词节点
        </span>
      </div>
      <div className="mt-4 divide-y divide-[var(--line)] border-y border-[var(--line)]">
        {invocations.map(({ nodeInstanceId, label, runtimeStatus, invocation }) => (
          <article key={nodeInstanceId} className="py-4">
            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
              <div>
                <p className="text-sm font-bold">{label}</p>
                <p className="font-data mt-1 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]"><span className="visual-code">{nodeInstanceId}</span> · {runtimeStatus}</p>
              </div>
              <p className="text-xs font-semibold">{valueOrDash(invocation.provider)} · <span className="visual-code">{valueOrDash(invocation.model_id)}</span></p>
            </div>
            <dl className="mt-3 grid gap-px bg-[var(--line)] sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-4">
              <EvidenceFact label="请求标识" value={valueOrDash(invocation.request_correlation_id)} />
              <EvidenceFact label="输入 token" value={valueOrDash(invocation.input_tokens)} />
              <EvidenceFact label="输出 token" value={valueOrDash(invocation.output_tokens)} />
              <EvidenceFact label="调用次数" value={valueOrDash(invocation.attempt_count)} />
              <EvidenceFact label="提示词版本哈希" value={shortHash(invocation.component_hash)} />
              <EvidenceFact label="模型配置哈希" value={shortHash(invocation.model_snapshot_hash)} />
              <EvidenceFact label="思考模式" value={valueOrDash(invocation.reasoning_effort ?? invocation.thinking_mode)} />
              <EvidenceFact label="服务状态" value={valueOrDash(invocation.upstream_status_code)} />
            </dl>
            <details className="visual-native-details mt-3 border rounded-md border-[var(--line)] bg-alias-surface-051">
              <summary className="font-semibold">原始响应（已脱敏）</summary>
              <pre className="max-h-80 overflow-auto border-t border-[var(--line)] p-3 text-xs leading-5">
                {JSON.stringify(invocation.raw_payload ?? invocation.parsed_output ?? {}, null, 2)}
              </pre>
            </details>
          </article>
        ))}
      </div>
    </section>
  )
}

function EvidenceFact({ label, value }: { label: string; value: string }) {
  return (
    <div className="min-w-0 bg-white px-3 py-2">
      <dt className="text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">{label}</dt>
      <dd className="font-data mt-1 truncate text-xs font-semibold" title={value}>{value}</dd>
    </div>
  )
}

===== FILE frontend/src/features/baseline-regression/real-model-execution.ts =====
import type { BaselineAnchorUsage } from "../../lib/types"

export type ModelExecutionMode = "simulated" | "real"

export type RealExecutionBudget = {
  expected_requests: number
  expected_input_tokens: number
  expected_output_tokens: number
  expected_cost_micros: number
  max_requests: number
  max_input_tokens: number
  max_output_tokens: number
  max_cost_micros: number
}

/** 「预计输出」的依据：优先同提示词版本的近期实际 p95，其次同节点，最后才按上限。 */
export type ExpectedOutputBasis = {
  source: "prompt_history" | "node_history" | "output_cap"
  sample_size: number
  p95_output_tokens: number | null
  output_tokens_per_request: number
  output_cap_per_request: number
}

export type ExpectedOutputSource = "history" | "mixed" | "output_cap"

/** 服务端返回的用量预估与硬上限。上限由服务端持有（可被环境变量调整），
 *  前端不再自带副本——否则改了服务端，界面还在展示过期的数字。 */
export type RealExecutionPreview = {
  anchor_usage?: BaselineAnchorUsage
  inference_route?: {mode: "online" | "batch"; endpoint_id: string | null}
  item_count: number
  expected: {
    requests: number
    input_tokens: number
    output_tokens: number
    cost_micros: number
    cost_basis?: "cold_cost_upper_bound"
    image_tokens?: number | null
    image_tokens_included_in_input?: boolean
  }
  retry_reserve?: {
    requests: number
    input_tokens: number
    output_tokens: number
    cost_micros: number
  }
  expected_output_source?: ExpectedOutputSource
  node_pricing?: Array<{
    node_instance_id: string
    provider: string
    model_id: string
    input_tokens_per_request: number
    output_tokens_per_request: number
    estimated_cost_micros_per_request: number
    pricing_version: string
    pricing_tier_key: string
    expected_cost_basis?: "cold_cost_upper_bound"
    expected_input_tokens_per_request?: number | null
    expected_output_tokens_per_request?: number | null
    expected_cost_micros_per_request?: number | null
    expected_pricing_tier_key?: string | null
    expected_request_count?: number | null
    expected_input_tokens?: number | null
    expected_output_tokens?: number | null
    expected_cost_micros?: number | null
    expected_tier_buckets?: Array<{
      pricing_tier_key: string
      request_count: number
      input_tokens: number
      output_tokens: number
      cost_micros: number
    }> | null
    configured_max_attempts?: number | null
    retry_reserve_request_count?: number | null
    expected_output_basis?: ExpectedOutputBasis | null
    pricing_tiers?: Array<{
      tier_key: string
      min_input_tokens: number
      max_input_tokens: number
      input_micros_per_1k: number
      cached_input_micros_per_1k: number | null
      output_micros_per_1k: number
    }>
    source_url: string | null
    effective_at: string | null
  }>
  caps: {
    max_requests: number
    max_input_tokens: number
    max_output_tokens: number
    max_cost_micros: number
  }
  exceeded: string[]
  within_caps: boolean
  /** 本轮真实调用将走的豆包推理接入点；路由开关打开且缺失时 blocker 有值 */
  doubao_endpoint?: {
    routing_enabled: boolean
    required: boolean
    binding: { id: number; endpoint_id: string; display_name: string; status: string; verification_status: string; foundation_model: string } | null
    blocker: string | null
  }
}

/** 费用以百万分之一元（micros）计。 */
export function formatCny(micros: number): string {
  const yuan = micros / 1_000_000
  if (yuan > 0 && yuan < 0.01) return "不足 0.01 元"
  return `${yuan.toLocaleString("zh-CN", { minimumFractionDigits: 2, maximumFractionDigits: 2 })} 元`
}

const fmt = (value: number) => value.toLocaleString("zh-CN")

/** 顶部「输出 token」旁的一句话依据；上限是预留，不再冒充预计。 */
export function expectedOutputSourceLabel(source: ExpectedOutputSource | undefined): string {
  switch (source) {
    case "history":
      return "按近期实际用量 p95 预计；输出上限计入重试预留"
    case "mixed":
      return "部分节点无历史用量，按输出上限预计；其余按近期实际 p95"
    default:
      return "暂无可参考的实际用量，按输出上限预计（实际通常远低于此）"
  }
}

export function expectedOutputBasisLabel(basis: ExpectedOutputBasis | null | undefined): string {
  if (!basis) return "输出依据：按输出上限"
  const cap = fmt(basis.output_cap_per_request)
  if (basis.source === "output_cap") {
    return `输出依据：无可参考历史（样本 ${fmt(basis.sample_size)} 次），按上限 ${cap} 预计`
  }
  const p95 = basis.p95_output_tokens == null ? "—" : fmt(basis.p95_output_tokens)
  const who = basis.source === "prompt_history" ? "同提示词版本" : "同节点（提示词已改版）"
  return `输出依据：${who}近 ${fmt(basis.sample_size)} 次实际 p95 ≈ ${p95}，上限 ${cap} 作预留`
}

const CAP_LABELS: Record<string, string> = {
  requests: "调用次数",
  input_tokens: "输入 token",
  output_tokens: "输出 token",
  cost: "费用",
}

export function exceededCapLabels(exceeded: string[]): string {
  return exceeded.map((key) => CAP_LABELS[key] ?? key).join("、")
}

export function realExecutionCanStart(
  mode: ModelExecutionMode,
  confirmed: boolean,
): boolean {
  return mode === "simulated" || confirmed
}

export function modelExecutionSummary(mode: ModelExecutionMode) {
  return mode === "real"
    ? {
        label: "真实模型评测",
        description: "按当前工作流冻结的提示词、模型和运行参数调用豆包，结果可用于正式调试。",
      }
    : {
        label: "快速模拟",
        description: "不调用外部模型、不产生模型费用，适合先检查流程分支、规则和字段是否接通。",
      }
}

type WorkflowNodeLike = {
  node_instance_id?: unknown
  kind?: unknown
  display_name?: unknown
  config?: unknown
}

type WorkflowEdgeLike = {
  from?: unknown
  to?: unknown
  branch?: unknown
}

export type PromptExecutionSummary = {
  nodeInstanceId: string
  displayName: string
  promptVersion: string
  modelName: string
  modelId: string
  maxTokens: number | null
  thinkingLevel: string
}

function record(value: unknown): Record<string, unknown> {
  return value && typeof value === "object" && !Array.isArray(value)
    ? value as Record<string, unknown>
    : {}
}

export function workflowPromptExecutionSummaries(manifest: {
  nodes?: WorkflowNodeLike[]
}): PromptExecutionSummary[] {
  return (manifest.nodes ?? []).flatMap((node) => {
    if (node.kind !== "prompt" || typeof node.node_instance_id !== "string") return []
    const config = record(node.config)
    const component = record(config.component_snapshot)
    const model = record(config.model_snapshot)
    const maxTokens = config.max_tokens
    return [{
      nodeInstanceId: node.node_instance_id,
      displayName: typeof node.display_name === "string" ? node.display_name : node.node_instance_id,
      promptVersion: String(component.version ?? config.prompt_version_id ?? "未标明"),
      modelName: String(model.name ?? model.model_id ?? "未标明模型"),
      modelId: String(model.model_id ?? "—"),
      maxTokens: typeof maxTokens === "number" && Number.isFinite(maxTokens) ? maxTokens : null,
      thinkingLevel: config.thinking_enabled === true
        ? String(config.thinking_level ?? "auto")
        : "none",
    }]
  })
}

function descendantsBeforeJoin(
  outgoing: Map<string, string[]>,
  start: string,
  joinId: string,
): Set<string> {
  const pending = [start]
  const found = new Set<string>()
  while (pending.length) {
    const current = pending.pop()
    if (!current || current === joinId || found.has(current)) continue
    found.add(current)
    for (const next of outgoing.get(current) ?? []) pending.push(next)
  }
  return found
}

export function estimatedPromptRequestsPerItem(manifest: {
  nodes?: WorkflowNodeLike[]
  edges?: WorkflowEdgeLike[]
}): number {
  const nodes = manifest.nodes ?? []
  const edges = manifest.edges ?? []
  const promptIds = new Set(
    nodes
      .filter((node) => node.kind === "prompt" && typeof node.node_instance_id === "string")
      .map((node) => String(node.node_instance_id)),
  )
  const outgoing = new Map<string, string[]>()
  for (const edge of edges) {
    if (typeof edge.from !== "string" || typeof edge.to !== "string") continue
    outgoing.set(edge.from, [...(outgoing.get(edge.from) ?? []), edge.to])
  }
  const grouped = new Set<string>()
  let perItem = 0
  for (const node of nodes) {
    if (node.kind !== "branch" || typeof node.node_instance_id !== "string") continue
    const config = record(node.config)
    if (config.mode !== "exclusive" || typeof config.branch_key !== "string") continue
    const join = nodes.find((candidate) => {
      if (candidate.kind !== "join" || typeof candidate.node_instance_id !== "string") return false
      return record(candidate.config).join_for === config.branch_key
    })
    if (!join || typeof join.node_instance_id !== "string") continue
    const targets = edges
      .filter((edge) => edge.from === node.node_instance_id && typeof edge.to === "string")
      .map((edge) => String(edge.to))
    if (!targets.length) continue
    const routePromptCounts = targets.map((target) => {
      const routePrompts = [...descendantsBeforeJoin(outgoing, target, join.node_instance_id as string)]
        .filter((id) => promptIds.has(id))
      routePrompts.forEach((id) => grouped.add(id))
      return routePrompts.length
    })
    perItem += Math.max(...routePromptCounts)
  }
  perItem += [...promptIds].filter((id) => !grouped.has(id)).length
  return perItem
}

export function expectedPromptRequests(
  manifest: { nodes?: WorkflowNodeLike[]; edges?: WorkflowEdgeLike[] },
  itemCount: number,
): number {
  return Math.max(0, Math.trunc(itemCount)) * estimatedPromptRequestsPerItem(manifest)
}
